# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Maskaram (Mois de maskaram)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 43  
**Columns**: pp. 326–504  

[p. 326] {#po.43.p326}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ንወጥን ፡ በረድኤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወሠናይ! ፡ ሀብቱ ፡ በጽሒፈ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ስንክሳር ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ዘቀድኅዎ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መምህራነ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ። አብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አትሪብ ፡ ወመሊግ ። 'ወአብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ 'ኤኢስ ፡ ቆይስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቡርልስ ። ወካልኣን ፡"አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወክቡራን ፡እለ ፣ አስተጋብ እዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፤ እምኩሎሙ"" ፡ ገድላት ፡ ዘመላእክት ፡ 'ወሐዋርያት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ወሰማዕታት፤! ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት፤፤ ፡ ወጸድቃን! ፡ ወገዳማዊያን ፡ ወመነኮሳት፤ ። ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ወያንብቡ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ኩሎ፤ ፣፡ አሚረ ፤ እም፳፤ ለመስከረም ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ዓመት ። ወኮነ ፤ አስተጋብ ኦቱ፤ ፡ በ፪፻፳ወ፻፤!"ዓመት ፣፤ ለሰማዕታት! ፡ ንጹሓን ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. We begin, with the help of God and his excellent favor, to write the book of the Synaxarium, that is to say, a collection which our holy fathers, doctors of the Church, the illustrious father Abba Mika'el, bishop of the cities of Atrib and Malig, and the illustrious father Abba Yohannes (= John), bishop of the city of Burles (= Borlos) - and other holy and illustrious fathers who compiled this book - have drawn (which they have therefore drawn) from all the lives of the angels, apostles, prophets, martyrs, patriarchs, righteous, anchorites, and monks so that their commemoration might be made and their lives read every day from the first of Maskaram until the end of the year. The compilation took place in the year 963 of the pure martyrs, may their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ" ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ 8ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። ወርኃ፤ ፣ ወስከረም ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ርእሰ ፣ አውራኅ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ዘግብጽ ፡ ወኢትዮጵያ ፡ መዓልቱ ፡፤ ወሌሊቱ ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሰዓተ ፡ እምዝ” ፡ የሐጽጽ ፡፤ እም፳ 'ለወርኀ ፤ መስከረም ፡ በእንተ ፡፤ ውእቱ ፡ ርእሰ! ፡ ዓውደ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ቡርክት፤፤ ፤ ዘግብጽ ፤ ወኢትዮጵያ፤" ።፤ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ንግበር ፡ ባቲ፤ ፣ በዓለ ፡ ዓባየ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ንጽሕና ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወቡር!ክ]ት ። ወንርሐቅ ፡ እምግባራት ፡ እኪት፤ ፡ ወንወጥን! ፡ 'በምግባራት ፡ ሐዲሳት፤" ፡ ዘይሰምር፤ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ 'ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ፤ ፡ እስመ" 0፣ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ተሐደሰ ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ምግባራት ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ "ጥቀ ፡ ኀለፉ፣ "ወናሁ ፡ ምግባራት፤፤ ፡ ሐዲሳት" ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ኮነ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሠምረ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ወወሀበነ ፡ መልዕክተ?፤ ፡ሣህል፤ ፡ ወምሕረት ። ወይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡መንፈሰ ፡እግዚኣብ ሔር ፡ ላዕሌየ ። በእንተዝ፥፤ ፡ ቀብአኒ ፡ ወፈነወኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እዜንዎሙ ፣ ለሙቁሓን
[READING FOR THE FIRST OF MASKARAM.] The month of Maskaram (is) blessed; it is the beginning of the months of the year of Egypt and (of) Ethiopia. The day and the night (are) equal - twelve hours; then it (= the day) decreases from the first of the month of Maskaram, because it is the beginning of the cycle of the blessed year of Egypt and (of) Ethiopia. It is fitting that we hold a great feast in it in all purity, for this day (is) holy and blessed. Let us turn away from evil actions and begin with new actions that God will accept, as the apostle Paul says: Everything is renewed in Christ; the old works have completely disappeared and behold, the new works have completely arrived, and everything is of God; he has accepted this for us in Christ and has given us the ministry of clemency and mercy. Isaiah also says: The spirit of God is upon me, that is why he has anointed me and sent me to announce liberation to the prisoners, to the blind that they would see, and to the chained that they would be unbound, and
፣ በተስድዶ ፡ ወለዕዉራን ፤ ከመ ፡ ይርአዩ ፡ ወለእሱራን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይት ፈትሑ ። ወእስብክ ፡ "በዓመተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ዘእግዚኣብሔር ። ወይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ወትባርክ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ምሕረትከ" ፡ ወይጸግቡ ፡ ጠላተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እምበረከትከ ።
to proclaim a year of God's mercy. David the prophet says: Thy mercy blesses the crown of the year and the fatness of the desert is satisfied by thy blessing.


[p. 328] {#po.43.p328}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፥ ፡ ተሐጸበ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ በመየ ፡ ተከዜ ፡ ወተፈወሰ ፤ እምኩሉ ፡ ደዌሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ ልማዶሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይመልእ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ተከዜ ፡ ይጠመቁ ፡ "በማይ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወይትባረኩ+0፤ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በዓውደ ፡ ዓመት ።
On this day also, Job the righteous washed himself in the water of the Takkaze and was healed of all his ailments. This is the custom of the people: when the Takkaze river is high, they plunge into the new water and are blessed by it for (the whole) period of the year.
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ። ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡በርተሎሜዎስ ፡ዘውእቱ ፡ እም፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ። ለ]ዝንቱ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ክፍሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ-፡ ሀገረ ፤ እልዋህት ፡ ወሐሩ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወጴጥሮስ" ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወጸውዖሙ+ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወእምድ ኅረ ፡- ገብረ5፤ ፡፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ።፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፤ ዓበ[ይ]ተ ፡ ዘአደንገፀ፤ ፡ አልባቢሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተመክነየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፤ ወሜጦ፤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብር ። ወኮነ ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓጸደ ፡ ወይን ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ያስተዳልው ፡ አዕፁቃተ ፡ ዓጸደ ፡ ወይን ፡ ይፈርዩ" ፡ በጊዜሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አን ሥኦ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በርተሎሜዎስ ፡እማእከለ ፡ምውታን ። ወአምኑ ፡ኩሎሙ ፡ወአጽን
On this day also died the holy apostle Bartholomew, who is (among) the twelve apostles. The lot given by fate to this apostle (was) to go to the land of Elwahet (= Khargah); he and Peter went to them (= the inhabitants of Elwahet) and he (= Bartholomew) preached to them and called them to the knowledge of God. After he had performed great miracles and wonders before them which frightened their hearts and after he had used a pretext to enter the city, Peter sold him as a slave. He worked in a vineyard with his master and, when he prepared the vine branches, (they) bore fruit immediately. When the son of the governor of the city died, the apostle Bartholomew raised him from the dead. Everyone believed, and he strengthened them in the knowledge of God.
ፆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር፤ ፡ሀገረ ፡ በርባር ፡ ወ ፈነዎ= ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለእ]ን]ድርያስ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይትራድኦ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እኩያን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኢተወክፍዎሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፤ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለ8እምነ ፡ ገጻተ ፡ ከለባት ፡ በላዕተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአዘዝ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ወኢይትአደዎሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘይኤዝዝዎ ። ወነሥእዎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ “ኀበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ። "ወኮኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አምጽኡ ፣ ሎሙ"" ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ለሐዋር ያት ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ይምስጥዎሙ ፡ ወይብልፅዎሙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገጸ ፡ከልብ ፡ላዕለ ፡እሙንቱ ፡አራዊት ፣ወመሰጦሙ ፡ወካዕበ ፣ ቀተሎሙ'"" ፡እም ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ። ወበእንተዝ?፤ ፣፡ ፈርሁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፤ ወተአዘዙ ፡፣ ሎሙ" ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣይ ማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ?- ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ ስብሐት ። ወሙ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ካህናተ ፣ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወሖሩ ፤ እምኀቤሆሙ ፡ እንዘ"” ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
After that our Lord Christ ordered him to go to the land of Barbar (= the barbarians?) and he sent Andrew to him so that he might help him. The people of this land were very evil and they did not welcome the apostles even though they performed miracles and wonders before them. God ordered one of the man-eating cynocephali to obey the apostles and not to disobey them in anything they ordered him. They took him with them into the land and the people of the land brought fierce beasts against the apostles so that they might tear them to pieces and devour them. The cynocephalus then stood up against the fierce beasts, tore them to pieces, and also killed (many) of the people of the land. Everyone became afraid because of this and they converted, prostrated themselves at the feet of the apostles, obeyed them, and entered into the faith of our Lord Christ - glory to him! They (= the apostles) ordained priests for them, built churches for them, and left them while praising God.


[p. 330] {#po.43.p330}
ወበርተሎሜዎስሰ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አህጉር ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ?" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፡-እለ ፡ ኢያአምርዎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወሰበከ ፣ ው ስቴቶሙ"፡፣ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ'" ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወኮነ ፡ በርተሎሜዎስ፤፤ ፡ ሐዋ ርያ ፤ ይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡፤ ንጹሓነ ፤ ወይርሐቁ ፡፤ እ[ም]ዝሙት ። ወሰምዐ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ አግርጳስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስይዎ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፣ በርተሎሜዎስ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ሠቀ ፡ ጸኾር ፡ ወይምልእዎ ፡ ፕፃ ፡፤ ወይግድፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ወተጋድሎቶ ፡፡'በከመ ዛቲ ፡ዕለት ። ትንብልናሁ ፡'ወጸሎቱ ፡
As for the apostle Bartholomew, he went to the lands that are by the sea which did not know God, he preached there and converted them to the knowledge of God. They believed in our Lord Christ and performed actions that befit Christians. The apostle Bartholomew ordered the people to be pure and to keep away from lust. King Agrippa heard (about) him and became very angry with him; he ordered the apostle Bartholomew to be put in a hair sack, to fill it with sand, and to throw it into the sea, and this was done to him. || finished his martyrdom and his combat on this day. May his intercession, his prayer, and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚልዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡፣ ጳጳሳት ፡ዘሀገር ፡ዐባይ ፡እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ሣልስይ ፡እምነ ፡አቡነ' ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፤ ወንጌላዊ ። ወተሰይመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ በ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለአስብስያኖስ፤ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወውእቱኬ” ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ 'ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ በ፲ጠወ፳ዓመት ። ወዓ ቀቦሙ ፡ ለመርዔቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፣ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚኣብ
On this day also died the holy father Abba Milyos, patriarch of the great city of Alexandria. He is the third (patriarch) after our father Mark, the evangelist apostle. This father was named patriarch in the fifteenth year of the reign of Vespasian, emperor of Rome. This therefore (took place) thirty-five years after the ascension of Jesus Christ. He guarded the flock of our Lord Jesus Christ with a good guard, he remained on the patriarchal seat for twelve years, he pleased God, and he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወትሩፍ ፡ አባ ፡ ሜልኪ ። ወለዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፤ እምዓበይተ ፡ ቀሩልዝም ፡፤ ወይእቲ ፡ እምአድያመ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወብዑላን ፡-እሙንቱ ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፡ ወይሁቡ" ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ለነዳያ ን ፡ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወጥቀ ፡ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ሎሙ ፡ወልድ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ሕፃናተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሜሐሩ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ እዴሆሙ ፡ ሰሌዳት ፡ ወጽሑፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ንዘከሮሙ ፡ ለአበዊነ ፡ ዘመሐሩነ ፡ በሕይወቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንኩን ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ አዕ ርፍ ፤ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ በሰማያት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፣ ወሰማዕታት፤'" ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሜልኪ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡እምአፈ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ ተከዘ ፡ጥቀ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አሌ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘአልብየ ፡፤ ውሉድ ፡ መኑ፤፤ ፡ ይዜከረኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ እመውት ።
[On this day also died the righteous and excellent Abba Melki. The parents of this righteous man were (among) the notables of Clysma - this (locality is part) of the territory of Egypt - and they were rich in gold and silver; they gave alms to the poor and the needy and they loved God very much. They had no son and, one day among days, they saw young children who were learning; in their hands (they had) tablets on which were written these words: "Let us remember our fathers who instructed us by their life so that we might become the children of God. O Lord, give rest to their soul in the heavens with the righteous and the martyrs." When the father of Abba Melki had heard this word from the mouth of the young children, he became very sad, saying: "Woe to me who have no son! Who will remember me when I die?"
ወተማኅለሉ፤‹፡-10 ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበውሒበ ፡ ምጽዋት ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ስእለቶሙ ። ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ፪ውሉደ፤ ፣ በአሐቲ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተባዕተ ፡ ወአንስተ ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፍሥሐ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብእዎሙ ፡ ክርስት ና ፤ ወሠመይዎ ፡ ለተባዕታይ ፡ ሜልኪ!" ፡ ወለእኅቱ፤" ፡ ሶፍና፤" ፡ ዘበትርጓ
They (= the parents of Melki) supplicated (God) in fasting and prayer and by giving alms; God heard their prayer and gave them two children at one time, a boy and a girl, and they felt great joy. Then they brought them into the Christian religion, they called the boy


[p. 332] {#po.43.p332}
ሜሁ ፡ ርግብ ፣ ወሐፀንዎሙ፤ ፡ በሠናይ ፣ ተሐፅኖ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ጻዓመተ ፡ ወሰዶ፤፤ዐ ፡ አቡሁ ፤ ለሜልኪ፤!፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መምህር ። ወተምህረ ፤ ኩሎ ፡ መጸ ሕፍተ፤፤። ፡ ብሉይ፤፤ ፡ ወሐዲስ፤፤ ፣ ወመልአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወኢይትዋነይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ ወኢይሥሕቅ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወበሕቱ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ መዓልተ፤፤፤ ፡ ወሌሊተ ።
Melki and his sister, Sofna - which means dove; and they raised them with a good education. When Melki was seven years old, his father sent him to a teacher, he learned all the books of the Old and New (Testaments) and the grace of the Holy Spirit filled him; he did not play with the young children nor did he laugh with them, but he read the Scriptures day and night.
ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ፤! ፡ ጸ[ው]ዕዎ ፡ ለወልዶሙ ፡ ወተናገርዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውስብ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ፈቂዶሙ ፡ ከመ፤፤ ፡ ይኩኖሙ ፡ ተዝካረ ፣ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢሠ ምረ ፤ በዝንቱ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ በተምያን"፤ ፡ ኦሆ ፣ ለቃልክሙ ፡፣ ወዘአ ዘዝክሙኒ ፡ እገብር ፡ ወነፍሱሰ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጉየይ ፡ እምዓለም ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ "ለአቡሁ ፡ አባ፤፤ዐ ፡ ሜልኪ፤፡፤ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግበር ፡ ምሳሐ ፤ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ እለ ፤ ተምሀሩ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡፤ ወወሀቦ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ፲፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ለመፍቅደ ፡ ምሳሕ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አግብርተ ፤ ወአ ፅማተ፤፡= ፡ ወወዓልያነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትለአክዎ ፡ ለመፍቅዱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተፅዕነ ፡ ፈረሰ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለወዓልያኒሁ ፤ ንሥኡ ፡ ፻ዲናረ ፤ ወርቅ ፣፤ ወአስተፋጥኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታስተዳልዉ! ፡ ለነ ፡ ማሳሐ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ አሐውር ፤፡ ወእውዖሙ ፡ ለአብያጽየ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወዓልያ ኒሁ ፡ ተመይጡ ፤ ወውእቱሰ፤፤ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወዘረወ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወፈረሶሂ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ለ፳ነዳይ ፤ ወኢያትረፈ ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፤ ዘእንበለ ፡ ልብስ ፡ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፣ጐንደየ ፣ምጽአቱ፤!፤ ፡ኃሠሥዎ ፡ ወዓልያኒሁ ፡ ወኢረከብዎ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡፤እንዘ ፡ ይበክዩ ፡ ወረከብዎሙ! ፡ ወነገር ዎሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፤ ወለእሙ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወእኅቱሰ ፡ እምብገኃ ፡
When he was twelve years old, (his parents) called their son and spoke with him so that he might take a wife, desiring that there be a commemoration for them. As for him, he did not agree to this, but he said to them by ruse: "Yes, (I agree) with your word and, what you order me, I will do." But his soul desired to flee the world. Then Abba Melki said to his father: "My father, I desire to have a banquet with the children who were instructed with me." His father gave him a thousand gold denarii so that he might use them for the needs of the banquet, and placed slaves, handmaids, and servants at his orders to assist him in what he might need. Then, he (= Abba Melki) mounted a horse, set out before his father, and said to his servants: "Take a hundred gold denarii and hasten to prepare the banquet for us; as for me, I will go alone to invite my comrades." Having heard this word, his servants returned and he, he went away alone and distributed the gold to the poor and the needy. He also gave his horse to a poor man and he kept nothing except the garment that was on him. Then, as he was late in coming, his servants began to search for him and did not find him. They returned in tears, (went) to find his father and mother and told (them); (they) wept bitter tears. As for his sister, her eyes became blind from weeping and she died.

As for Abba Melki, he went to the monastery of Tur which was the monastery of his mother's brother, Mar Awgin (= Eugene); he was righteous and performed many miracles; there were seventy-two chosen monks under his authority. Abba Melki prostrated himself at his feet and said to him: "I want to dwell in the shadow of your house and for you to clothe me in the monastic garment." Abba Awgin said to him: "From what city are you?" He told him that his city was Clysma (and that) he was his sister's son. Having heard this, he (= Awgin) rejoiced with great joy and permitted him to test himself for three years. He then clothed him in the monastic garment and he (= Abba Melki) multiplied vigils and (ascetic) combats so much that his reputation was known throughout the land of Fars (= Persia). When thirty years had passed after (his) taking the habit, God gave him the gift of healing to such an extent that he cast out demons (from the possessed) by his voice.
ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡፤ ወያነብብ ፤ መዝ ሙረ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ግብ ፣ ዐቢይ ፡ ወውስቴቱ፤!" ፡ ፅፀ፤ ፡ በለስ ፡

ወብዙኃን ፡ ኖሎት ፡ይበክዩ፤ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አባ ፣ ሜልኪ!0 ፡ ምንት ፡ያበክየ ክሙ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ በለሰ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ተመን ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወውሕጦ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግቡ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይነግርዎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መጽአ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሠጥጥ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወይጸፍዕ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወአስተብቀ-ዖ፤፤ ፡ ለማር ፡ ሜልኪ! ፡ በእንተ ፣፡ ወልዱ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራእ፤ ፡ ተአመን ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወትሬኢ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ወማጠ ፤ ገጾ፤ ፣ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ! ፡ ወጸለየ ፣ ነዋኃ ፤ ወፈጺሞ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ጸውዖ ፡ ለተመን ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቂዖ ፡ ለሕፃን፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይለክፎ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ለግሙራ ፡ ወሰይጣን ፡ ከልሐ ፡፤ ዘውስተ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ሀሎ፤" ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አይቴ ፡ አሐውር ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ኦሜልኪ፤ ፡ ቀርልዝማዊ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተዘርወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አበ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ !ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ! ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ወአበየ ፡ ቅዱስ፤የ ፡ ተመጥዎቶ ። 'ወባሕቱ ፤ ይቤሎ፤፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሳተፍ ፡ በበይና ቲነ ፤ ሕንጽ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ደብረ፤= ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አበ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፻፻አብያተ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወቅጽረ ፡ ደብርሂ ፡ አፆደ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ እምሐዩን ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐንጹ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ በመዓዝኒሃ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓባየ ፡ዘኢይክሉ ፡ አልዕሎታ ፡፻፻ዕደው ፡ወበጸሎቱ ፡፡'ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ፤! ፡ ተሐን ጸት ፡ በፈቃዳ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እብን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይፈቅድዋ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ህየ ፡
fig tree, and many shepherds were weeping (there). Abba Melki said to them: "What makes you weep?" They said to him: "While the son of the governor of the city was eating a fig, a large dragon came out, swallowed him, and went back into his cave." While they were telling him this, his father (= the child's father) - and the people of the city - arrived, tearing his clothes and striking his face, and he begged Mar Melki for his son. He (= Abba Melki) said to him: "Do not have fear, believe in God and you will see a wonder." He turned his face toward the east, stretched out his hand, and prayed for a long time. Having finished his prayer, he called the dragon in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ and ordered him to spit out the little child without any harm reaching him. The demon that was in the serpent cried out and said: "Where shall I go far from you, Melki the Clysman?" Having said this, he dissipated like smoke. When the father of the little child saw that his son was alive, he brought the saint gold and silver: the saint refused to take them, but said to him: "So that we may be associated, build a monastery for me." The father of the little child rejoiced and he built about three hundred monk's cells for him, and the wall of the monastery surrounded (them), and he made an iron door (there). While the church was being built, they found in its corners a large stone that three hundred men could not lift and, by the prayer of this righteous man, this stone arranged itself as they wanted. Then, five hundred people gathered there and became chosen monks.
፳፻ነፍስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ መነኮሰተ ፡ ኅሩያነ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኮኑ፤ ፡፣ ያመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ድውያነ ፡ ዘዘዚአሁ፤፤ ፡ ደዌሆሙ ፡ ወእለ፤ ፡ አጋንንት ፣፤ ወይት ፌወሱ ፡'እምኩሉ ፡ ደዌሆሙ፤፤" ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወተዐቀቡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወሮም ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለዝንቱ፤ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ሠናያት ፡ ቀንዓ፤ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ሮም ፡ ወአዕበዳ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ትኬልሕ ፤ ወትብል ፡ አልብየ ፣፤ ፈውሰ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ሜልኪ!፤9 ፣ ሦኑልዝማዊ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ፪፻ብእሴ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ በትጋህ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሜልኪ!፤ሩ፤ ፣፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ መሐለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትር ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ።
The fame of the saint was known throughout the land; all the sick with their different ailments and the possessed were brought to him, and they were healed of all their ailments by his prayer. The people of Fars and Rome were guarded by the intercession of this righteous man. When Satan, the enemy of all good, saw him, he was jealous of him and entered into the daughter of the emperor of Rome and made her mad. She cried out and said: "There is no healing for me if it is not (by) Melki the Clysman." Having heard (this), the king her father sent four hundred men of his soldiers in all directions so that they might bring Abba Melki to him with diligence. He swore, if (they) did not (bring him), to cut off their heads with the sword.
ወሐራሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ተፈነዉ ፡ እምዕፁብ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ፤፡ ፡ ታሕተ ፤ እገሪሁ ። ወነገርዎ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወደዌሃ ፡ ለወለቱ፤ ፡ ወነገረኒ ፡ ዘትቤ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ሜልኪ'፤ ፣ አንሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወኢይክል ፡ እሖር ፡ ሮምያ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሐራ ፡ እመሰ ፤ ኢሖርከ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሰፈወነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ሞት ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ "ተከዘ ፣ ጥቀ፤ ፡ ወኃዘነ፤ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፤ ሑሩ ፡ ፍኖተክሙ ፡ አነ ፡ ወአንት ሙ ፡ ንትራከብ ፡ በአንቀጸ ፡ ሮምያ ፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ሖሩ ። ወበተፍጻሜተ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ብጽሐቶሙ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡
As for the soldiers who had been sent, they found the saint with difficulty and prostrated themselves at his feet. They told him the king's message, the illness of his daughter, and also the word she had said. Abba Melki said to them: "I am an old man and I cannot go to Rome." The soldiers said to him: "The king promised us death if you did not come with us." Having heard this, the saint was very afflicted, was greatly saddened, and said to them: "My children, go your way; I and you will meet at the gate of Rome." Having heard his word, they went away. At the end of the year, when he (= Abba Melki) knew the day of their arrival

ቅዱስ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ጸሎተ ፤ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጾሮ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሮምያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ልኡካን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወነገርዎ፤ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፤ ምጽአቶ ፡ ወንጉሥኒ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፤ ዐቢየ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ወተቀበሎ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርጉጐ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መን በሩ ፡ወነገሮ ፡ በእንተ ፡ደዊዌሃ ፡ ለወለቱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ያምጽእዋ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አምጽእዋ ፡ ነጽሓ ፡ ጋኔን ፡ ወአሰወና ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሐዘብዋ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሜልኪ!፤ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ነዋኃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነዝኃ ፡ ማየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ወቀብዓ ፡ ዘይተ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚእነ፤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፃእ ፡ እምላዕሌሃ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ገብር ፡፣ ጸሊም ። ወአኃዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወዓፀዎ፤'?" ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'ቤተ ፣፤ ሞቅሕ፤?፤ ፡ ወዓተበ ፤
by the Holy Spirit, he addressed a prayer to God. Then the Holy Spirit carried him and brought him to the gate of Rome. When the envoys found the saint again, they rejoiced greatly; they announced his arrival to the king and the king also rejoiced greatly. He went out to meet him, introduced him into his palace, made him sit on his throne, and spoke to him about the illness of his daughter. He (= Melki) ordered that she be brought; when she had been brought, the demon shook her violently and made her foam at the mouth to such an extent that they believed she was dead. The saint Abba Melki then stood up and prayed for a long time. Then he sprinkled water on her face, anointed her with oil, and ordered Satan in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ to come out of her. And he came out of her in the form of a black slave. The saint seized Satan, locked him in prison, and made the sign of the cross on his face so that he would not come out.
ገጾ፤?። ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፃእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወአምጽእአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ለ]መፍቅደ ፡ ደብሩ! ፣፡ ወአበየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ንበር ፤ ዝየ ፣ ወአነ ፡ አሐንጽ ፡ ለከ ፡፣ ደብረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ኢይትከሀለኒ ፤ እኅድግ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ወነበረ ፣ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ሮምያ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይፌውስ ፡ ድውያኒሆሙ ፡ ወአመ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ሀገሮ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ያስተፋንዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ። ወበጽሐ፤” ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ጽርሕ ፤ ወርእየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዕብነ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ስቁረ ፡ ዘአንበርዎ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ወገብላተ ፡እብንኒ ፡ዘያሰትዩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ አፍራሰ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ሀበኒ ፡እሎንተ ፤ አዕባነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑኒ ፤ ሊተ! ፡ ለመፍቅደ፤ ፡ ደብርየ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መኑ ፡ ይፀውር ፡ ለከ ፤ ዘኢይክሉ ፡ ይፁርዎ[ሙ ፤ ፲ወ፪ዕደው ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእዎሙ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወእመሰ ፡ ብከ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ግበር ፡ ዘፈቀድከ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ አውፅኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አሰሮ ፡ ወሰቀሎ፤" ፣ "ለእብን ፡ ስቁር፤፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ወገብላተ ፡እብን ፡ አጾሮ ፡ዲበ ፡ርአእሱ ። ወአዘ ዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ጥቀ ፡
When the king saw this, he prostrated himself at his feet and brought him much money for the needs of his monastery, but he (= Abba Melki) refused. The king said to him: "My father, stay here, and I will build you a monastery." The saint said to him: "It is not possible for me to abandon my children." And the saint stayed a few days in the city of Rome, healing their illnesses (= those of the inhabitants). When the saint went out to return to his country, the king stood up to accompany him with his soldiers. The saint arrived at the gate of the palace and saw a large pierced stone that had been placed at the edge of a well, and also a stone trough where horses were watered. He said to the king: "Give me these stones so that they may serve me for the needs of my monastery." The king said to him: "Who will carry for you (these stones) that twelve men cannot carry to take them out of the city? But if you have the strength (to carry them), do what you want (with them)." Then the saint made Satan come out from where he had chained him, hung the pierced stone around his neck, and, the stone trough, he loaded it onto his head and ordered him to walk before him. When the king saw this, he was very astonished and returned praising God.
ወተመይጠ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወቅዱስሰ፤ ፡ አባ ፡ ሜልኪ! ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ውስተ ፡ደብሩ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይፀውር ፡ እሎንተ ፡ አዕባነ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ያንብርዎሙ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብሩ፤5-1፤ ፡ ወሀለዉ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወለሰይጣንሰ ፡ ሞቅሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፤ ወሐተመ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፃእ ፡ ዳግመ ፡
As for Abba Melki, he led Satan carrying these stones into his monastery and prescribed that they be placed in his monastery; they are (there) still now. As for Satan, he locked him in a cave and sealed it with the sign of the cross so that he would not come out again and lay traps for him.
ወኢያመክሮ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ዳወ፳ዓመተ ፡ እምአመ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ! ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ቃል ፡እምሰማይ ፡ዘይብል ፡ናሁ ፡ቀርበት ፡ዕለተ ፡ሞትከ ፡ወኢተረፈከ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ዞዕለት ፣ ወተሐውር ፡ውስተ ፡ፍሥሓ ፡ዘለዓለም ። ወእምዝ ፣ጸውዖሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ይትቀነዩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ወይርሐቁ ፡እምአ ስሕቶቱ ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፻ዕለት ፤ መጽኡ ፤ ኅቤሁ ፡ 'መነኮሳት ፡ አበው፤ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንስ፤፤ ፡ ወአባ ፡ መቃርስ! ፡ ወአባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፡ ወአባ 0፣
When it had been forty-five years since this saint had become a monk, a voice came to him from heaven which said: "Behold, the day of your death is near, you have only three days left and you will go into eternal joy." The saint then called his children and prescribed that they serve God and keep away from the seductions of Satan. Three days later, the monk fathers Abba Anthony, Abba Macarius, Abba Shenoudi,


[p. 338] {#po.43.p338}
ብሳይ ፡ ወአባ ፣፤ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ነዓ ፣፤ እነ ፤ ትትፌሣሕ"፤ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ዘንተ ፤ ወፅአት፤" ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ እምሥጋ ሁ ፡ ወአዕረግዋ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በስብሐት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜምሩ ። ወይብሉ ፡ ዘጻመወ ፡ በዓለም ፡ የሐዩ ፣ ለዝላፉ ። ወአብእዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ገነተ ፣ ትፍሥሕት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ጳጳሳት ፣ወኤኢስ፤ ፡ቆኦሳት ፡ ወካህናት ፡ወገነዝዎ ፡ወቀበርዎ ፡ኀኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአስተርአየ ፤ ውስተ ፡፤ መቃብሩ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡፤ ዘአልቦ ፡ጐልቀ- ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡-ምስለ ፡ንጉሥነ ፡ኢያሱ ፡ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፤ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Abba Psoi and Abba Pachomius came to him, and they said to him: "Come, our brother, you will rejoice with us in the kingdom of heaven." When they had said this to him, his soul went out of his body and the angels made it ascend (to heaven) in glory while singing, and they said: "He who has labored in the world will live forever." And they made it enter the garden of delights. Then the metropolitans, the bishops, and the priests gathered, shrouded him, and buried him near the church. On his tomb occurred miracles and wonders without number. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king Iyasu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፤ ፅለት! ፡ በዓሉ ፣ ለራጉኤል ፤ መልአክ ፡ 8እምዘ፪ሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘአለበዎ ፡ ለኤኖክ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘይነድድ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ድምሳሴ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ብርሃናት ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡-መልአክ ፡የሀሉ ፡ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
On this day (is) the feast of the angel Raguel, one of the nine archangels, who instructed Enoch about the fire that burns and (about) all the destruction of the world; he is the angel of the luminaries. May the intercession of this angel be with our king Iyasu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus, for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 2ND OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምለክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፣ ኮነ፤ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወመጥምቅ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወልደ ፡ዘካርያስ ፡ ካህን ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፥ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ሶበ ፡ ገሠጾ ፡ 'ነቢይ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሄሮድያዳ ፣ ብእሲተ ፡ እሁ" ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ብእሲተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ዮሐንስ፤፤ ፡ኢይደልወከ ፡ከመ ፡ትንሣእ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡እተቱከ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡'ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ፤" ፡ ወወደዮ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የዓቅቦ ፡ ወይፈርህ ፡ እምኔሁ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy John, the prophet and baptizer, son of Zacharias the priest, became a martyr by the hand of Herod the impious king. This one, - when the prophet John rebuked him about Herodias, the wife of his brother whom he had taken for himself as a wife - and saint John had said to him: "It is not fitting that you take your brother's wife" -, Herod (therefore) took the holy John and threw him into prison; he kept him and was afraid of him.
ወሶበ ኮነ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ምሳሐ፤ ፡ ለልሂቃነ ፡ መንግሥ ቱ ፡ ወመኳንንተ፤ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ገሊላ ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ወለተ ፡፤ እሁ ፡ ወቆመት ፡ በማእከለ ፡ምርፋቅ ፡ ወዘፈነት ፡ ወአደመቶ፤ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወተካየዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀባ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘትስእል ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መንፈቀ ፣ መንግሥቱ ። ወወፅአት ፡ ኀበ ፡እማ ፡ወትቤላ ፡-ምንተ ፡እስእሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡የሀበኒ ። ወትቤላ ፡እማ ፡ ሰአልዮ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብኪ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ፡ 'ውስተ!፤ ፡ ጸሕል ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ዘንተ፤ ፡ 'ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ፤ ። ወበእንተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይረፍቁ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፤ ኢፈቀደ፤፤ ፡ ከመ፤፤ ፡ ያስተኃፍራ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወየሀብዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ወለት ። ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወወሀብዋ ፡ ለወለተ ፣
When it was Herod's birthday, (the latter) made a feast for the great ones of his kingdom and the magistrates of the land of Galilee. The daughter of his brother entered, stood in the middle of the tables, danced, and pleased the king. He promised her to give her everything she would ask of him, even if it were half his kingdom. She went out to her mother and said to her: "What shall I ask the king to give me?" Her mother said to her: "Ask him to give you the head of John the baptizer on a platter." When Herod heard this, he was very afflicted and, because of those who were seated at the table with him, he did not want to shame her. He then ordered the head of the holy John to be cut off and given to the young girl. They cut off the head of John the saint in the prison and gave it to the daughter of Herodias on a platter, and she gave it to her


[p. 340] {#po.43.p340}
ሄሮድያዳ ፡ውስተ ፡ጻሕል ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ወሀበት ፡ለእማ ። ወኮነ ፡ድንጋፄ ፡ዓቢይ ፡ በይእቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ ፍሥሓሆሙ ፡ ውስተ?" ፡ ሐዘን ። ወርእሱሰ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሰረረት፤- ፣ እማእከለ ፡ እደዊሆ ሙ ። "ወኮነት ፡ ትጸርኅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አየር፥፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኢይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፤ ብእሲተ ፡፤ እከ ። ወተብሀለ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ" ፡ በብሔረ ፤ ኣራብያ ። ወሥጋሁሰ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ ወአ ንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ እስከ ፡፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አትናትዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እልስክንድርያ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወተከሥተ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አትናትዮስ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡፤ እስከ ፡ የሐንጽ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያነ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ "ጸሎቱ ፤
mother. || There was great consternation on this day and their joy (= that of the guests) turned into affliction. As for the holy head of the saint John, it escaped from their hands and it was crying out in the air saying: "It is not fitting that you take your brother's wife." It is said that it is in the land of Arabia. As for his holy body, his disciples came, took it, carried it away, and put it in a tomb (where it remained) until the time of the holy Athanasius, patriarch of the city of Alexandria. God willed that he discover the body of the saint John, and (it) was discovered to the holy Athanasius (who) placed it near him until he had built a church for him. May his intercession, his prayer, and his blessing be with us, amen and amen.
ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ 'አሜን ። ወአሜን" ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፥፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ= ፡ ዳስያ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ታይዳ ፡ 'እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ አርያኖ ስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አድያና ፡ ኩነኔ ፣ ዓቢየ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ ለ]አክሊለ ፤ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን" ።
On this day also became a martyr saint Dasya, the soldier, who (is) from the city of Tida in the land of Egypt. Arianus, governor of the city of Antinoe, made this saint undergo great tortures; (then) they cut off his head with a sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ዲማስዮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ወመሪና ፡ ቅድስት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፻[ለመስከረም ፡]ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ፅለት ፡ኮነ ፡ማኅበረ፤ ፡ጉባኤ ፡፤ኤኢስ ፡ቆጸሳት ፡ በሀገረ ፡እስክንድርያ ፡ በዳግማይ ፡ዓመተ ፣ሚመቱ" ፡ ለአብ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ሊቀ ፡ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ድዮናስዮስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ዘአስተርአዩ ፡ በብሔረ ፤ ኣራብያ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ትመውት ፡ምስለ ፡ሥጋ ፡ ወበዕለተ ፡ትንሣኤ ፡ ትትነሣእ ፤ ምስሌሁ ። ወደረሱ ፡ ድርሳነ ፡ በሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ እኪት ፡ ወፈነዉ ፡ ውእተ” ፡ድርሳነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ዘይነብሩ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ድዮናስዮስ" ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ምክር ፡እኩይ ፡ ወኢሰምዑ ፡ እምኔሁ ።
[On this day also are Däsyos, martyr, and the holy Marinä (= Marina). May their prayer and blessing be with us forever and ever, amen.] READING FOR THE 3RD [OF MASKARAM] In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, the council of bishops assembled in the city of Alexandria, in the second year of the patriarchate of the holy father, the patriarch Abbä Dyonäsyos (= Dionysius). This assembly took place because of the wicked people who had appeared in the land of 'Aräbyä (= Arabia): they believed that the soul died with the body and (that), on the day of the Resurrection, it would rise again with it. They composed a homily in their wicked faith and sent this homily to the people who dwelt in the city of Alexandria. When our father, the patriarch Abbä Dyonäsyos, heard (of) this matter, he was greatly grieved for them (= the heretics), rebuked them, and ordered them to turn away from this wicked way of thinking, but they did not listen to him.


[p. 342] {#po.43.p342}
ወበእንተዝ ፡አስተጋብአ ፡ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ 'ዘንተ ፡ማኅበረ ፡ ወተዋሥኡ፤" ፡ምስሌ ሆሙ ፡ ወከሠቱ፤፤ ፡ስሕተቶሙ ፡ወኢነስሑ ፤ ወኢተመይጡ ፡እምክሮሙ ፡እኩይ ። ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ወረገሞሙ ፡ ወደረሰ ፡ ድርሳነ! ፡- በእንቲ[አ]ሆሙ ። ዘይቤ! ። እስመ ፍቅረ፡ እግዚኣ ብሔር፤፤ ፡ ለእጓለ! ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ዓቢይ፤' ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአጠየቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ድርሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስሰ፤ ፡ ኢትመውት ፣ ወኢትደመስስ ፡ ወኢት ማስን ፡ አላ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡፣ መላእክት ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡መንፈሳዊት ፣ ወኢትትወለጥ! ፡ ወኢትማስን ። ወእምጊዜ ፡ ትወፅእ ፡ እምሥጋ ፡ ይወስድዋ ፡ ውስተ፤፤ ፡ መካን ፡ በከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዘይደልዋ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትነፋኅ ፡ ቀርን ፣ ይትነሥኡ ፡ አብድንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡፣ በትእዛዘ ፤ ፈጣሪሆሙ ። ወይ ከውኑ ፡፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ ለለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ አሐቲ" ፡ እምኔሆን ፡ ትበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወትትነሣእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ምግባራ"" ፡ እመሂ ፤ ትፍሥሕተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ኩነኔ፤ ። ወይነብሩ ፤ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ዘነሥኡ፥ ፡ ወኢይትወለጡ0፥፤ ፡ እምኔሁ" ፡ እስከ፥ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወእስከ ፡ 'ለዓ
This is why he convened this assembly against them. They (= the bishops) disputed with them and brought their error to light; they did not come to repentance nor did they turn away from their wicked way of thinking. The patriarch, with the whole assembly, excommunicated and anathematized them, and he composed a homily concerning them which says: "Assuredly, God's love for man is very great." He explained in this homily that the soul does not die, is not destroyed, and does not corrupt, but (that) it subsists as the angels subsist, for it is spiritual (in essence), is not changed, and does not corrupt; at the moment it departs from the body, it is placed in a location consistent with what is fitting. On the day of the Resurrection, when the trumpet sounds, the corpses of men will rise at the command of their creator, all souls will enter each into its own body; they will rise with it according to their actions, either (for) eternal joy or (for) punishment, and both will remain in what they have received and will not change from it forever, unto the ages of ages. May the prayer of this assembly of saints and its blessing be with us, amen.
ለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ፥ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ" ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ዐቢይ ፡ድልቅልቅ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ግብጽ ፡ወበኩሉ ፡ አድያሚሃ ። ወወድቀት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘዳስያ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፻"ሰዐት ፤ ዘመዓ ልት ። ወይእቲ" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣፤ በስሙ ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፣፤ መላእክ ት ። ወተብህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምዓላውያን ፣ ሰአሎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብዎ፤-2 ፡ ጸባሕተ ። ወኢወሀብዎ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወነሠታ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በ፪ ዓመት ፤ በሚመቱ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ በዓመት ፡ ቿ፻፳ወ2ቿ ዘሰማዕታት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወም
[On this day also there was a great earthquake in the land of Egypt and in all its provinces, and the church of Däsyä collapsed at the third hour of the day. This church (was) in the name of Mikä'ël (= Michael) the archangel. It is said that (this) was the work (of someone) among the pagans; he asked the Christians to pay him tribute and they gave him nothing. When evening came, he went to the church with his helpers and destroyed it. This took place in the ninth year of the patriarchate of the patriarch Abbä Magäres, the year 828 of the martyrs. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus, forever and ever.]
ስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ወዲዮ ናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወሰማዕታት ፡ ዘበትንክል ፡ ያቆቶን ። ወብዙኃን ፣ ሰማዕታ ት59 ፣ ዘፋርስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም፤ ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ= ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ አባ ፣፤ አንበሳ ፡ ዘብሔረ ፡ ኢት ዮጵያ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also is the commemoration of Diyosqoros (= Dioscorus) the martyr and (of) Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius) the patriarch and of the martyrs who (were) in Tenkel Yägoton and (of) many martyrs of Färs (= Persia). May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu forever and ever.] [On this day also died Abbä 'Anbasä of the land of Ethiopia. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu forever and ever.]


[p. 344] {#po.43.p344}
"ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ ሲሐት ። እስመ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡እምአመ ፡መንኩሰ ፡ኃደጋ ፡ለዓለም ፡ ወሖረ ፡ገዳመ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ለጾም ፡ ወለጸሎት ፡እስከ ፡ አብርሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ለርእየ ፡ገነት ፡እምነ ፡ ዘይቶን ፡ ወሆሣዕና ፡ ወለለይሠርቅ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ዝኩ ፡፤ ዕፅ ። ወይበልዕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የአክሎ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሲሲተ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ወልብሱኒ ፤ እምልሕጸ5 ፡ ሆሣዕና ፤ ወቄጽሉኒ ። ወረከቦ ፡ ዋዕየ ፡ ኃጋይ ፡ ወሞረ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ዘጥቀ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ዝኒ ፡ ጽሙድ ፡ ለጸሎት፥" ፡ ወለ ጾም ። ወኮነ ፡ ዘኢያአምር ፤ መጽሐፈ ፡ ወአራዊተ ፡ ገዳምኒ ፣ ይመጽኡ፣ ኀቤሁ ፤ በበዘመዶሙ ፡ ወይናዝዞሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ይቀርብ" ፡ ጊዜ ፣ጸሎቱ፤ ፡ ይቀጽቦ ሙ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወየሐውሩ ። ወለእመኒ ፡ ዓባረ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይ ቀውሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይፈቅዱ ፡ ተናብቦቶ ፡ ወያአምር ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ኃሠሣሆሙ ፡ ወያወርድ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ዝናመ ።
[On this day died Abbä Musë (= Moses) of the desert of Sihat (= Scetis). This saint, from the moment he became a monk, left the world, went to the desert, and devoted himself to fasting and prayer to the point that God gave light to his eyes so that he saw an orchard of olive and date trees; each time the new moon rose, he would take what fell from the fruits of these trees; he would eat (it) and it sufficed him as food (which was) as much as the food of a small bird. His clothing (was made) of palm bark and its foliage. The burning heat of summer reached him as well as the cold of winter which (is) very harsh. Despite this, he was assiduous in prayer and fasting. He did not know writing; the animals of the desert would come to him species by species, and he would console them. When the time for his prayer approached, he would signal to them with his hand and they would go away. If (there was) a drought, they would come to him and stand before him as if they wanted to speak to him; he understood their request through the Holy Spirit and would make rain fall for them.
ወነበረ ፤ ከመዝ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ቋወ፳ዓመት ፡ ወቀንዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወተመሰለ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘረስዓ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወተርእዮ ፡ በአንጻረ ፤ በዓቱ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የሐውር" ፡ በበንስቲት ፡ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡፤ ሀለየ ፤ ከመ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡፤ ውእቱ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወወሰዶ‹ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ በዓቱ ። ወነበረ ፡ ሠሉሰ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ኢይጹሊ ፡ ወአራዊትኒ ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ለዝኩ ፤ አረጋዊ ፣፤ ተግሕሥሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ ተስእሎ ፡ ሀገሮ ፡ ወሃይማኖቶ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብሮ ፡፤ ወለአረጋዊኒ" ፡ ተስእሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፣ ውእቱ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡፤ 'ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በገቢረ ፤ ኃጢአት ፤ ብ ዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተፆ ፡ ወወለድኩ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡፡፣"ሶበ ፡ ተዘከርኩ?" ፡ ኅልፈተ ፤ ዓለም ፡ ወፃእኩ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወነበርኩ ፤ ጓዓመተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተዘከርኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወለትየ ፣ወኃጣእኩ ፡ዘያወስባ ፡ ካልአ ፡ዘእንበሌከ ። ወበዝ ፡ ምክንያት ፡መጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፤ እመኒ ፡ ሞትኩ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትቀብረኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለወኒ ፡ አዕፃዳት?፤!፣
He remained thus for thirty-five years and Satan envied him. He took the appearance of an old monk whose days had grown old and he showed himself to him in view of his cave, advancing with small steps. As for this saint, when he saw him, he thought it was a hermit, he went to him and led him into his cave. He remained three days without praying, and the animals that had come to him, when they saw this old man, avoided him. Then the old man asked this saint (what were) his city and his faith; Abbä Musë told him all his deeds and actions and also asked the old man who he was. (The latter) said to him: "I remained in the world committing sin for many years and I fathered a daughter. Then, when I remembered the end of the world, I went into the desert and remained (there) for forty years. When I remembered my daughter, I found no one other than you who could marry her; it is for this reason that I have come to you. If I die, you will bury me, for I have fields and you will inherit everything."
ወአንተ ፡ ትወርስ ፡ ኩሎ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አባ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፎ ፡፣-እገብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡እንዘ ፡ አነ ፣ መነኮስ ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ?፡-?፤ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ስምዓ ፣ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ከመ ፡ አው ሰቡ ፡ እለ ፣፤ አብርሃም ፣ ወሙሴ ፡ ወዳዊት'” ፡ እንዘ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ጸድቃን ። ወበዝን ቱ ፡ አጽነነ ፡ ልቦ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ጽርኃ ፡ ስርግወ ፡ ወውስቴቱ” ፡ ወለት ፤ ሥር ጉት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ወርአዮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ ወቀበሮ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ወለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ መንፈሰ፣ ዓውሎ ፡ ወተነጽሐ ፡ በድኅሬሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ ጽርሐኒ ፡፤ ወገነተኒ ፡ ወወለተኒ ፡ ወአስተዓጺቦ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ይብ ላዕ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ዕፅ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ይበልዖ"”" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መሪረ ፡ውስተ፡፣ አፉሁ ።
Having heard, Abbä Musë was astonished and said to him: "How shall I do that while I am a monk?" The old man produced for him the testimony of the Scriptures according to which Abraham, Moses, and David had married while they were righteous. By this he shook his heart, and he showed him an ornate palace and, inside, (his) daughter adorned, and he was before him as if he were dead. Seeing (him), the saint wept over him, shrouded him, and buried him. When he wanted to enter to the daughter, there was a blast of a storm and he was knocked backward. Then, when his heart had recovered, he found neither the palace nor the garden nor the daughter and, wondering, he went to his dwelling, wanted to take his food from the fruit of the trees that he ate before, and (it) was bitter in his mouth.


[p. 346] {#po.43.p346}
ወሶ[በ ፤ አጽዓቆ” ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ተራከቦ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡፤ በአምሳለ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ነጋዲ ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወውእቱ ፡ ተፅዒኖ ፡ ዕዋለ ። ወኀቤሁ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፤ ወኃደጎ ፡ህየ ።
When hunger pressed him, he went out into the desert and Satan came to meet him again under the appearance of an old traveler who was going to Alexandria riding on a small donkey. He had food and drink. He took him (= Abbä Musë) with him, brought him into the city, and left him there.
ወካዕበ ፡ ተርእዮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡፤ ብእሲት ፡ እንተ ፣ ትቀድሕ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወርእየቶ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ገዳማውያን ። ወተስእለቶ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘ አመክሮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወስሕቀት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በልባ ፡ ወወሰደቶ ፡ ውሰተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወወ ሀበቶ ፡ መብልዓ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወዘከረቶ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ግብረ ፤ አውስቦ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ንዋይ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየት ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ፀነ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አይሁዳዊት ፡ አነ ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አሰፈወቶ ፡፣ በብዙኅ ፣፤ ትምይንት ፡ እስከ ፡ ክህደ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ። ወት ቤሎ፤ ፣፡ ንትመጠው ፡ ንዋይየ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አንበርኩ ፡ ወወሰደቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ብዱት ፡ ኀበ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወዓርገት ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ፡፤ ወተወለጠ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይቤሎ"" ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአስሐትክዎሙ ፡፣ ለብዙኃን ፡ ወ ለከኒ ፤ አግኃሥኩከ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ ወአውፃእኩከ ፡ 'እምገዳም ፤ ወእምሃይማኖትከ' ፡ ናሁ ፤ ትመውት ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወተሐውር፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ "ደይን ፡ ዘገዳም ፡ ወዘገሃነም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተሠወረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ይምነ ፡ ወጽግመ ፡ ወኃጥአ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ ወማየ ፡ ዘይሰቲ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ጸበበቶ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወድቀ ፡ውስተ ፡'መሬተ ፡ምድር ፡ ወዘረወ ፡ መሬተ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወአንገርገረ ፡ እንዘ" ፡ ይበኪ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፈቀደት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ከመ ፡
Satan showed himself to him again under the appearance of a woman who was drawing water; she saw him (= Abbä Musë) under the appearance of the hermits and questioned him about his way of life. He told her all (the snares) that Satan had laid for him and she laughed at him in her heart; she led him into her house, gave him food and drink, and reminded him of the matter of (his) marriage, that she was the daughter of a king who had died and that she had possessions. When she saw that his heart was shaken, she said to him again: "I am Jewish and we are many." Then she made promises to him with much cunning until he renounced his faith. She said to him: "Let us take my possessions from where I have put (them)"; and she led him into the desolate desert, where there is no water; she climbed a mountain and transformed herself upon it. He (= Satan) said to him (= Abbä Musë): "It is I who have deceived many (monks) and, you too, I have turned away from the desert and I have made you leave the desert and your faith. Behold, you will die in this desert and you will go into the torments of the desert and of hell." Having said this, he vanished from his sight. The saint turned to the right and to the left and found no path that he (could) follow nor water that he (could) drink. Then, when the situation had overwhelmed him, he fell into the dust, spread dust on his face, and rolled about weeping to such an extent that his soul wanted to depart.
ትትፈለጥ ። ወሰምዓ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገዓሮ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወልአከ ፡ ዘይናዝዞ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ተኃድገ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኃጢአትከ ። ወእስከ ፡ ርመዋዕል ፡ ተዓርፍ ፡ ወይቀብረከ ፤ ገብርየ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገዳማዊ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገዳ ማዊ” ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ አጋቦስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፣ ዘተሠወረት፤'" ፡ እምዓይነ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወኮነት ፣ መቃብረ ፡ ወጉባኤ፤፤ ፡ ዘገዳማውያን፤ ። ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ሆየ ፡ ረከቡ ፤ ጉባኤ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወአዕረጉ ፡ ቱርባነ ፡ ወተመጠዉ ፡ ምስጢራተ ፡፤ ቅድ ሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰርሐተ፤" ፡ ሕዝብ ፤ ወሖረ፤" ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሴ፤" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡'እመቃብረ ፡ ቅዱሳን፤ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ህየ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ በብረኪሁ ፡ ወበጊዜ ሃ ፡ አፅረፈ ፤ ወቀበሮ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ዜናሁ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይ ምሐሮ፤‹ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
God heard his groans and sent an angel to him who consoled him and said to him: "Your sin is forgiven you. You will die on the third day, and my servant, Sämu'ël the hermit, will bury you." Abbä Sämu'ël the hermit then arrived to him, gave him peace, consoled him, and took him with him to the church that King 'Agäbos had built in the desert, which was hidden from the eyes of men and was the tomb and (the place of) meeting of the hermits. Arrived there, they found the assembly of the saints (men), who were celebrating the (divine) sacrifice, and they received the holy mysteries. When the people were dismissed, Abbä Musë went to receive the blessing of the tomb of the saints. Arrived there, he prostrated himself on his knees and died immediately. Abbä Sämu'ël buried him and wrote his history. May God, by his prayer, have mercy on our king 'Iyäsu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus forever and ever.]
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። < ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይወጥን ፡ አይሉል ፡ ወሻተበር ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አውራጊ
“On this day also begins 'aylul and sätabar (= September) which are the

ሆሙ ፡ ለዮናናውያን ፡ ወዕብራውያን ፡ ወለሰብአ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡፣ ወለሰብእ ፡... መዋዕ ሊሁ፡ቋወ።> አመ ፡ ፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ፤ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ድንግል ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምፕልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳ ሳት ፡ ፳ወ፪ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ ወመስተጋድለ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወ ፈተወ ፡ አልባሰ ፣ ምንኩስና ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጤስ ፤ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስተ" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ 'ዝአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ መቃርስ ። ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በምንኩስና ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ነፍሶ፤፤ ፡ በአንብቦ ፣ መጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፤ ወትርጓሜሆሙ፤ ፡ ወይጠይቅ ፣ ፍካሬሆሙ፤ ። ወተለዓለ ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣፤ ወተሠይመ፤ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ።
months of the Greeks, the Hebrews, the Westerners and the … its days are thirty.> READING FOR THE 4TH OF MASKARAM. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father, virgin, pure, Abbä Magäres (= Macarius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs he is the sixty-ninth. This father was devout and devoted to ascetic combat from his childhood and he desired (to put on) the monastic habit; he went up into the desert of 'Asqêtës (= Scetis) and became a monk in the church of the holy father Abbä Magäres. He led a great ascetic combat in the monastic state and he enlightened his soul by reading the holy Scriptures and their interpretation and by attaching himself to their explanation. He rose by doing what is just and was ordained priest in the desert of 'Asqêtës.
ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ሖሩ፤" ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ "ብዙኃን ፡ ካህናት! ፡ ማእምራን፤' ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይመኖት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በአስቄጥስ፤ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ልሂቃን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘአስቄጥስ ። ወነበሩ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡፤ እንዘ ፡ የሐትቱ ፤ ወይጠይቁ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለዘ ፤ ይጌይሱ! ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቲሚመት ፡፤ ክብርት" ። ወረከብዎ ፡ 'ለዝንቱ ፡ አባ፤፤ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ይይስ።፡ ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ሚመት ። ወተሰናአዉ ፤ ኩሎሙ" ፣ በ፳ምክር ፤ ከመ ፤ ይሚምዎ ፡ በ እንተ ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዘአስቄጥስ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ው እቱ ፡ ይጌይስ ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ሚመት ፡ በእንተ ፣ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ 'ወተጸምዶቱ ፡ ወጽድ ቁ፤ ። ወነሥእዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፣ ወውእቱ" ፡ ይጸርኅ ፡ ወይስእ ል ፤ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግዎ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ 'እምየ ፡ አውሰበት ፡ ፪ዕደወ ፡ ወኢይደልወኒ፤' ፡ ለሚመተ ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ። ወአእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግዎ ። ወመጽኡ" ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእን ቲ[አ]ሁ ፤ ከመ ፡ እሙ ፤ ድንግል፤ ። ወአኀዝዎ፥" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወአብጽ ሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት፥- ። ወእምዝ ፡ አምጽእዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምስር ፤ ወተናበበ ፡ ጦማረ ፤ ሚመቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ 'ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ "ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡፤ ዘማዓልቃ ፡ በልሳነ ፣ ዮናናዊ" ፡ ወቅብጢ ፡ ወዓረብ ።
When Abbä Mikä'ël, the patriarch his predecessor, died, the bishops - and with them many learned (and) orthodox priests - went to the monastery of Abbä Magäres in (the desert of) 'Asqêtës. They gathered in the church with the heads of the monks (of the desert of) 'Asqêtës and remained for many days scrutinizing and examining the history of those who were most suitable for this distinguished office; they found (that) this Abbä Magäres was the most suitable for this office. All, with one opinion, agreed to appoint him because many saints (men) (of the desert) of 'Asqêtës testified concerning him that it was he who was most suitable for this office because of his (ascetic) combat, his devotion, and his justice, they took him against his will and bound him, and he cried out and prayed that they would release him. He said to them: "As for me, my mother married two men and I am not worthy of the patriarchal office." It was known that he said this so that they would release him and many (people) came and testified concerning him that his mother (had been) a virgin. They seized him against his will and brought him to the city of Alexandria and established him as patriarch. They then brought him to Mesr (= Cairo) and his letter of enthronement was read in the church of our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary - Mä'ällegä (= Mo'allagah) - in Greek, Coptic, and Arabic languages.
ወኮነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ይትቄሰክ ፡ በገቢረ፤ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወይት ጋደል ፤ ፈድፋደ ። ወኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡፤ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወ[ይ]ጌሥጾሙ ፡ በፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ወምሕረተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪ ናን ። ወበመዋዕለ ፣ ሚመቱ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ አብያተ፤ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዘይተርፍ ፡ እምጸባሕተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ይሁቦ ፣ ለእግዚኣብ ሔር ፡ ልዑል ። ወነበረ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመተ ፣ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
During all the days of his office, he added to the practice of justice and he performed a great (ascetic) combat. Every day, he taught the people and exhorted them to fear God. He gave alms and mercy to the poor and the needy. During the time of his office, he took nothing from the goods of the churches, but he reserved from his own income (and) gave it to God, the Most High. He remained in his office for twenty-seven years, he pleased God, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever and ever, amen.


[p. 350] {#po.43.p350}
አሜን" ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለስምዖን ፡ ዘጾማዕት ፡ ወኢ ያሱ ፡ወልደ ፡ነዌ ፡ረድኡ ፡ለሙሴ ፡ ወአርዳሴ ፡ሊቀ ፡ጸጳጳሳት ፡፣፻ወዛዘኮኑ" ፡ በአንጾኪያ ፡ ወአንትያ ፡ ዘእምሰብአ ፡፤ እንጽና ፣ ወታኦድሮስ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆይፅስ፤፤ ፡ ዘመቃብረ ፡ እልዳኑ ፡ ወቴዎጎሎስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይምሐሮ፤፤ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓጊም ራ ቾሜን ።
[On this day also is the commemoration of Sem'on of the cell (= Simeon), (of) 'Iyäsu (= Joshua) son of Nawë (= Nun), the disciple of Moses, (of) 'Ardäsë the patriarch, (of the) one hundred and fifty who were in 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), (of) 'Anteyä who (was) of the inhabitants of 'Ensenä (= Antinoë), (of) Tä'odros (= Theodore), bishop of the tomb of 'Eldänu and (of) Téëwogolos (= John the Theologian?). May God, by their prayer, have mercy on our king 'Iyäsu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus forever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 5TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፳ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሶፊያ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፣ አረማዊት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አግዋሪሃ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወሖረት ፡፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኅበ ፤ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአምነት ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሖረት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኤስ ፡ቆይፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ መንፍ ፡ ወአጥመቃ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትና ፡ በስመ ፡አብ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy Sofiyä (= Sophia) became a martyr. This saint was a pagan and her neighbors were Christians. She went many times with them to the church and believed in our Lord Christ. She went to the bishop of the city of Manef (= Upper Menouf) and he baptized her with Christian baptism in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit.
ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወኮነት ፡ ወትረ ፤ ተሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አስተዋደይዋ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገላውድዮስ ፤ መኩንን ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ኮነት ፡ ክርስቲ ያናዊተ ። ወአብጽሓ ፤መኩንን ፡ ወተስእላ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወኢክሕደት ፡ አላ ፡፤ አ ምነት ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወኩነና ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወቀሠፋ ፡ በጥብጣቤ ፡ ዘአልህምት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንደዱ ፡ አብትርተ ፡ ዘሐጺን ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወወደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መልያልያቲሃ ፡ ወሰቀልዋ ። ወኮነት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ትጸርኅ ፡ ወትቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ክርስቲያናዊት ። ወአዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ልሳና ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ልሳና ። ወእምዝ ፡ ...]፡'ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ብእሲቶ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሒጣ ፡ ወተካየደታ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኪዳናተ ፡ ብ ዙኃተ ፤ ወኢተወክፈት ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተካየደታ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እም
She went constantly to the church; wicked people accused her before Galäwdyos (= Claudius) the governor, and they told him that she was a Christian. The governor had her brought and questioned her about this matter; she did not deny but confessed our Lord Christ. He (= the governor) made her undergo great tortures on many occasions and (had) her flogged with a bull's nerve. Then, iron rods were heated in the fire, they were put into her joints, and she was suspended. She cried out in these tortures and said: "I am a Christian." The governor ordered that her tongue be cut off and her tongue was cut off. Then, [he threw her] into prison and sent his wife to her to abuse her; the latter made many promises to her, but she (= Sofyä) accepted nothing of what she had promised her. When he was weary of torturing her, he ordered that her
ኩነኔሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወጸለየት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ጸሎተ ፡ ነዊሃ ፡ ወሰአለቶ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወለሰራዊቱ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመት ፡ ጸሎታ ፡ አድነነት ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሰያፊ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሳ ።
head be cut off with the sword. The holy Sofyä made at that moment a long prayer and asked God to have mercy on the king and his soldiers because of her. After having finished her prayer, she inclined her head toward the executioner and he cut off her head.


[p. 352] {#po.43.p352}
ወሀለወት ፡ በህየ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ መሃይምንት ፡ ወሀበት ፡ ንዋየ ፡፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ለሐራሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ወገነዘቶ ፡ በግንዘታት ፡ ክቡራት ፡ ወአንበረቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ በዓላ ፡ ለለ ፤ ዓመት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እምሥጋሃ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡፤ ብዙኃት ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይሬእዩ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓላ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወይበጽሕ ፤ እስከ ፡ሥጋሃ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዕጣን ፡ ዘዕፍረት ፡ ጹናሁ ፡-ምዑዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ዜናሃ ፣፡ ፈነወ ፡ ወአፍለሰ ፣፤ ሥጋሃ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆቀስጠንጥንያ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ሠናይተ ፡ ወአንበረ ፤ ሥጋሃ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ጸሉታ ፡
There was there a believing woman, she gave much money to the king's soldiers, took the pure body of the holy Sofyä, shrouded it in precious linens, and placed it in her house. On the day of her feast, every year, many miracles manifested from her body. All the people saw, on the day of her feast, a great light like a column (descending) from heaven and (which) reached the body of the saint, and a perfume of aromatics with a sweet odor came out of it. When Quastantinos (= Constantine), the righteous king, ascended the throne and heard her story, he sent (messengers) and (had) her holy body transported to the city of Constantinople; he built for her a beautiful church and placed her holy body there. May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ" ፡ ፅለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ወ፪አዋልዲሃ ፡ ወስሞን ፡ አክሶስያ ፡ወበርናባ ። ዛቲሰ ፡ኮነት ፣፤አረማዊተ ፡ወያመልኩ ፡አበዊሃ ፣ጣዖታተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ቆጾስ ፡ ዘመኖፌ ፡ወተአምነት ፡በክር ስቶስ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ አመንኩ ፡ በአምላክከ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መሐራ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ሕገ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወአድያሚሃ ፣ ወአጥመቃ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አዋልዲሃ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወበአንተዝ ፣፤ አስተዋደይዋ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ከላድያኖስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፣ ኮነት!" ፡ክርስቲያናዊተ ፡ ወኃደገ[ት ፤ ሰጊደ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፤ ወመኩንንሰ ፡ ሐተታ ፡ ወተአመነት ፡ወኢክህደት ፡ ወኩነና ፡'ዙነኔ ፡ ዐቢየ ፣ወብዙኃ፤! ፡ምስለ ፡አዋልዲ ሃ ። ወይእቲሰ ፡ ታስተዔግሦን ፡ ወትቤሎን ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ አዋልድየ ፡ ወተዓገሣ ፡ ከመ ፡ አዋልዲሃ ፡ 'ለሶፍያ ፡ ለእግዝእትየ፤፤ ፡ አንጌቤናዊት፤" ፣ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ መኩሲሃ ፡ ወአንትንሂ ፤ ተመሰላ ፡ በአዋልዲሃ ፡ ኢስ ፡ ጢስ ፡ አላኢስ ፣ አጋሏስ፤ ፡ ወእማን ቱሂ ፡ አውሥኣሃ ፡ ወይቤላሃ፤ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንተሉ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ዚአኪ ። ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ተሰዓማ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሞን ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮን ፡ መኩንን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአምኃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይቅሥፍዋ ፡ ለእሞን፤ ፣ በአስዋጥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርሆን ፡ ለእማንቱ ፡፣ ደናግል ፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ ርእያ!" ፣ አዋልዲሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወረደ ፡፣ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሠወራ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ አመ ፡ አዕረቅዋ ፡ ልብሳ"፤፣ ረሲኣን ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ይቅሥፍዋ ፡ ወይእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ቀሠፍዋ! ። ወተፈሥሓ'፣
[On this day became martyrs the holy Sofyä and her two daughters whose names (are) 'Aksosyä and Barnäbä. This (saint) was a pagan and her parents worshipped idols. After that, she came to the bishop of Manofë (= Upper Menouf) and believed in Christ. She said to him: "I believe in your God." After that, he taught her all the law of the Church and its limits (= the regions where it was followed?), and baptized her with her daughters in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. Because of this, she was accused before Kalädyänos (= Claudianus), the governor of the city, of being a Christian and of having abandoned the worship of idols. The governor questioned her and she confessed (it) and did not deny (it), he made her undergo great and many tortures with her daughters. She exhorted them and said to them: "Be strong, my daughters, and be enduring like the daughters of Sofyä, of my mistress 'Angéëbéënäwit. Indeed, I am her namesake and, you too, you resemble her daughters Pistis, 'Aläpis, and 'Agäpis." They answered and said to her: "Do not be afraid, for we will follow in your footsteps." Saying this, they exchanged kisses with their mother. When the governor saw them kissing, he ordered to flog their mother with rods to terrify the virgins. Her daughters (= those of Sofyä) then saw that an angel of God was descending and hiding the saint while the impious were stripping her of her clothing to flog her and, at that moment, they flogged her. They rejoiced and exulted.
ወተሐዎሥያ ። ወእሞንሂ"፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀሥፍዋ ፡ ወኢያንተገት ፡ ጸሪኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያና ዊት ፡ይእቲ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡መኩንን ፡ያውቅዩ ፡ልሳና ፡እምጐንዳ ፡ወከመዝ ፡ ገብሩ ። 'ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝኒ" ፡ ኢኃደገት ፡ ጸሪኃ ፡ ወተነግሮ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡
As for their mother, while she was being flogged, she did not cease to cry out that she was a Christian. The governor then ordered to cut off her tongue from its root, and it was done so. And, even after that, she did not cease to cry out and to


[p. 354] {#po.43.p354}
አዘዘ ፡ ይስድዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወለአከ ፡ 'ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ" ፡ ብእሲቶ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡-ከመ ፡ ትቲጣ ፡ ወሶበ ፡[ኢ]ተሰጥወታ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ርአሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ፤ ወመተርዋ ፤ ወነሥአታ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ለሥጋሃ ፡፤ ወአብአታ"" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ። ወሠርዓት ፡ ላቲ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ወማዕጠንተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖን ፡ ለአዋልዲሃ ፡ ወአፍርሆን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ ይቤላሁ ። ወሶበ ፡፣ አበያሁ ፤ መተረ ፡ አርእስቲሆን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተታልዎን ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አሐቲ ። ወከመዝ" ፡ ፈጸማ ፡ ስምዖን ፣ ወሥጋሆንሰ ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡፤ መሃይምንት ፡፤ ወደመረቶን ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ እሞን ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ተኣምር ፡ በውስተ ፡ መቃብሪሆን ፡ ዘይፌውስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዱያነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ወነሥአ” ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወአፍለሳ ፣ ቀሩስጥንጥንያ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ብጽሐተ" ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ሐነጹ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያነ ። ወይጽሕፉ ፡ ሰርከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ፤ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት፡፣ "ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቄስጠንጢኖስ ። ወይወርድ ፡ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወይደመስሶ ፡ ወይጽሕፍ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘሶፍያ ፡ ወኃጥኡ ፡ ኬነ ውት ፡ዘይገብሩ ፡ወኃደጉ ፡ ጽሒፈ ። ወአሐተ ፡ዕለተ ፡እንዘ ፡ይትዋነይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በባሕቲቱ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሩፋኤል ፡፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ ስማ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡፤ ቤት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ኢያእመርኩ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ መልአከ፥ ፡ እግዚአብሔር?" ፡ በሎ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ ስምያ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሶፍያ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ትጸንሐኒሁ፥ ፡ እስከ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ዘንተ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወቀተሎ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይእትት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ መካን ፡ በምክንያተ ፡
(= to the governor) speak. After that, he ordered to lead her to prison and he dispatched at that moment his wife to her to abuse her and, as she (= Sofyä) did not receive her, he ordered to cut off her head with a sword, and it was cut off (for her). A woman took her body, carried it into her house, and arranged for it a lamp and an incense burner. Then, (the governor) summoned her daughters and frightened them so that they would say yes to him. As they refused (it) to him, he (had) their heads cut off by making them succeed one another, one after the other. They thus finished their martyrdom. As for their bodies, the believing woman took (them) and joined them to (that of) their mother. Many miracles manifested on their tomb which healed all the sick. When Quastantinos ascended the throne, he learned the story of the holy Sofyä, (had) her body taken and (had) it transported to Constantinople. Before the coming (to the knowledge of the people) of her story, a church was built and these words were written in the evening: "This holy church (is) that of Quastantinos"; an angel of God descended, erased them, and wrote these words: "This house (is) that of Sofyä." The workers did not know what to do and left the inscription. One day, while the king's son was playing alone, the angel of God whose name is Rufä'êl (= Raphael) appeared to him and said to him: "What is the name of this house?" The child said to him: "I do not know." The angel of God said to him: "Tell your father (that) its name (is) the house of Sofyä." The child said to him: "Will you wait for me until I return?" (The angel) said to him: "Yes." Hearing this, his father knew that it was an angel and he killed his son because he said (to himself) that the angel would not leave this place for the reason that he was waiting for the child.
ጸኒሖቱ ፡ ለሕፃን፤ ። ወለይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ኮነ ፡ ኑኃ ፡፪፻ወ፪ 'በአመት ። ወግድማ ፡፻፻ወ፪ ወአዕማዲሃ ፡የ፻፻ጅወኒ ወኃዋኅዊኃኒ ፡ ወጠረጴዛቲሃኒ ፡ ቿ ወባሕርያቲሃኒ ፡8ወኪሩ ቤልኒ ፤ ዘመልዕልተ ፡ ምስሐል ፡ በበ፪ክንፍ ፡ ይጹልሉ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ሕጉ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ። ወአብኡ ፡ ሀየ ፥ ሣፁና ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አዋልዲሃ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ክብር ፡ወስብሐት ፡ወለይእቲ ፡ብእሲት ፡ዘአስተጋብአት ፡ አብድንቲሆን ፡ከበርዋ ፡ ምስሌሆን ። "ጸሎቶን ፡ ወበረከቶን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ለእሎንቱ ፡ አንስት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፣ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
The length of this church is 709 cubits, its width 309, its columns (are in number of) 367, its doors (of) 4, its altars (of) 8, its fountains (of) 4, and the cherubim who (are) above the mercy seat overshadow, each with two wings, the ark of the law of God. They brought here the sarcophagus of the holy Sofyä and of her daughters in great honor and glory. As for the woman who had gathered their remains, she was buried with them. May the prayer and the blessing of these holy women be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus, forever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማማስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ፣ ቅዱስ፥" ፡ ኮነ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለኡርያኖስ ፣፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሜሞ" ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አውላጦስ ። ወለዝንቱሰ፤፥፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ወበእንተ" ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ በክርስቶሰ፥ ፡ ሞቅሕዎሙ ፡ውስተ ፣
On this day also Saint Mämäs (= Mammes) became a martyr. This saint lived in the time of the reign of the emperor 'Uryänos (= Julian) and he (= 'Uryänos) had made him governor of the land of 'Awlätos (= Paphlagonia?). The parents of this saint were Christians and, because of their faith in Christ, they were incarcerated


[p. 356] {#po.43.p356}
ቤተ ፤ ሞቅሕ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አዕረፉ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡፤ ነሥአቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ አሐቲ ፡፤ ብእሲት ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ ወሐ ጸነቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወሰመየቶ፤፥" ፡ ማማስ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ እጓለ ፡ ማውታ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አበ ፤ ወኢእመ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ አስተዋ ደይዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡"ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ፥ ፡ ወአኀዝዎ ፣ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ በአብትር ። ወሰቀሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ሰክመ፤ ፡ ክቡደ" ፡ ዓረር ፡ ወገደፍዎ ፡-ውስተ ፡ባሕር ፡ ወድኅነ ፡እምባሕር ፡ በኀይለ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወተኃብ አ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡ በሐሊበ ፡ ጋሙስ ፡-እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አልህምት ፤ ጸሊማት ፡ ዘጐስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አኃዝዎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወደኅነ ፣ 'እም[እ]ሳት ፤ በኀይለ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር” ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰደዱ” ፡ አናብስተ ፡ከመ ፡ይምስጥዎ ፡ ወበኀይለ ፡እግዚኣብሔር ፤ ተጽዕነ[]ዲበ ፤ አንበሳ ። ወእምዝ ፣፤ ኩነንዎ ፡ ወሰሐቡ?፤-?። ፡ መላያልያቲሁ”' ፡
in prison. After his father and mother died in prison, a Christian woman took in this blessed one, raised him as her son, and called him Mämäs - which means orphan; indeed, he had neither father nor mother. When he was fifteen years old, he was accused before the governor as being a Christian; they seized him, beat him with sticks, hung a heavy load of lead around his neck, and threw him into the sea. He was saved from the sea by the power of God and hid under (?) a cave. He fed on the milk of the gämus which are black cows which (are) buffaloes. They then took him again and threw him into a furnace of fire and he was saved from the fire by the power of God. They then brought lions so that they would tear him to pieces and, by the power of God, he mounted on the back of a lion. Then, they tortured him and tore out his entrails with a three-pointed iron instrument and, by that, he finished his combat and his martyrdom. May his prayer be with us, amen.
