Lord Jesus Christ raised him from the dead, intact (and) without harm. All the people who were dwelling there, when they saw this, marvelled greatly and believed in Christ, and they cursed the governor. He became angry with them and ordered his soldiers to cut off their heads with the sword, and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Their number (was) 14,700 men (and) 37 women. Then, they threw the saint Fäsiladas into a large cauldron and cooked him until he became liquid and was like water. The governor ordered a well to be dug on the mountain and for him to be buried there, and it was done so. Then our Lord Christ raised the saint Fäsilädäs from the dead again. He came, stood before the governor, cried out, and said: "Be ashamed, Governor Madures, as well as your renegade emperor and your impure idols. Indeed, behold, our Lord Jesus Christ has raised me from the dead, intact (and) without harm." When the people who were there saw this miracle, they believed in our Lord Christ and became martyrs. Their number (was) 2300. Then, he (= the governor) had the saint Fäsilädäs mounted on a wheel in which (there was) a saw and subjected him to great torture. Then, they laid him on an iron bed and lit a fire under him.
ዓራት ፤ ሐጺን ፡ ወአንደዱ ፡ 'መትሕቴሁ ፡ እሳተ” ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ?" ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአንሥ ኦ ፡ 'እምውስተ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ሙስና ፡ ወተካየዶ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ ፋሲላዳስ ፡ አእምር ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ አው ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ለነዳይ፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፡ 'አው ፡ ይሁብ ፣ አል ባሰ ፣ ለምስኪናን ፡ አው ፣ ይሁብ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በዕለተ ፡ "ተዝካርከ ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ እመሂ ፡ በሕዳጥ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡፤ አንሰ5" ፡ አኀድግ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አበሳሆሙ ፤ ወእሬሲ ፥ ማኅደሮሙ ፡ ምስሌከ ፤ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዓርገ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት" ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ፋሲላዳስ ፡
Our Lord Christ—glory to him!—then appeared to him and raised him from this torture intact (and) without harm. He made him a promise and said to him: "O my chosen Fäsilädäs, know that whoever shall perform your commemoration or give alms to a poor person in your name or give clothes to the needy or give incense to a church on the day of your commemoration, and all those who shall perform your commemoration, whether with little (or) with much, (to those then) I will forgive all sins and I will establish their dwelling with you in the kingdom of heaven." After saying this to him, our Lord ascended into the heavens in great glory. The saint Fäsilädäs, when he heard this from our Lord, felt great joy.
ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ፤ ፡ እምነ”፤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተማከረ ፣፤ መፁርስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ምስለ ፣ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ ምንተዝ ፡ እገብር ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ" ፡ ፋሲላዳስ ፡ እስመ ፡ 'ኢተርፈ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፤ እምኩነኔ ፡ 'ዝእንበለ ፤ ዘኩንንክዎ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ፡ እም ክሩ ። ወአምከርዎ ፤ እሙንቱ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ምትር ፡ ርእሶ፤ ፡ ወአዕረፍ፤"፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ኃልቁ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ አዘዞሙ፤።= ፣ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ይምትሩ ፡ርእሶ ፡'ለቅዱስ ፡ፋሲላዳስ ፡ወመተሩ ፡
Then, Madures the governor consulted with his officers and said to them: "What am I going to do with this man, Fäsilädäs, for there is no torture left besides (those) with which I have tortured him, and he has not turned from his opinion?" They advised him and said to him: "Cut off his head and be delivered from him, for behold, the people of this city have perished because of him." The governor then ordered them to cut off the head of the saint Fäsilädäs, and they cut off his head


[p. 396] {#po.43.p396}
ርእሶ፤ቦ ፣ በሰይፍ፤ ፡ ወነሥአ፤ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፤ ሀየንተ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ምድራዊት ፡ 'ዘኃደጋ ። ወኮነ፤"ቦ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዓበይተ ፤ ወኖልቆሙ!፤0" ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ምስ ሌሁ ፡ '፪፻፻ወጓ፻፳ወ፪፤" ። 'ጻሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ!0 ፡ ወትንብልናሁ!፤!" ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
with the sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven in place of the earthly royalty he had abandoned. There were great miracles and wonders from his body. The number of all those who were martyrs with him (is) 24,067. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በነፍስ፤፤፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ጹናሃ ። ዛቲ፤፤። ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ አረጋዊት ፡ ወልህቅት ፡ በመዋዕሊሃ ፡ ወተጋደለት ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለሳቦር፤፤ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ። እስመ ፡ ኮኑ፤፤ ፡ አፄ ውዋ!፤ ፡ ወአሰርዋ ፡ ምስለ ፡ 3፻!፤!ክርስቲያን ፡ እለ ፡ አፄወዎሙ!፤" ፡ ወኩነና ፤ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ መተረ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ መተሩ፤፤ ፡ ርአእሳ፤፤-፤፡0 ፣ ዓርገ!፤፤ ፡ ደም ፡ ዘውሕዘ ፡ እምክሳዳ ፡ እስከ ፤ መጠነ ፤ ርሑቅ ፡፤ ወደክመ ፡ ኀይሎሙ ፡ ለጸላእያን ፡ እለ፤ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ! ፡ ወጸልመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ፀሐይ ፣ ወመልአ፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ወካን ፡ ዕፍረት ፤ ምዑዝ፤ ፡ ናሁ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also the saint Banafes—which means "whose odor is sweet"—became a martyr. This saint was old and advanced in her days and she lived in the time of Säbor (= Sapor), the king of Färs (= Persia). She was taken into captivity and chained with 9000 Christians who had been taken into captivity. The army chief subjected her to great torture, then had her head cut off with the sword. After her head had been cut off, the blood that flowed from her neck rose up to a distance. The strength of the enemies, who were the soldiers dwelling there, weakened, the light of the sun darkened, and the place was filled with a perfume of sweet odor. May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፣፤ ጀገባራውያን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስና ፡ እለ ፡ ስሞሙ!፤፡ ፡ ሱርስ ፡ ወአንጢክዮስ ፣ ወመስሐድሬ!" ። ወእንዘ ፣ ይትመየጥ ፡ አር ያኖስ ፤ እመንገለ ፡ ደቡብ ፤ ቀተለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትርፍ ፡ እስከ ፥ደክሙ ፡ኩሉ፤፤ ፡ሐራሁ ፡ወአብኡ ፡ሰይፎሙ ፡ውስተ ፡ቤቱ ፡ወተራከብ ዎ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፻ገባራውያን፤ ፡ ማእከለ ፣ ተንከተም ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓትዉ ፡ እምሐቅል ፤ ወጸርሑ፤ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ገሀደ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ይቤ ሎሙ፤፤፤ ፣ ለመኳንንቲሁ ፡ ኢትሰምዑኑ ፡ እሎንተ ፡ ገባረ ፡ መፍትውኬ ፡ ትቅትል ዎሙ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡'ደከምነ ፣ ወአስይፍቲነኒ ፡ አባእነ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቀቀባሆሙ ፤ ወሰሚዖሙ ፤" ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ነዋ፤ ፡ መክርይነ ፡ ቅትሉነ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡አንበሩ ፣ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡መልዕልተ ፡እብን ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ከሳውዲ ሆሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይምሀሮሙ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡
[On this day also three peasants from the city of 'Esnä (= Esna), whose names (were) Sures, 'Antikyos, and Mashadrë, became martyrs. While 'Aryänos (= Arianus) was returning from the north (of Egypt), he killed all the people of the city without (a single one) remaining, so that all his soldiers were tired and put their swords back in their sheaths. These three peasants met him in the middle of the bridge returning from the fields. They cried out and said: "We are Christians publicly." Hearing (this), 'Aryänos said to his officers: "Have you not heard these peasants? You must kill them." His soldiers said to him: "We are tired and our swords, we have put them back in their sheaths." Hearing (this), the saints said: "Here are our spades, kill us with them." Then they placed their heads on a stone and (the soldiers) cut their necks. May God have mercy, through their prayer, on our king 'lyäsu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ፍቁሩ ፡፤ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፤ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ ቆርኖልዮስ፤ ፡ ሊቀ -፡፣ ሐራ ። ዝንቱ፤ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነ! ፡ በመዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወአስመሮ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ወአስተርአዮ ፤ መልአክ ፣ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ! ፣ ወይጴጹውዖ!"0 ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ሐዋርያ፤፤ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይስማዕ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘይደሉ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወነገሮ! ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ! ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ቃለ ፣
On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the saint Qornolyos (= Cornelius) the captain. This righteous man lived in the time of the holy apostles. His life was beautiful and he pleased God. An angel appeared to him and ordered him to send (men) and invite Pëtros (= Peter) the apostle to come to him so that

"እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ወአምነ! ፣ ወተጠምቀ!" ፡ 'ወኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ! ፡ ቤቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ "ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሐዋርያ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ረከባ ፡ ምልእት ፡ በአምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታት፤የ ። ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውስቴታ፤፤! ፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወአምነ ፡ ዲማጥርዮስ፤ ፡ መኩንን ፤ ወአጥመቆ፤ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና፤ ፡ ወለኩሉ፤ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ቤቱ ። ወፈጸመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋ ፅለ! ፣ ሕይወቱ፤፤" ፡ በከመ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ወውእቱ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘአምነ ፡ እምነ ፤ አሕዛብ፤ ። በረከቱ፤ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡-አዕረፈት ፡ቅድስት ፣ታኦዶራ፤" ። ዛቲ ፡ቅድስት፤፤ ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ፤ ፡ ለዚኖን፤ ፡ ንጉሥ፤" ፡ አግበራ፤ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ እምታ ፡ ወአማሰና ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሐዘነት ፡ 'ሐዘነ ፡ ዓቢየ፤ ፡ ወነስሐት ፡ ወበከየት ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ፅደው ፡፡'በኅቡእ ፡ ወወፅአት፤" ፡'እምሀገረ ፡-እስክንድርያ፤ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወሰመየት ፡ ስማ፤ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ። ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወለብሰ ት ፤ አስኬማ፤ ፡ ምንኩ[ስ]ነ፤"፤-፤"፤ ፡ ዘመላእከት ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይሬእይዋ፤ ፡ የሐልዩ! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ፤ ፡ ሕፅው ። ወይእቲሰ፤ ፡ ተፀም ደት ፤ ወተጋደለት ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወተዐገሠት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጽምእ ፡ ወረኃብ ፤ ወትጋህ ፡ በ]ሌሊት ፤ ወቀዊም ፡ በመዓልት ። ወነበረት ፡ ከመዝ!?'" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትፀመድ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወኮነ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘመወ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ብእ ሲት ፡ ወፀንሰት ፡፤ እምኔሁ ፡ ወወለደት ፡ ወልደ ። ወይቤልዋ ፡ አበዊሃ ፣፤ መኑ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናኪ ። ወተናገረት ፡ በሓሰት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወትቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ "ውስተ ፣ ደብር፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ አማሰነኒ ፡ ወፀነስኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን፤ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልዱ ፣ ለቴዎድሮስ፤ ፣፤ መነኮስ ፡ ወልድከ ። ወጸውዓ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሕሱመ ፡ ወአምጻእከ ፡ ኃፍረተ፤፤፤ ፡ ላዕለ ፡"ዙሎሙ ፡፣ መነኮሳት፤ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ያአምር ፡ከመ ፡!ይእቲ ፡-ብእሲት፤ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ታኦዶራ፤ ፥ አበስኩ ፡ 'መሐረኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ፤ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፤ ወወሀባ ፡ 'ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን፤ ፡ ወሰደዳ ፡ እምደብር ። ወነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ገዳም ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ወተዐገሠት ፤
he (= Qornolyos) might hear from him what was fitting. When Pëtros the apostle came to him and told him, and all those who were dwelling with him, the word of our Lord Jesus Christ, he believed and was baptized with all the people of his house. Then Pëtros the apostle appointed him in the church of the city of Alexandria. When he arrived there, he found (the city) full of the worship of idols. He baptized many people there with Christian baptism. Dimätreyos (= Demetrius) the governor believed and he (= Qornolyos) baptized him with Christian baptism, (him) and all the people of his house. He completed all the days of his life according to the struggle of the apostles. He is the first of the Gentiles who believed. May his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the saint Tä'odorä (= Theodora). This saint was from the city of Alexandria; in the time of Zinon (= Zeno) the emperor, a man (other than her husband) did violence to her, took her from her husband, and dishonored her. She then felt great sorrow, did penance, and wept bitter tears. She put on men's clothing in secret, left the city of Alexandria under the guise of a man, and took the name of Tëwodros (= Theodore). She entered a monastery of monks and put on the monastic habit—that of the angels. All those who saw her thought she was a eunuch. As for her, she devoted herself to asceticism, led a beautiful struggle, and endured thirst, hunger, night vigils, and standing during the day. She remained thus, devoting herself to asceticism for many years. It happened (that) a man fornicated with a woman and she became pregnant by him and gave birth to a child. Her parents said to her: "Who is the one who destroyed your virginity?" She spoke falsely against this saint and said: "Tëwodros, the monk who lives in the monastery, (it is) he (who) corrupted me and I became pregnant by him." When her parents heard these words, they were very indignant, took the child, brought him to the superior of the convent, and said to him: "Take this child, for he is the son of Tëwodros the monk, your (spiritual) son." The superior called this saint and said to her: "Why have you done this abominable thing and brought shame (thus) upon all the monks?" He did not know that she was a woman. The saint Tä'odorä said to him: "I have sinned, have mercy on me, my father." The superior then became angry with her, gave her the child, and drove her from the monastery. She remained in the desert for seven years and the child (was) with her. She endured many temptations from Satan and (the demons) tormented her and subjected her to great tribulations.
ላዕለ ፡ መከራ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ "እምነ ፡ ሰይጣን፤ ፡ ወአመንደብዋ ፡ ወኩነንዋ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተወክፍዋ ፡ ወአብእዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዳግመ ። ወነ በረት ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፣ ወመጠወት ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ "እዴሁ ፡፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር፤ጾ ። ወነሥአት ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ምስለ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ቅዱሳን ፡ ወፈጸመት ፡ ተጋድሉታ ፡ ሠናየ ። ጸሎታ ፤ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
After that, they received her and made her re-enter the monastery. She remained (there) a few days, died, committed her soul into the hand of God, received eternal life with all the saints, and completed her beautiful struggle. May her prayer be with us, amen.


[p. 400] {#po.43.p400}
1ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ!" ፡ ባስልዮስ ፤ ወታዎድሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፣ ዘኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወቀውስጦስ ፡ርአእሰ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወእለ ፤ ምስሌሁ!፤ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the saint Bäselyos (= Basil) and (of) Täwodros (= Theodore), the martyr bishop of Jerusalem, and (of) Qawstos (= Caustus), chief of the martyrs of the city of Alexandria, and (of) those who (were) with him. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 12TH OF MASKARAM
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ በዓሉ" ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክ ት ። እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አሞጽ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወተሣሃለ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፤ ወመሐሮ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፤ ፳ወቿዓመተ ፡፤ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይዜንዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈወሶ ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ወወሰኮ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ አውሰበ ፡ ወወለዶ ፡ ለምናሴ ። ትንብል ናሁ” ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day is the feast of the glorious angel, Mikä'el (= Michael) the archangel. [Indeed, on this day, God—glory to him!—sent him to 'Isäyeyäs (= Isaiah), son of 'Amos (= Amos), the prophet. He (= God) forgave him and had mercy on him after having been angry with him for twenty-eight years. He ordered him to go to Hezqeyäs (= Hezekiah) the king and announce to him that God was healing him of his illness and adding fifteen years to his days so that he (could) marry and beget Menäsé (= Manasseh).] May his intercession be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፪፻ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ በሀገረ ፤ ኤፌሶን ፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ሣልሳይ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ 'እምነ ፡ ጉባእያን ፡ ዓቢያን ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ ወልደ!‹" ፣ አርቃድዮስ ፡፤ ወልደ ፤ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ተጋብኦቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንስጡርስ?" ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡'ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ! ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ። ወስሕተ" ፡ ወይቤ ፤ ከመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት፤ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኢወለደቶ ፡ ለአምላ ክነ! ፡ በሥጋ ፡ አላ ፡ ወለደቶ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዕራቁ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኃደረ ፡ ውስ ቴቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አኮ ፡ ኃደረ፤" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ አላ ፡ ኃደረ ፤ በፈቃድ ፣ ወበሥምረት ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ወበእንተዝ"" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ ጠባይዓተ ፡፤ ወክልኤተ ፡፣ ሕላዌ!" ። ዛቲ ፣ ሃይማኖቱ" ፡ እኪት ፡ ለንስ
On this day also there was the assembly of the council of the saints, the two hundred bishops, in the city of 'Éféson (= Ephesus). This was the third assembly among the great councils. They (= the bishops) gathered in the twentieth year of the reign of Tëwodosyos (= Theodosius) the Younger, the son of 'Argädyos (= Arcadius), son of Tëwodosyos the Great. Their assembly took place because of Nestures (= Nestorius) who was patriarch of the city of Constantinople. He was wandering and saying that our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, had not given birth to our God in the flesh, but (that) she had given birth to a simple man and (that), after that, the Son of God had dwelt in him—he had not dwelt (in him) and been one with the flesh, but he had dwelt (in him) by (his) will and by (his) good pleasure only; and, because of this, Christ was of two natures and two persons. That is the evil belief of Nestures the impious.
ጡርስ፤ ፡ ከሓዲ ። ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ እሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ፪፻ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፤ ወተዋሥኡ ፡
It is because of him that these two hundred father bishops gathered; they
ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአጽንዑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአጠየቁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ "እምነ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘተሰብአ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአምጽ ኡ፣፤ ሎቱ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቃል ፡ ገብርኤል ፤ መልአክ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘሉቃስ ። እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ምስሌኪ ፡ ወዘይትወለድ ፡ እምኔኪ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወቃለ ፤ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በትንቢቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ትፀንስ ፡ ወትወልድ ፡ ወልደ" ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ኣማኑኤል ። ወካዕበ ፣፤ ይቤ” ፡ ይወፅእየ ፡ ሥርው፤ ፡ እምኤሴይ ፡ ወዘይቀውም?" ፡ እምኔሁ ፣፤ ይከውን ፥፣ ተስፋሆሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ፥ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ገሠጾ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ" ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ጸጳጳሳት ፡ ዘ[ሀ]ገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡-እስቱ ራ ሾባይዓትቴ ራኢይትታቿጡ ኸእምድኅቷ ራኮኑ ኹጉገዴ ኹጁጌዋሕዶቶሙ ሬላ?" ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ነአምን ፡ ወንቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘተሰብአ ፡ አሐቲ ፡
discussed with him, proved to him, and demonstrated to him that he who is born of our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, is God who became man. They brought him the testimony of the word of Gabr'ël (= Gabriel) the angel when he said to our Lady Mary in the holy gospel of Lugäs (= Luke): "God (is) with you, and he who will be born of you is holy and he will be called the Son of God." (They also cited to him) the word of 'Isäyeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet in his prophecy: "Behold, a virgin shall conceive and give birth to a son and he shall be called by the name of 'Amänu'ël (= Emmanuel)." He also says: "A rod shall come out of 'Éséy (= Jesse) and he who will arise from it will be the hope of the nations." Our father, the saint abbä Qêrellos (= Cyril), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, also exhorted him on this subject, made an exposition for him, and said to him: "The natures are not separated after having become one by their union; on the contrary, we believe and we say that the Word of God who became incarnate is one single nature."


[p. 402] {#po.43.p402}
ጠባይዓትቶ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወንስጡርስሰ፥ ፣ ከሐዲ ፣ ኢተወክፈ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ፡፤ እምክ ሕደቱ ። ወገሠጽዎ ፡ 'ኩሉ ፡ ጉባኤ፤፣ ከመ፡፣ ይምትርዎ ፡ እምክህነቱ ፡ ወሚመቱ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢተመይጠ ፡ እምክሩ ፡ እኩይ ። ወውእቱሰ፡= ፡ ኢሰምዓ ፣ ወኢተወክፈ፥፤ ፡ ቃሎሙ፤ ፣ 'ለሹሎሙ ፡ ጉባኤ- ። ወእምዝ ፡ መተርዎ ፡ ለንስጡርስ ፡ እምክህነቱ ፣፤ ወአውገዝዎ ፡ ወረገምዎ ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፣ እምሚመቱ፥" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሞተ ፡ በህየ ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ 'እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወእሉሰ ፤ አበው ፡ ፪፻ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ 'ወአጽንዕዋ ፡ ለሃይማ ኖት ፡ 'ወጸሐፉ ፣፤ ወአጠየቁ" ፡'በዝንቱ ፣፤ ጉባኤ፤ ፡ ወይቤሉ ። እስመ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፤ ማርያም፣ ወለደቶ ፤ ለቃለ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘተሠገወ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሠርዑ ፡ ቀኖና ፣፤ ወሕገ ፡ ወእሙንቱ- ፡ በእዴዊሆ ሙዶ9 ፣ ለመሃይምናን ፡ እስከ ፤ ይእዜ ። ወለእመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንስጡራዊ ያን" ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኢየአምኑ ፡፣ በሃይማኖተ ፡ ንስጡርስ ፡ እኩይ0፣ ወንሕነሰ ፡ንቤ ፡እስመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተደምሮቶሙ ፡ምስለ ፡ይዕቆባዊያን ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ምስራቅ፤ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ እምክሮሙ ፡ እኩይ ። ወንሕነ ፡ንስአሎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ ይምርሐነ ፡ውስተ ፡ፍኖተ ፡ መድኃኒ
As for Nestures the impious, he did not accept this word and did not turn from his heresy. The whole council warned him that he would be removed from his priesthood and his office if he did not turn from his evil opinion. But he did not obey and did not accept the word of the whole council. After that, they (= the bishops) removed Nestures from his priesthood, excommunicated him, anathematized him, and drove him from his office (by sending him) to Upper Egypt. He died there a bad death and went into Sheol forever. As for these two hundred father bishops, they strengthened the faith, wrote and made an exposition in this council, and said: "Our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, gave birth to the Word of God who became incarnate." Then, they fixed the canon and the law, and these are in the hands of the believers even now. If one says that the Nestorians in these days do not believe in the faith of Nestures the evil, we say, we, that, because of their mixing with the Jacobites—the Syrians of the land of the East—there are some among them (who) have turned from their evil opinion. We ask, we, God to guide us in the way of salvation, for to him belongs the glory forever, amen. May the prayer and the blessing of this council be with us, amen.
ት፣፤ እስመ፣ ሎቱ ፣ ይደሉ ፤ ስብሐት ፣ ለዓለም ፤፡ አሜን ። 'ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፤ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፣ ፍልስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አፍላሆስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፤፡ ወለአብያጺሁ ፤ ሰማዕታት ፤ በሀገረ" ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን" ።
On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the translation of the body of the saint 'Aflähos (= Clement?) the martyr and of the martyrs his companions to the city of Alexandria. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለሉያራስ" ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወገላኒኮስ"" ፡ ሰማዕት?፤ ፡ ወተዝካረ ፡ ኢያቄም ፡ ወሐና ፡"ወያብዕሉ ፡ ባቲ” ፡ ሮም ፡ በዓሎ" ፡ ለእስጢፋኖስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወም ስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Luyäräs the martyr and (of) Galänikos the martyr and the commemoration of 'lyägëm (= Joachim) and of Hannä (= Anne), and one celebrates there (at) Rome the feast of 'Estifänos (= Stephen). May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'lyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 13TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፲፻ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ተአምር ፡ 'ዓቢይ ፡ ዘገብረ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወክ ቡር ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ፡ ዘቀጳዶቅያ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወሬዛ ፤ ዘፈተዋ ፡ ለወለተ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ወውእየ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍትወታ ። ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ዘመድነ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለ፳ከሓዲ ፡ ወመሠር ይ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ክርታሰ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ ወይቀውም” ፡ ኅበ ፡ 8እምኔሆሙ ፡ "በመንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወያነሥእ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መልዕልት ፡ ወበውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ይእቲ ፤ ክርታስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብር ፡ ኅሠር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፤ ወሖረ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ በማእከለ፤ ፡ መቃብረ፤! ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ክር ታስ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ውእቱ!" ፡ መሠርይ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽአ! ፡ ፳እምሰይጣናት ፡ 'ወአኃዘ ፣ እዴሁ፤ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡፤ ኅበ፤ ፡ ሊቆሙ ፡ ወውእቱ ሰ! ፣ ነሥኣ፤' ፡ እምኔሁ፤ ፡ ባለ]ይእቲ ፤ ክርታስ፤ ፡ ተስእሎ"የ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቦኑ፤ ፡ ተአምን" ፡ ብየ ፡ ኦወሬዛ ፡ ወትክሕዶ ፡፤ ለመሲሕከ ፤ ወኢትትመየጥ ፥፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ለከ ፡ ፈቃደከ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብር ፣ ምስኪን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እገብር ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዝከኒ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡፤ ሕጐላዊ፤ ፡ መስሐቲ ፡ ጸሐፍ ፡ ሊተ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ክርታስ ፡ በእዴከ" ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ዘንተ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ክርታስ ፡ ወከሐዶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡"ወአምነ ፡ በሰይጣን፥" ፡ ጸላኢነ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day there is the commemoration of the great miracle that the saint and illustrious Bäselyos (= Basil), bishop of the city of Qisäryä (= Caesarea) of Qapadoqyä (= Cappadocia), performed with an adolescent who desired the daughter of his master—his heart was inflamed with desire for her. This adolescent went to Satan, the enemy of our race, through the intermediary of an infidel and magician. This impious man wrote a paper for him, gave it to him, and ordered him to go near the tombs of the pagans and to stand near one of them at midnight and to raise his hand upward with the paper in his hand. When this vile servant had received these words from him, he went and stood in the middle of the tombs of the pagans, the paper in his hand as the magician had ordered (him). One of the demons then arrived, took his hand, and brought him to their chief. The latter took the paper from him, questioned him, and said to him: "Do you believe in me, young man? Will you deny your Messiah and not return to him after I have accomplished your will for you?" The poor servant answered him and said to him: "Yes, my Lord, I will do as you have ordered me." The devil, the pernicious (and) deceitful, said to him: "Write for me on a paper, with your hand, that you, you will do this." He wrote for him with his hand on a paper, denied Christ our Lord, and believed in Satan our enemy; and he separated himself from Christ in the flesh after having been one with him in heart and soul.


[p. 404] {#po.43.p404}
ወተፈልጠ ፡ እምነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ እምደኅረ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ምስሌሁ፤ ፡ በልብ ፡ ወነፍስ ። ወበውእቱ ፡፤ ጊዜ ፡ አንደደ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፡፤ ለወለተ ፤ እግዚኡ ፤ ፍትወተ ፤ ወአፍቀረቶ፥ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡፤ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኢተክህላ ፡ ትትዓገሥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ። አላ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትጸርኅ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡፤ ኢያስተዋሰብከኒ ፤ ለገብረ ፡ ዚአነ- ፡ እገሌ ፡ ወእመአኮ ፡ አነ ፣፤ እቀትል ፡ ነፍስየ ። ወሐዘነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወኮነ ፣፤ ያሥተዔግሳ ፡ ወኢተክህላ፥፤ ፡ ትትዓገሥ ፡ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፍቅራ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ገብር ፡
At that moment, Satan ignited the desire in the heart of the daughter of his (= the servant's) master; she loved the adolescent greatly and could not bear (to be away) from him for a single hour; what is more, she cried out in public to her father and said to him: "If you do not marry me to our servant So-and-so, I will kill myself." Her father was afflicted by this and wept bitter tears. He exhorted her to
ይትዌሰክ ፡ውስተ ፡ልባ ፡ወመውአቶ ፡ለአቡሃ ። "ወበእንተ ፡ፍርሃታ" ፡ከመ ፡ ኢትቅትል ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ወበእንተ፥ ፡ ኃፍረት ፤ እምሰብእ ፣ ወሀባቦ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ለው እቱ ፡ ወሬዛ፥፤ ፡ ገብሩ፤ ። ወተመጠዋ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብር ፡ እምአቡሃ ፡ ወአ ብኣ ፡ውስተ ፡ቤቱ ፡ወፈጸመ፥ ፡ ፍትወቶ ፡እምኔሃ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነበረት ፡ በኀቤሁቶጾ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ ይበዝጉ ፡ ብካየ ፡ ወላሐ ፡ ወገዓረ ፡ኀበ ፡እግዚኣብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ይሣሀል ፡ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ወያእትት ፡ ሐዘኖሙ ።
patience, but she could not contain herself; on the contrary, the love for this servant increased in her heart and overcame her father. Because of the fear (he felt for) her that she might kill herself, and because of the shame before the people, her father gave her to the adolescent, his servant. The servant took her from her father's house, brought her into his house, and satisfied his desire for her. After she had dwelt in his house for many days, her father and mother multiplied tears, lamentations, and groans before God so that he might have mercy on them and put an end to their sorrow.


[p. 406] {#po.43.p406}
ወሰምዓ ፡እግዚእነ ፡ክርስቶስ ፡ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ገዓሮሙ ። ወአለበዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ላቲ ፡-እስመ፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ዘአፍቀረቶ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ኢርእየቶ። ፡ ቦአ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አሐተ ፡፤ ፅለተ ፡በነዊኅ ፡ዝንቱ ፡ኩሉ ፡መዋዕል ፡ዘነበረ ፡ምስሌሃ ፡ ወኢነሥአ ፡ እምሥጢራት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፤ ወኢይትዓተብቶ፤ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በትእምርተ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ማኅ የዊ ። ወበከየት ፡ ወነስሐት ፡ ወሐዘነት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ላቲ ፡ ከሐዳ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኢገብርኩ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምእኩይ ። ወአውሠ|አ]ቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ክርስቲያ ናዌ ፤ ወዘትቤ፤ ፡ እሙን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ንሖር ፡ ነዓ" ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወንባእ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወንሣእ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡፣ በቅድሜየ ። ወሶበ ፡ አገበረቶ ፡ አይድዓ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፤ ወዘከመ ፡ ሖረ ፤ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ "ውእተ ፡ ክርታሰ። ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ከሐዶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ "ወዘከመ ፡ አምነ ፡ በሰይጣን ፡፤
Our Lord Christ heard their prayer and accepted their groans. He awakened the spirit of the daughter and revealed to her that the adolescent she loved was not a Christian, (that) she had not seen him enter a church a single day during all these many days that he had dwelt with her, and (that) he had not received the holy mysteries nor had he signed himself with the sign of the life-giving cross. She wept, repented, and was greatly afflicted. When the servant learned that his way of acting was revealed to her, he denied it and said to her: "I have done nothing wrong." She answered him and said to him: "If you are a Christian and (what) you say is true, come, go with me and let us enter the church together, and receive the holy mysteries before me." When she had (thus) constrained him, he made known to her everything he had done because of her and how he had gone to the magician, (how the latter) had written the paper for him, how he had denied Christ our Lord, and how he had believed in Satan, written for him on a paper with his hand, and given it to Satan.
ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ክርታስ?፤ ፡ በእዴሁ' ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሐዘነት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወገሠጸት ፡ ርእሳ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአት ፡ ወአፍጠነት ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ቤተ ፡ክርስቲያን ፡ ወብርሃና ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆቆስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወአጠየቀቶ ፡ "በዘ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። ወበከየት ፡ ወሰገደት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወሰአለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባልሓ ፡ ወያድኅና” ። ወፈነወ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፣ ወአብጽ ሖ፥፣ ኀቤሁ' ፡ ወተጠየቀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፤ ግብሮ ፡፤ ወከሠተ ፤ ሎቱ" ፡ ኩሎ"?፣ ዘገብረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተስእሎ ፡ቅዱስ?፤-" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡-ትፈቅድኑ ፡ከመጾቦ ፡ ትትመ የጥ ፡ ወትከውን፤ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፤ ዳግመ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይቤ ሎ ፡ ይከውነኒኑ ፡ ዝንቱ፤ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አጽንዕ ፡ ልበከ ፡ ወኢትፍራህ" ። ወአተቦ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወሞቅሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡፣ በኀቤሁ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ 'ጸሎተ ፡ ዘይጹሊ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፻ደመዓልት ። ወሖረ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፻መዓልት ፡ ሐወጾ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ፻መዓልት ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ ከመ ፡
When the daughter heard this statement from him, she was greatly afflicted and blamed herself. Then she rose and went in haste to the pillar of the Church and its light, the saint Bäselyos, the bishop of her city, and she informed him of what had happened to her. She wept, prostrated herself at his feet, and begged him to free her and save her. The saint Bäselyos sent for him, had him (= the servant) come to him, and inquired of him about his affair, and (the servant) revealed to him everything he had done. Then, the saint questioned him and said to him: "Do you want to convert and become a Christian again?" He answered him and said to him: "Can this be for me, my lord?" The saint said to him: "Comfort your heart and do not be afraid." And he made the sign of the cross over him, locked him in a place near him, and prescribed a prayer for him to say until the third day. The saint Bäselyos went away and prayed for him. After three days, he visited him and said to him: "What has happened during these three days?" He informed him that he (had been) in great
ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እምክልሖሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወያርእ ይዎ ፡'ለውእቱ ፣ መጽሐፍ፤ ፡ ወይትመዓዑ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮ ስ። ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር” ፡ የዐቅበከ” ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወማየ ፡ ወአግብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡መካኑ ። ወጸለየ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ካዕበ ፡ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐወጾ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ምንት ፡ ዘበጽሐከ ፡ ወይቤ” ፡ አንሰ ፡ እሰምዕ ፡ ክላሖሙ ፡ ወኢይሬእዮሙ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፤ ወአግብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ። ወሖረ ፡ "ቅዱስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወኮነ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ባስልዮስ፤ቦዐ ፡ ከመዝ፤0፤ ፡ በበደቦመዓልት፤" ፡ የሐውጾ ፡ እስከ ፤ ተፍ ጸሜተ ፡፤ ጓመዓልት ። "ወእምድኅረ ፤ ተፍጻሜተ ፤ ጓመዓልት! ፡ ሐወጾ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ባስልዮስ፤ ፡ ወሰአሎ!" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ፤ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ርኢኩከ፤"" ፣ በዛቲ ፤ ሌሊት፤" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትፀብእ ፡
torment because of the cries of the demons against him and (that) they had shown him the writing and were angry with him. The saint Bäselyos said to him: "Do not be afraid of them, for God is guarding you." He gave him bread and water and brought him back to his place. The saint prayed for him again, then visited him and said to him: "What has happened to you?" He said to him: "I have heard their cries (= those of the demons), but I have not seen them." He (= Bäselyos) gave him bread again and said to him: "Do not be afraid." And he brought him back to his place. The saint went away and prayed for him. The saint Bäselyos visited him thus every three days until the end of forty days. After forty days were completed, the saint Bäselyos visited him and questioned him about what had happened to him. The young man said to him: "My holy father, I saw you last night fighting Satan for me and defeating him then."

ለሰይጣን ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ወሞእኮ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ እምኔሁ ፤ ተፈሥሐ ፡፤ ፈድ ፋደ ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አድባራት፤ ፡ ወካህናት፤!" ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ኩልንታሃ ። "ወበሳኒታ ፡ አብጽሖ፤፤፤ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንሥኡ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወይጸልዩ፤፤ ፡ ወያስተብሞዑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ መሐረነ ። ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ "ከመዝ ፡ ይጸር፤፤! ፡ ወይቤ ሉ፤፤ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘጸሐፈ ፡ ውእቱ!!፤ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡፤ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወከሠቶ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ባስልዮስ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ወአንበቦ ፡ "ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝ ብ፤፤-፤፤7 ፣ ወባረኮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡'ወመጠ ዎሙ ፡ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ!፤! ፣ ወባረኮሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡፤ ቤቶሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይትሐሠዩ ፡ በአድኅኖቶሙ!! ፣ ወስርየተ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ። ወሰብሕ ዎ፤፡0 ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአእኩትዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ዘአድኀኖሙ ፡ እግዚኣ
When the holy Basil heard these words from him, he rejoiced greatly. He summoned all the monks of the convents and the priests, and they prayed for the adolescent that entire night. The next day, he (= Basil) brought the adolescent to the church, and the people of the city came. The holy Basil ordered everyone to lift their hands to heaven, to pray, to beseech God, and to say: "Lord, have mercy on us." They did as he had ordered them and cried out thus and said: "Lord, have mercy on us," until the document that the adolescent had written with his own hand for Satan fell, and it fell before all the people. The holy Basil opened the document, read it before the people, blessed the adolescent, and gave him the holy mysteries—he presented them (to him?) and to his wife—and blessed them together, and they went to their house rejoicing and exulting in their salvation and the forgiveness of their sin. They glorified God and praised the holy Basil, by whose prayer God had saved them from the seduction of Satan. May his intercession, his prayer, and his blessing be with us, amen.
ብሔር ፡ 'እምስሕተ[ተ ፡፤ ሰይጣን፤፡፤ ፡ በጸሎቱ ። 'ትንብልናሁ ፡ ጸሎቱ!" ፡ ወበ ረከቱ፤ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ፤" ፡ አባ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፤ ባሕታዊ ። ጸሎቱ፤፤ ፡ ወበረከቱ! ፡ ወትንብልናሁ፤ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲፪8ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ዘዓምድ ። ዝንቱ፤ ፡
On this day also died the holy Abba Isaac the Solitary. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us, amen. READING FOR THE 14TH OF MASKARAM. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Abba Agathon of the column
ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ትኒስ ፡ እምደቡበ" ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ጻድቃን ፡ፈራህያነ ፡እግዚኣብሔር ፡ወመፍቀርያነ ፡ምጽዋት ፡'ወለምሕረት ፡ለነዳያ ን፦ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሕሊና”" ፡ 'ምንኩስና ፡ ይትሀወክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ" ፡ በኩሉ ፣ ጊዜ ። ወኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ መጥራ ፡ 'ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ!" ፡ ማርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወጠወ፳ዓመት ፣ ተሠይመ! ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተጸምደ ፡ ወትረ' ፡ ለቤተ!" ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ። ወኮነ ፡ ይስእሎ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ! ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበመዓልት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጺሕ ፡ ሎቱ፤ ፡ 'ፀአተ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም፤ ፡ ወየሐውር!” ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይመንኩስ ። ወአመክነየ ፡"'ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእ ነ ፤ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምሀገሩ ፡ ወመጽአ'" ፡ ኅበ" ፡ ሀገረ ፣፡ መርዩት ፡ ወእምህየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፣ መልአከ ፡ እግዚኣብ ሔር ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወአንሶሰወ ፡ ምስሌሁ"! ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ኩላ" ፡ ገዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ዘአስቄጥስ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃ ም ፡ ወአባ ፡ ጋኦርጊ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ረድአ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ" ፡ ዞዓመተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አቆምዎ ፡ቅድመ ፡ቤተ ፡፣መቅደስ ፡ወቅድመ ፡አበ ፡ምኔት ፡አባ ፣ ዮሐንስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ቦመዓልተ"" ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጹልዩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወአልበስዎ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ መላእክትጾ ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ተፀምደ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወትረ- ፡ ወበጸሎት መዓልተ ፡ወሌሊተ ፡'ወሰኪብ ፡ዲበ" ፡ምድር ፡ዘእንበለ ፡መንጸፍ ፡እስከ ፡
(= the Stylite). This saint was from the city of Tenis (= Tanis) in northern Egypt. His parents were righteous people who feared God, loved almsgiving, and had pity on the poor. The thought of the monastic state stirred in his heart at all times. The name of his father was Matra and the name of his mother was Marya (= Mary). When he was thirty-five years old, he was ordained a priest and, from then on, always served the holy Church. He asked our Lord Christ, night and day, to facilitate his departure from this world, so that he (could) go into the desert and become a monk. Our Lord Christ found an opportunity for him, and he left his city, came to the city of Maryut (= Mareotis), and from there, to the desert of Asqetes (= Scetis). The angel of God appeared to him in the guise of a monk and walked with him throughout the desert until he had brought him to the convent of the holy Abba Macarius of Scetis. The holy Abba Agathon came to the holy elders Abba Abraham and Abba George, became a disciple to them, and remained with them for three years. After that, they made him stand before the sanctuary and before the superior, Abba John. Then they remained for three days praying over a monastic habit and clothed him (= Agathon) in the garb of the angels. From that hour, he served God and waged a great struggle in continual fasting, in prayer day and night, and by lying on the ground without a mat until his skin stuck to his bones, and he read continually the life of the holy Abba Simeon of the column and continually envied his life.


[p. 410] {#po.43.p410}
ለጽቀ ፡ ማእሱቶ፥ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓጽሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ወትረ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ ገድለ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ዘአምድ ፡ ወይቀንእ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገድሉ ። ወሐለየ፥ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሞቅሕ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወተማከረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በእንተዝ ፡፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምክር?" ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወወፅእ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰካ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በህየ" ። ወሐነጹ+፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸምድ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ መጠነ" ፡ ዛዓመት ።
He thought in his heart to enclose himself and took counsel with the holy fathers on this subject. They said to him: "This design is beautiful." He was blessed by them and they prayed over him; he left the desert, arrived near the world (= near an inhabited place) near Saka (= Sakha), and remained there in a church. The faithful built him a column; he climbed (it) and stood there, devoting himself to devotion and asceticism for fifty years.
ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ርኩስ ፣ ወያስሕ ቶሙቶ፤ ፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፤ ወየአውድዎ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፣ ይሰምዑ ፤ ትምህርቶ ፣ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ አዕ ፁቃተ ፡ ዕፀው ። ወፈነወ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ወአብጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወጸለየ ላዕሌሁ ፡ 'ወአውጽአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሰይጣነ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ይትናገረኒ ፡ ወኃ ደገቶሙ፤ ፣ ለሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወከረዩ ፡፣ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ማይ ፡ በስሙ ፡ 'ለአባ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣፤ ከመ ፣ ይትፈወሱ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትሐፀቡ ፡ እምኔሃ" ፡
In his time appeared a man in whom (was) an unclean demon (who) deceived many people. This (man) stayed in a church, and the people who listened to his teaching surrounded him; (they had) with them branches of trees. The holy Abba Agathon sent (someone), had (the man) brought to him, prayed over him, and cast out of him the demon that was speaking in him to the people. Likewise, a woman said: "Menas the martyr is conversing with me," and she abandoned the people of her country. A well of water was dug in the name of Abba Menas the martyr so that those who washed in it would be healed of their


[p. 412] {#po.43.p412}
እምደዌሆሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ፥ ፡'አውፅኦ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡[ለ]ውእቱ ፡መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይድፍንዋ፤፤ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡፤ ዓዘቅት ። "ወካልእ ፡ ብእሲ" ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ያነሥኦሙ ፡ ለአብዳን ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ጋኔነ ፡ ወይዘብቶሙ ፡ ወየኀድግዎሙ" ፡ ጋኔን ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፤ ብዙኃን ፡እምነ ፡እለ ፡ቦሙ ፡ጋኔን ። ወፈነወ ፡-ኀቤሁ ፡ቀዱስ ፡አባ ፡አጋቶን ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢመጽአ ፡ ወኢ ኃደገ ፡ ስሕተቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃለፈ" ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ሀገር ፡ረገምዎ ፣-እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፤ ቦሙ ፡ ጋኔነ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወካልእ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ በዝሙት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወማሰነ ፡ መንፈቁ ። ወጾርዎ ፣ ወአውጽእዎ' ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ወጸለየ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወፈወሶ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ከመ ፡
ailments. The holy Abba Agathon prayed over this woman until he cast out of her that unclean spirit, and the saint ordered the people of that city to fill in the well. Another man also took the madmen who had demons, struck them, and the demons left them for a short time. Many of those who had demons gathered to him. The holy Abba Agathon sent to him many times so that he would come to him, but he did not obey him, did not come, and did not abandon his error. When the governor of the city passed by, those who had demons insulted him. Because of this, the governor took this man, subjected him to great torture, and he died then in the torture. Another man, a priest, fell into fornication with a woman inside the church, and half of him rotted. He was carried and brought out to the holy Abba Agathon; the latter prayed over him and God healed him. The saint ordered this priest M to abstain from fulfilling the functions of the priesthood for all the days of his life (that remained to him).

This saint performed many miracles and healed many sick people. Demons appeared to him in the guise of angels singing a beautiful song and declared him blessed. He recognized their ruse by the power of Christ, made the sign of the cross over them, and they fled from before him quickly. When God wanted to make him rest from the fatigue of this world, he (= Abba Agathon) was struck by a small illness and surrendered his soul into the hand of God. Many people gathered to him who had come to him, to whom he had taught the way of God and who had been healed of their ailments by his prayer. When they found him dead, they shed bitter tears over him, for they (no longer) had their father who consoled them and they had become orphans of this excellent father. All the days of the life of this holy Abba Agathon were one hundred years. He remained in the century forty years, in the desert ten years, and in retreat and on a column fifty years. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለመቃረ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ወበርተሎሜዎስ ፤ ወአውድራ ፡ ወናሶን ፡ ወዕረፍቱ ፡ ለዴገና ፣፤ ቀሲስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡፣ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Macarius the martyr, (of) Bartholomew, (of) Eudore(?), and (of) Nason, and the death of Degana the priest. May God, by their prayer, have mercy on our king Iyasu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the ages of ages.]

### READING FOR THE 15TH OF MASKARAM

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day took place the translation of the body of the holy Stephen, chief of the deacons and first of the martyrs. How did his translation take place? It (took) place when many years had passed since his death, exceeding three hundred years, after Constantine the righteous had reigned and the right faith and good worship had been revealed; there was a man in whose enclosure the body of the holy Stephen was buried—which was called "enclosure of Gamaliel"—near Jerusalem. The name of this man (was) Lucian. The athlete (of God), the holy Stephen, appeared to him in his sleep many times, said to him: "I am Stephen," and indicated to him the place where his body was buried. This man, Lucian, came and told it to the bishop of the city of Jerusalem.

When the bishop heard this matter, he rejoiced greatly, then arose, took with him two bishops and church people, and they arrived at the place where the body of the saint was. They dug in that place, there was a great earthquake, and the coffin in which (was) the body of the saint was discovered. A perfume of very pleasant odor exhaled from it, and the voices of angels were heard giving praises and saying: "Glory to God in the heavens and peace on earth! (May) his good pleasure (be) for men!" They gave praises and sang thus ten times. The bishops and priests prostrated themselves before the coffin of the body of the saint. Then they carried it with hymns, chants, and many lamps until they had brought it into the chamber of Zion.
ወበማኅተው፤ ፡ ብዙኃት፤" ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ጽዮን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አለስክንድሮስ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይነብር ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሐነጸ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ቤተ ፡ክርስቲያነ ፡ሠናይተ ፡ወአፍለሱ ፣፡ሥጋሁ፤ ፡ለቅዱስ ፡ወአንበ ሩ! ፡ ውስቴታ" ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፤ በ፲ዓመት"፤፤ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አለስክንድሮ![ስ ፤ ወቀበረቶ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ለሣፁን ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ወእምድ ኅረ[ዝ ፤ በ፳ዓመት" ፣ ካልእ" ፡ ሐለየት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡፤ ለአለስክንድሮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ቄስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወትውሰድ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለምዎታ ፡፤ ምስሌሃ ። ወበጽሐት ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘቀበረቶ” ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፣ በውስተ?" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ምታ ፡ አለስክ ንድሮስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሣፁነ ፡ ምታ፥፤ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ሣፁነጎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡
After that, there was a man, whose name (was) Alexander, of the people of the city of Constantinople—he lived in Jerusalem. He built for the holy Stephen a beautiful church, and the body of the saint was transported and placed there. After that, in the eighth year, Alexander died, and his wife buried him next to the coffin of the holy Stephen. After that, after eight more years, the wife of Alexander thought of going to the city of Constantinople and taking the body of her husband with her. She arrived at the place where she had buried him, next to the holy Stephen in the church that her husband Alexander had built for him. The coffin of her husband had the appearance of the coffin of the body of the holy Stephen, and, by the will of God, she took the coffin in which (was) the body of the
እስጢፋኖስ ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ሣፁነ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ጾረቶ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አስቅላን ፡ ወእምህ የ ፡ ተጽዕነት ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቂስጥንጥንያ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኮነት" ፡ ትሰምዕ ፡ እምሣፁን ፡ ስባሔ ፡ ወዝማሬ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአንከረት ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወተንሥአ[ት ፤ ከመ ፡ ትርአዮ ፤ ለው እቱ ፤ ሣፁን ፡ 'ወጠየቀት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱቶ ፣ ሣፁን ፡ ዘውስቴቱ" ፡ ሥጋሁ ፤ ለቅዱስ ፡-እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወአእመረት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ኮነ፥፤ ፡"በፈቃዱ ፣ ለእግዚኣብ ሔር= ፡ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወኢተክህላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመየጥ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወአእ
holy Stephen. Then she carried it to the city of Ascalon and, from there, she boarded a boat to go to the city of Constantinople. While they were advancing on the sea, she heard from the coffin many praises and chants, and she was very astonished. She got up to go see the coffin, realized that it (was) the coffin in which (was) the body of the holy Stephen, and recognized that this was happening by the will of God—glory to him! She could not return to Jerusalem, and she praised God for what he had done for her.

ኩተቶ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'ዘገብረ ፡ ላቲ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ኀበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ቀኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ ሖረት ፡ይእቲ ፡ብእሲት ፡ኅበ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአይድዓቶ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መርሶ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀሩስጥንጥንያ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ 'ወብዙኃን ፡፣ ካህናት ፡ 'ወሕዝበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ ዲበ ፣ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወበሐዔት ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አብጽሕ ዎ፤፣ ኀበ፣ ጽርሐ ፡ መንግሥት ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፤ እምኔሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወበውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወእምው ስቴቶሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጾሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ፪አብ ቅል ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ቄስጠንጥንዮስ ፡ 'ወውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንብሩ ፡ 'ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስቴቱ"" ፣ ወነድኡ ፡ አብቅለ ፡ ወኢያንሶሱ ፡ አላ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ በህየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዘበጥዎሙ ፡ ለአብቅልት ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እም፳እምኔሆሙ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዝየ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያን ብሩ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ወአንከሩ ፡፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዑ፤! ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ወአእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘአንበባ ፡ ለአድገ ፡ በለ ዓም ፡ 'ርኩስ ፡ ውእቱ። ፡ ዘአንበበ ፤ አብቅልተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትሐነጽ“ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወሐነጹ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡፤ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ሠናይተ ። ወአንበሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አንቄ ፤ ባሕርየ" ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ወንጹሐ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅ
When they arrived in the city of Constantinople, this woman went to the emperor and made known to him the story of the holy Stephen, how miracles had occurred from him, and how he had arrived in the port of the city of Constantinople. The emperor, the patriarch, many priests, and the people of the city went out and arrived at the body of the saint. They carried it on their heads with many chants and canticles in joy and exultation until they had brought it into the imperial palace. God revealed through him, in the boat and in the palace of the emperor, many miracles; among them (this one): when the body of the saint was transported on a chariot pulled by two mules and they arrived at the place that is called Constantinus—it (was) the place where the saint wanted his body to be deposited—they urged the mules, but they did not walk; on the contrary, they stood still there. When the mules were struck, a voice was heard (coming) from one of them saying: "(It is) here that it is fitting to deposit the body of the holy Stephen." All those who heard this were astonished, praised God, and recognized that he who had made the impure ass of Balaam speak was he who was making the mules that were transporting the body of the holy Stephen speak. The emperor ordered that a church be built for him, and a beautiful church was built for him. The precious and pure pearl was deposited there, that is to say, the body of the holy Stephen the apostle, chief of the deacons and first of the martyrs. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us, amen.
ዱስ' ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ሐዋርያ?" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ዲያቆናት ፡ ወቀዳሜ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፣ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ?" ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ 'ለአብ ፡ ጸድቅ”" ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ጠራው ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ኮነ ፣ ብፅአተ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ልብ ፡ ተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ወትረ ። ወኢኮነ ፣ ይበልዕ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ 'እመባልዕተ ፡ ሰብእ" ፡ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡
On this day also (there is) the commemoration of the righteous father Abba Peter who (was) from the city of Taraw. This saint was dedicated to God from his mother's womb. When he had grown up, he was perfect of heart, waged a great (ascetic) struggle, and served God in fasting and prayer day and night, continually. He ate nothing of the food of men, but he


[p. 418] {#po.43.p418}
በሣዕር?" ፡ ንስቲት ፡ 'ረከባ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወተመሰለ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በገድሎሙ”" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጢአ ፡ ሲሳይ ። እስመ ፡ ሲሳዩሰ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ "ዝክረ ፡ ስሙ5፤ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ 'እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ክርስቶስ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበመዓ ልት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ጽርዓት ። ወደለዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ በእገሪሁ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ አሎን ፡ ወኢርሕሱ" ፡ እገሪሁ ። እስመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይቡስ ፡፣ ፈድፋደ ፤ እም[ብ]ዝኃ ፣ ምንዳቤ ፤ በክበደ ፡ ሐጺን ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይለብስ ፡ ዲበ ሥጋሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡፤ ሕይወቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምትሐት ፡ ወአኮ ፥ ሰብእ ። ወአሥመሮ‹ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር፤ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ ኀቤ
nourished himself with a little grass (that) he found in the desert. He resembled the angels in their life because of the absence of food: indeed, his food was the mention of the name of God, our Lord Jesus Christ, night and day, without ceasing. He was worthy to walk with his feet on the river that is called Alon, and his feet were not wet; indeed, his body was very withered by the great torment (caused) by the weight of the iron that he carried on his body all the days of his life, so much so that he became like a ghost and not (like) a man. He pleased God by this struggle and went to him. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 16TH OF MASKARAM
ሁ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ በዓለ ፡ 'ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ፤ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወመቃብረ ፡ እግዚእ ነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወኩሎን ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት፤ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡'እስመ ፡እሌኒ ፡ንግሥት ፡ቅድስት ፡ሶበ ፡ኮነ ፡አመ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ አስተጋብአት ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱ ሳን ፡ ፻፻፲ወቿ፤"በሀገረ ፤ ኒቅያ ። ወነሥአት ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ብዙኃ፤፤ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለወልዳ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኮንኩ፤" ፡ አብፃዕኩ! ፡ ለእግዚኣብ ሔር፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ኅበ፤ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቅድስት ፡ 'ወአኅኀሥሥ ፡ ወእከሥት፤ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ መስቀል ፤ ማሕየዌ ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ሳራዊተ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአልባሳተ ፡ ዲባ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day there is the feast of the consecration of the temple and the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ in the city of Jerusalem the holy, and (of) all the holy Places. This (took place) because Helena, the holy queen, when they were in the twentieth year of the reign of Constantine her son, gathered the council of the three hundred and eighteen saints in the city of Nicaea. She took much money and said to her son Constantine: "My son, as for me, I have made a vow to God to go to the city of Jerusalem the holy; I will search (there) and discover the wood of the life-giving Cross." Constantine rejoiced at this proposal, sent soldiers with her, and gave her much money and precious garments inlaid with gold.
ጋት ፡ክቡራት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተባረከት፤ ፡ እምነ ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐተተት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ መስቀል፤ ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ወበዓቢይ ፡ ጻማ ፡፤ ወበምንዳቤ'"" ፡ ረከበቶ" ። ወሰብሐቶ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ስብሐተ፤፤ ፡ ወአክበረቶ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ክብረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጹ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅ ደስ ፡ወጎልጎታ ፡ወቤተ ፡ልሔም ፡ወበዓት ፡ዘተወልደ ፡ውስቴታ ፡እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወጽርሐ ፡ ጽዮን፤ ፡ ወጌተሰማኒ ፡ ዘተቀብረ ፣ ሥጋሃ ፣ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ድንግል" ፡ ወደብረ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወኩሎን ፡ መካ ናት ፡ቅዱሳት ። ወአዘዘት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያሰርግውዎሙ ፡በዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ወወርቅጾ ፡ ወበብሩር ። ወሀሎ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ወአምከራ ፡
When she had arrived at the city of Jerusalem, she received the blessing of the holy Places. Then she set out in search of the wood of the life-giving Cross and, with great pain and difficulty, she found it. She glorified it with great glory and rendered it great honors. Then, she ordered the construction of an altar (for) the sanctuary and (the churches? of) Golgotha, (of) Bethlehem, (of) the cave where our Lord Christ was born—glory to him!—(of) the chamber of Zion, of Gethsemane (where) the body of our Lady, the holy Virgin, was buried, (of) the Mount of Olives, and (of) all the holy Places. And she ordered them to be adorned with precious stones, gold, and silver. There was in Jerusalem a bishop whose name (was) Abba Macarius; he


[p. 420] {#po.43.p420}
ለእሌኒ ፤ ንግሥት ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ 'ኢትግበሪ ፡ ዘንተ" ፣ ከመዝ፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ 'እምድኅ ረ ፡ ሕዳጥ" ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወይነግሠ ፡ 'ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይፄውውዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፣፤ ወያንሕልዎ ፡ 'ወይነሥኡ ፡ ዘትገብሪ ፤ እምወርቅ ፡ወእ[ም]ብሩር ፡ አላ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ከመ ፡ትሕንጽዮ ፡ሕንጸተ ፡ጽኑዓ ፡ "ዘኢያንቀለቅል ፡ ወዘኢይትነሠት-' ፡- ወዘተረፈ ፡- እምዝንቱ ፤ ንዋይ ፡ ተሀብዮ፤ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወተወክፈት ፤ ቃሎ ፣፣ ወመጠወት ፡ ሎቱ፥ቦ ፣ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፤ ወአዘዘቶሙ፤ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ መኳንንገ[ት ፡ እለ ፡ የሐንጹ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡
advised Helena the queen and said to her: "Do not do it this way, for in a few days nations will come and reign over this country; they will plunder this place, destroy it, and carry away what you will have made in gold and silver. But it is fitting that you build it (= the set of churches) with a solid construction that does not waver and cannot be overturned. (As for) what remains of this money, give it to the poor and the needy." She accepted his word, gave him much money, and ordered all the chiefs who were building to obey the holy Abba Macarius, the bishop, in everything he would tell them to do for the construction.
በኩሉ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ ለገቢረ፤ ፡ ሕንጸት ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጠት ፡ እሌኒ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘገብረት ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ብዙኃ ፣ወመኳንንተ ፡ሥዮማነ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ሕንጸት ። ወአዘዘ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብዎሙ ፡ ለነደቅት ፡ ዓስቦሙ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ4+" ፡ እለ ፡፤ ይትለአኩ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ሕንጸት ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕፀት ። እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈርሃ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይግዓሩ ፣ ወኢይሕዝኑ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢይሁብዎሙ+ ፡ ዓስ ቦሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይትመዓዕ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ገዓሮሙ ።
When Queen Helena returned to her son, King Constantine, and (when) she told him what she had done in Jerusalem, he rejoiced greatly. He sent much other money and chiefs in charge of the construction work. The king ordered the architects and all those who would serve to do the construction to be given their wages at the time of evening each day without reduction: the king feared indeed that they would groan and grieve if they were not given their wages, so that God would be angered against him because of their groans.
ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሕንጸቶሙ ፡ ለመካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ አመ ፡ ጠዓመተ፤ ፡መንግ ሥቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡፣ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ፈነወ። ፡ ንዋየ ፣፡ ቅድሳት ፣ ወአልባሳ ተ ፡ዲባጋት ፡ ክቡራት ፡"ዘብዙኅ ፣ሜጫጦሙ5 ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኀበ ፡ሊቀ ፡ጸጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ ቀቱስጥንጥንያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፤ ኤኢስ ፣፤ ቆኦሳተ ፣፤ ዚአሁ ። ወኀበ ፡ አትናትዮስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆጾሳተ ፡ ዘብሔሩ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወአ ዘዞሙ፤ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ 'ምስለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይቀድሱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፤ 'ወኩሎሙ ፡ መሥዋዕተ' ፡ ወመካና ት ፡ እለ ፡ ተሐንጻ ። ወተጋብኡ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ውስተ፤ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወነ በሩ ፡፤ በህየ= ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪'ለወርኃ ፡ መስከረም ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወቀደሱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ 'ወኩሎሙ ፡ መሥ[ዋ]ዕተ ፡ ወመካናተ ፡ እለ ፡ ተሐንጹ ፡ ወኮነ ፡፤ 'ዓቢይ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወሠርዑ ፡ 'ወቀደሱ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፤ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወአ ስተርአየ ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ዓቢይ ፣፤ ወተኣምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ። አመ ፡ ፲ወኒ 'ለመስከረም ፡ ኦዱ ፡ በመስቀል ፡ ክቡር" ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሙንቱ"" ፡ መካ ናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወሰገዱ?፤ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ' ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወነሥኡ ፣፤ ምሥ ጢራተ ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወአክበርዎ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወወደስዎ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ፡ በሰላም ። 'ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ወትንብልናሆሙ ፡ የሀ ሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን” ።
When the construction of the holy Places was completed, in the thirtieth year of the righteous King Constantine, (the latter) sent holy vessels and precious garments inlaid with gold whose price (was) great. He commanded the patriarch of the city of Constantinople to take his bishops, Athanasius, the patriarch of the city of Alexandria, to take the bishops of his country, and likewise, the patriarch of the city of Antioch. He ordered them all to gather with the patriarch of the city of Jerusalem and to consecrate the sanctuary and all the altars and places that had been built. All gathered in the city of Jerusalem and remained there on the seventeenth of the month of Maskaram like this day (= the sixteenth). They consecrated the sanctuary and all the altars and places that had been built. There was a great joy on this day such as there had never been the like. They founded and consecrated the consecration and received the holy mysteries; a great light and many miracles appeared on this day. On the seventeenth of Maskaram, they turned with the precious Cross in these holy Places. They worshipped God there, received the holy mysteries, glorified the glorious Cross, and sang praises to it, and they went to their countries in peace. May their prayer, their blessing, and their intercession be with us, amen.


[p. 422] {#po.43.p422}
ወበዛቲ ፡፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ገባኤል ፡ ዘእምነ ፡ አሳ ሔል ፤እምነገደ ፡ንፍታሌም ፡ዘተፄወወ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ አኔሜሴር?"” ፡ንጉሠ ፡ፋርስ ፡ ዘእምነ ፡ ታቤስ ፡ እንተ ፡ እምየማና ፡ ለቃዴስ” ፡ እንተ ፡ ንፍታሌም ፡ በገሊላ ፡ እንተ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አሴር ፤ ወውእቱ" ፡ ጦቢት ፡ ሖረ ፡ ፍኖተ፤ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወርትዕ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። ወምጽዋተኒ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ገብረ ፡ ለአኃዊሁ ፡ ወለሕዝቡ ፡ እለ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ፋርስ ፡፤ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ክህዱ ፡ፇዑ ፡ ለበዓል ፤ ወለደማሊ ። ወውእቱ ፡ የሐውር ፡ መብዝኅቶ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይወስድ ፤ ፀምረ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ወዓሥራተ ፡ እክሉ ፡ ወሣልስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ለነዳይ ። ወአመ ፡ ፄወወ" ፡ ነነዌ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፡ በልዑ ፡ እምእክለ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡፣ ወውእቱሰ ፡የዓቅብ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ከመ ፡ ኢይብልዕ ፡እስመ ፡ ይዜከሮ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ።
[On this day also died Tobit, the son of Gabael who (descends) from Asiel, of the tribe of Naphtali who was deported in the days of Enemessar, the king of Assyria; (this is the Tobit) who (is) from Thisbe which (is) to the right of Qadesh of Naphtali in Galilee above Asher. This Tobit followed the path of justice and righteousness all the days of his life. He gave much alms to his brothers and to his people who had gone to the land of Assyria; all the people, who had denied (God), sacrificed to Baal and to the calf. As for him (= Tobit), he often went to Jerusalem, he brought the wool of his sheep, the tenth of his wheat, and gave the third of his share to the poor. When Nineveh took all his brothers into deportation, (the latter) ate the food of the pagans, but he kept himself from eating (it), for he remembered God with all his heart.
ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ረከበ ፤ በድነ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ምሥያጥ ፤ ውእቱሂ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ይጥዓም ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ቤት ፡ እስከ ፡ የዓርብ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወሶበ ፣ ዓርበ ፤ ፀሐይ ፡ ከረየ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ወቀበሮ ፡ ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ስዑበ ፣ ኀበ ፡ አረፍተ ፡፤ ዓፀድ ። ወኢያእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አረፍት ፣ ወእንዘ ፡ ክሠት ፡ ገጹ ፡ አኩስሔ፤- ፡ ውስተ ፤ አዕይንቲሁ" ፡ ውዑየ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘበቀ'ዖ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተዘከረኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ወነጽር" ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምስኪነትየ ፤ ወኢትዝክር ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ ወኃጢአተ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ዘረሰይከኒ ፡ ለተፄ ውዎ ፡ ወለተበርብሮ ። ወይአዜኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይኤድመከ ፡ ግበር ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ በቅ ድሜከ፤ ፣ ወአዝዝ ፡ 'መንፈስየ ፡ ዘይትሜጦ” ፡ ከመ ፣ እሠዓር”፤ ፣ ወእኩን ፡
One day, he found a corpse in the market. He got up without eating and carried it into a house until the sun set. When the sun had set, he dug the earth and buried it (= the corpse); that night, he spent the night, defiled, against the wall of the house. He did not know that there were birds in the wall and, while his face was uncovered, they deposited their hot dung in his eyes; a smoke came out into his eyes and no one (could) help him. Tobit prayed to God saying: "Remember me, Lord, look at my miserable state and do not remember my sin and the sin of my fathers, you who delivered me to deportation and plunder. Now, do with me according to what will please you before you and order that my spirit be rendered so that I disappear and become dust, for it is better for me to die than to live."
መሬተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቴይሰኒ ፣ መዊት ፡ 'እምሐይው ። ወበይእቲ ፡ፅለት ፡ ረከባ ፡ ለወለተ ፡ ራጉኤል ፡ ሣራ ፡ ጋኔን ፡እኩይ ፡ አስማድ ስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተዋሰብዋ ፡ ለዕደው ፡ ወሶበ” ፡ ይበውኡ ፡፣ ኀቤሃ ፡ ይቀትሎ ሙ ፣ እስከ ፡ ተዓየራሃ ፡ አፅማተ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ወትጹሊ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ከመ ፡ ያድኅና ፣ እምትዕይርቶን" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ጸሎታ ፡ ወጸሎተ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ለሩፋኤል ፡ ወተዘከረ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብሩር ፡ ዘአማኀፀኖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገባኤል ። ወጸውዖ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ጦብያ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፤ ኅሥሥ ፡ ለከ ፡ አሳበ ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገባኤል ፡ ከመ"፤ ትትመጠው፡፤ 'ብሩረ ፡ ዘአዕቀብክዎ5 ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ እሙት ። ወረከቦ፤‹ ፣ ለሩፋኤል ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አሳብ ፡ ወሖረ፤፤ ፡ ወሠመየ ፡ ስሞ ፡ አዛርያ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ በጽሑ፤" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጤግሮስ ፡ ወወረደ፤ ፡ ይትሐፀብ ፡ ወሰረረ ፡ 'ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዓሣ!" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሩፋኤል ፡ ለጦ
That day, the evil demon Asmodeus met the daughter of Raguel, Sarah; for (her father) had married her to seven men and, when they entered after her, he (= Asmodeus) killed them so much so that the servants of her father insulted her (= Sarah). She remained weeping and praying to God that he would deliver her from their outrages. Then her prayer and the prayer of Tobit were heard before the glory of the greatness of Raphael, and Tobit remembered the money that he had deposited with Gabael. He called his son Tobias and said to him: "Look for a hireling who will go with you to Gabael so that you may receive (from him) before I die the money that I entrusted to him." He (= Tobias) met Raphael under the appearance of a hired man. The latter went (with Tobias who) called him by the name of Azarias. While he was walking on the road, they arrived at the Tigris. (Tobias) went down to wash and a fish


[p. 424] {#po.43.p424}
ብያ ፤ አኃዝ ፡ ወንሣእ ፡ ልቦ ፡ ወከብጾ ፡ ወሐሞቶ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ራጉኤል ፡ ተቀበለቶሙ ፡ ሣራ ፡ ወፈተዋ ፡ ጦብያ ፡ ወተናገሮ፤፤ ፡ ለአቡሃ ፡ከመ ፡የሀቦ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አስተዋሰብክዋ!" ፡ ለ፲ዕ ደው ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ወይቤሎ፤" ፣ ጦብያ፤" ፡ ይኩን ፡ ፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ። ወሶበ ፤ አብእዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ተዘከረ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለሩፋኤል ፡ መልአክ፤" ፡ ወአጤሰ ፡ ልቦ ፣፤ ወሐሞቶ ፡ ለዓሣ ። ወጉጐየ ፡ ጋኔን ፡ እኩይ፤!" ፡ እምኔሃ ፣ ወተመይጠ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አቡሁ፤!፤ ።
rushed at him; Raphael said to Tobias: "Seize (it) and take its heart, its liver, and its gall." And (Tobias) did as he had prescribed to him. When they arrived at the house of Raguel, Sarah welcomed them; Tobias desired her and spoke to her father so that he would give her to him. The latter said to him: "I have married her to seven men and all are dead." Tobias said to him: "May the will of God be done." When they were introduced into the bridal chamber, (Tobias) remembered the word of the angel Raphael and made the heart and the gall of the fish go up in smoke. The evil demon fled from her (= Sarah) and (Tobias) returned with her to the house of his father.
ወሶበ፤፤። ፡ ተቀበሎ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወኩሐሎ!፤፤ ፡ በሐሞቱ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዓሰየ ፡ አዕይን ቲሁ ፤ ተሥዕዓ ፡ እምነ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡፣ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ!! ። ወይቤ ፡ ብፁዓን ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተከዙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ ሕማምከ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ብከ ፡ ርእዮሙ፣ ኩሎሙ፤፤ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወካዕበ ፤ ይቤ ፡ ትትሐነጽ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ በሰንፔር ፣ ወበመረግድ ፡ ወበዕንም ፡ ክቡር ፡ አረፋቲሃ ፡ወማኅፈዲሃ ፡ ወአናቅጺሃ ፡ ወርቅ፤፤ ፡ንጹሕ ፡ወመርኅባኒ ፡ ቢረሌ፤!' ፡ ወበዕንቄ፤፤አትራኮስ ፤ ወዕንቄ፤፤ ፡ ሶፎር ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ፍና ዊሃ ፡ ሃሌሉያ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይትመየጡ ፡ ፄዋ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም" ፡ እምባቢሎን ፡ ወየሐንጽዋ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በንድቅ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ለጦብያ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፃፅዕ ፡ እምነ ፡ ነነዌ ፡ አእምሮ፤፡፤ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ምዝባሬሆሙ"፡፡ ፡ ዘነበበ ፡፣ ዮናስ ፤ ነቢይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ርኢ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምጽዋት ፡ ያድኅን፤፡ ፡ ወታጸድቅ ። ምስለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወፅአት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓራቱ ፤ በ፻ኾ፪ዛወቿ፳ዓመታቲሁ ፡፤ ሎቱ ፡፤ ወቀበርዎ ፡ በክብር ። እግዚአብሔር ፡
When his father welcomed him, he anointed him with his gall (= that of the fish); when he had rubbed his eyes, it dissipated from his eyes like smoke and he saw his son. He rejoiced, prophesied about the sufferings of our Lord Jesus Christ, and said: "Happy are all those who are afflicted because of your sufferings, for they will rejoice in you; they will all see your glory." He also said: "The heavenly Jerusalem will be built of sapphire, emerald, and precious stones; its walls, its towers, and its gates (will be) of pure gold; its squares will be made (of) beryl, carbuncle, and sapphire, and it will be said in all its streets: Alleluia!" He also prophesied that the captives of Jerusalem would return from Babylon and would build Jerusalem with a precious construction. He ordered Tobias, his son, to leave Nineveh, knowing that its destruction would occur, of which the prophet Jonah had spoken. He said to his son: "See, my son, that almsgiving saves and makes righteous." Upon this word, his soul departed (while he was) in his bed, in his one hundred and fifty-eighth year (?), and he was buried with honor. May God have mercy on our king Iyasu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus, in the ages of ages.]
ይምሐሮ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለወርቅላ ፡ ወስምዖን ፡ ወመርቅያኖስ ፡ ወዖዝያኖስ፤፤ ፡ ወሮኒኮስ ፡ ወሉክያኖስ ፡ አዝርማኖስ ፡ ወጴጥሮስ ፡ ወሐና ፡"ወዕረፍ ቱ ፡ለአባ፤ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ዘተፍእመ ፡እብነ ፡"ውስተ ፡አፉሁ፤፤ ፡ከመ ፡ ኢይትናገ ር ፡ ነገረ ፡ ጽሩዓ!" ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Wargela, Simon, Marcian, Ozianus, Ronikos, Lucian, Azremanos, Peter, and Anne, and the death of Abba Agathon who filled his mouth with a stone so as not to utter vain words. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.]


[p. 426] {#po.43.p426}

### READING FOR THE 17TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ "ኮነ ፤ ተዝካረ፤ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ዘለእግዚእነ" ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ዘከሠተቶ ፡'ቅድስት ፡ እሌኒ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ መፍቀሪ ተ ፤ አምላክ ፡ እሙ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኩስተረት" ፡ ወግረ ፡ ጎልጎታ ፡ ወረከበቶ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ። ወለምንት ፡ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወግር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ታኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡፤ ብዙኃት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያስተርእዩ ፡ እምነ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ዘለእግዚእነ'" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤፤ ። "ምውታን ፡ ይትነሥኡ!" ፡ ወመፃኾጐ ዓን፤፤ ፡ ይትፌወሱ! ፡ ወድውያን ፡የሐይዉ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይትመዓዑ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወኦዱ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ?" ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይኩ ሰትር ፤ ቤቶ ፡ አው ፡ መሬተ ፡ አው ፡ ጐድፈ ፡ ይወግሩ፤ ፡ 'ዲበ ፡ መቃብሩ"" ፡ ለኢየሱስ! ፡ ናዝራዊ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዘይፈደፍድ ፡ እም፪፻ዓመት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ግብረ" ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ መጽአት ፡ እሌኒ ፡ ንግ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day there is the commemoration of the feast of the glorious Cross of our Lord Jesus Christ. It is it that the holy Helena, the queen loving God, mother of Constantine the righteous king, discovered; when she (had) the hill of Golgotha cleaned, she found the glorious Cross. Why was this hill great? Because many miracles and wonders had appeared from the holy tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him! The dead were resurrected, the paralytics were healed, and the sick regained their health. Because of this, the Jews were angered and traveled throughout the country of Judea and Jerusalem so that whoever cleaned his house—(whether it was) dust or filth—would throw (it) on the tomb of Jesus the Nazarene. They did this thus for more than two hundred years and (it) became a great work until the moment when Queen Helena arrived.
ሥት ። ወአኀዘቶሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ወሞቅሐቶሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ አጠየቅዋ ፡ መካነ ፡ መቃብሪ ሁ ፤ ለእግዚእነ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአገበረቶሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኩስተሩ ፡ ውእተ ፣ ወግረ ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተሐንጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ወቀደስዋ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ፤ ፡ በዓለ፣ ዓቢየ ፣0፣ አመ፣ ፲ወጸለመስከረም ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዓቢያ ፤ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ በከመ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ትንሣኤ[ሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡
(The latter) seized the Jews and imprisoned them until they indicated to her the place of the tomb of our Lord—glory to him! She did violence to them so much so that they cleaned this hill; the holy Cross was discovered and a beautiful church was built for it. It (= the church) was consecrated and a great feast was held there on the seventeenth of Maskaram—on this day. All the people of the Christians came from all their countries to Jerusalem and held a great feast for the glorious Cross like the feast of the resurrection of our Lord Jesus Christ.
ክርስቶስ ። ወእንዘ ፡ሀለዉ ፡"ክርስቲያን ፡ የሐውሩ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ኮነ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፤ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ሳምራዊ ፡፣ዘስሙ ፡ይስሐቅ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሳምራዊያን ፡ምስሌሁ ። ወኮነ ፣ ውእቱ ፤ ይስሐቅ ፡፤ ሳምራዊ ፡ ይሳለቅ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወይጌሥ ጾሙ ፤፤ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ- ፡ ትጸውሙ-ጎ ፡ በከንቱ?፤ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ተሐውሩ ፡ ወትሰግዱ ፡ ለዕፅ ። ወኮነ ፤ እምውስተ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ቀሲስ" ፣ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣፤ አውኪዶስ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸምዑ ፡ እምሕዝ በ፤ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኢረከቡ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘይሰትዩ ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡፤ ፳ግብ፣ ወረከቡ ፤ ውስቴቱ" ፡ ማየ ፡ ጽዩዓ ፣፡ ወመሪረ፥" ፡ ተመንደቡ ፣፤ ሕዝብ፥ ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ። ወአኀዘ፥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይስሐቅ፤ ፡ ሳምራዊ ፡ ይሳለቅ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ ለእመሰ ፡ ኮነት ፤ ሃይማኖትክሙ ፡ ርትዕተ ፡ ይትወለጥ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማይ ፣
While Christians were going on the road, there was with them a Samaritan whose name (was) Isaac and many Samaritans (were) with him. This Isaac the Samaritan mocked the Christians, blamed them, and said to them: "You fast in vain. And why do you go to worship a (piece of) wood?" There was in the crowd of Christians a man, a righteous priest, whose name (was) Eukhidès. While they were walking on the road, (some) among the crowd of Christians were thirsty and they did not find anything to drink. They arrived near a well and found stagnant and bitter water there; the crowd was greatly grieved. This Isaac the Samaritan began to mock them and said to them: "If your faith is right, let this water which is stagnant and bitter be changed and become
ዘጽዩዕ ፡ ወመሪር ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ማየ ፡ ጥዑመ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ አውኪዶስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፤ እምኔሁ ፡፤ ቀንአ ፡ ቅንአተ ፣ መለኮታዊተ ፡፤ ወተዋሣአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ሳምራዊ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ይስሐቅ ፡ ሳምራዊተቶ፥ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ አንሰ ፡ አአምን" ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ወጸ ለየ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ አውኪዶስ" ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ጽዩዕ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ "ሶቤሃ ፤ ወሰትዩ" ፡ 'እምኔሁ ፤ ኩሉ ፤ ሕዝብ ፤ ወእንስሳሆሙ ። ወይስሐቅሰ ፡፣ ሳምራዊ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጸምአ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተይ ፡ ማየ፣ ዚአሁ ፡ በውስተ፥" ፡ ዝቅ ፡ ወረከባ5፥5-5 ፣ ጽዩዓ ፡ ወውስቴቱ ፡ ፅፀይ" ። ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀሲስ፤ ፡ አውኪዶስ። ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሰትየ ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡
sweet water." When Eukhidès the priest heard this word from him, he was seized with a divine zeal and argued with this Isaac the Samaritan. Isaac the Samaritan said to him: "If I see a miracle (accomplished) in the name of the Cross, I, I will believe in Christ." The priest Eukhidès prayed over the stagnant water; it then became sweet, and all the crowd and their mounts drank from it. As for Isaac the Samaritan, when he was thirsty and wanted to drink his water from the waterskin, he found it stagnant (with) worms in it. He shed bitter tears, came to the holy priest Eukhidès, prostrated himself at his feet, believed in the name of our Lord Christ, and drank of this water which had become sweet thanks to the prayer of the priest Eukhidès.

ማይ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለቀሲስ ፤ አውኪዶስ ። ወኮነ ፡-ውስተ ፡ውእቱ ፡ማይ'ሩ ፡ ኀይል" ፡ከመ ፡ይኩን ፡ጥዑመ ፡ ለመሃይም ናን ፤ ወመሪረ ፡ ለከሓዲያን ፡ ወለአረማዊያን ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡፤ መስቀል ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሠናይተ ። ወሶበ ፡፤ በጽሐ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ሳምራዊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሖረፆዎ» ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጾስ ፡ ወአጥመቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ሎቱ”" ፡ "ወለ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ?፤ ፡ ቤቱ?" ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ዙሎሙ'" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ” ፡ ወኮኑ ፤ መሃይምናነ ። ወአስተርእዮቱሰ ፡፤ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወኮነ' ፡ አመ፣ ፲"ለወርኃ ፡ መጋቢት ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዓለ ፡ በጾም" ፡ ረሰዩ ፡ በዓላ?” ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወኒለመስከረም ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አስተርእዮቱ ፡ ለመቃብር፤ ፡ ቅድስት= ። ወስብሐት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡
There was in this water a virtue by which it was sweet to the believers and bitter to the infidels and the pagans. || A cross of light appeared in this water, and a beautiful church was built over this well. When Yeshaq the Samaritan arrived at the city of Jerusalem, he went to the bishop and (the latter) baptized him with Christian baptism, him and all the people of his house. They all entered into the faith of Christ and became believers. As for the appearance of the glorious Cross, it took place on the tenth of the month of Magabit and, since one cannot hold a feast for it during the fast, its feast was placed on the day of the consecration of its church, which is the seventeenth of Maskaram, which is (also the day of) the appearance of the Holy Sepulcher. Glory and adoration to our Lord Jesus Christ for ever and ever, amen!
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ታኦግንስጣ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ በመዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ለነገሥት ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ አኖርዮስ" ፡ ወአርቃድዮስ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፤መጽኡይ ፡መልእክቱ ፡ለንጉሠ ፡ህ[ን]ደኬ ፤በአምኅ ፡ኀበ ፡ነገሥት ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ አኖርዮስ፤ ፡ ወአርቃድዮስ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ህ[ን]ደኬ" ፡ ረከብዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ድንግል ፤ ታኦግንስጣ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሃ ፡ መጸሐፍ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ታነብብ ፡ ወመሰጥዋ ፡ ወወሰድዋ ፡ ኀበ" ፣፡ ብሔ ሮሙ ። ወኮነት ፡ እመ ፡ ምኔተ ፤ ወተዓቅብ ፡ አንስቲያሁ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ህንደኬ ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ደወየ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ደዌ ፡ እፁበ፤‹ዐ ፡- ወነሥአቶ ።- ቅድስት ፡ ታኦግንስጣ፤!፤፤ ፡- ውስተ ፤ ሕጽና ፡
On this day also died Tä'ognestä (= Theognosta). This saint lived in the time of the righteous emperors 'Anoryos (= Honorius) and 'Argädyos (= Arcadius). At that time, envoys of the king of Hendakë (= India) came with a gift to the righteous emperors 'Anoryos and 'Argädyos. When the envoys of the king of Hendakë returned, they found this virgin Tä'ognestä with a book in her hand, reading. They seized her and took her to their country; she became an abbess and watched over the women of the king of Hendakë and all the people of his house. At that moment, the king's son fell seriously ill; the saint Tä'ognestä took him to her bosom, made the sign of the cross over him, and he was healed on the spot.
ወተዐትብ፤"= ፣፡ ላዕሌሁ!" ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተኣ ምር ፡ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኢኮነት ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አመት ፡ አላ፤ቦ ፣ እግዝእት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ህንደኬ ፡ ኀበ ፡፣ ጸብእ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡
The story of this virgin was divulged in the country because of the miracle that had occurred through her. From that day she was (no longer) among them (= the Indians) as a slave, but (as) a lady. When the king of Hendakë went to

ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጊሜ ፡ ወነፋስ ፡ አውሎ ፡ ወጣቃ ፡ ወጽልመት ። ወንጉጐሥሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የአ ምር ፡፤ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ 'እንተ ፡ ትገብር ፡ ድንግል ፡ ታኦግንስጣ ፡ ወአተበ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ "በትእምርተ ፣መስቀል፤" ፡ላዕለ ፡ጊሜ ፡ ወነፋስ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ፀሐይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጽሕወ፤' ፡ ወዘሐነ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወበትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ሞኦሙ ፡ ለጸላእ ቱ ። ወሶበ" ፡ ገብአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡-እምፀብእ፤" ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሃ ፡ ለቅድስ ት ፤ ታኦግንስጣ ፡ ወሰአላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀቦ ፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ "ወለኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ፤የ ፡ ብሔሩ!!" ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይደልወኒ ፡ እግበር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ ኅበ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ አኖርዮ ስ፤፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ካህነ ፡ እስመ ፡ "አነ ፡ ኢይትከሀለኒ፤! ፡ ከመ ፡
war, mist, a storm wind, darkness, and gloom came upon him. The king knew the sign of the cross that the virgin Tä'ognestä made, and the king made the sign of the cross over the mist and the wind. The sun appeared, (the sky) became serene, and (there was) a great calm. And, by the sign of the cross, (the king) defeated his enemies. When the king returned from the war, he prostrated himself at the feet of the saint Tä'ognestä and asked her to give him the holy Christian baptism, to him and to all the people of his country. She said to the king and all his people: "It is not fitting that I should do this, but send (a message) to the emperor 'Anoryos so that he may send you a priest, because I cannot baptize anyone."
ኣጥምቅ ፡ መነሂ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ፈነዉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ አኖርዮስ፤፤ ፡ ወአጠየቅዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ገብኡ ፤ ውስተ፤፤! ፣ ሃይማኖተ፤!፤ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ሎሙ፤፤ ፣ ቀሲሰ ፡ከመ ፡ ያጥምቆሙ ፡ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትና ። ወፈነወ፤!፤! ፡ሎሙ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፤ ባሕታዌ ፡ ጸድቀ ፣ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስት ና!፤ ። ወወሀቦሙ ፤ እምሥጋሁ ፤ ቅዱስ ፤ ወደሙ!፤፤ ፣ ክቡር ፡ ዘእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ፤ቦ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወተፈሥሐት ፡ ፈድፋደ፤፤፤ ፡ ድንግል ፤ ታኦግንሥጣ ፣ መፍቀሪተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር! ፡ ወአእመረቶ ፣ ለውእቱ! ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወተባረኩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወሐነጸት ፡ ላቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ወተጋብኣ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወአፍቀራ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ዘዚአሃ ፡ ወኮና ፡ ከማሃ ። ወውእቱሰ፤ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ሶበ ፡አጥመቆሙ ፡ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትና ፡ተመይጠ ፡ኀበ ፡አኖርዮስ"፡ ፡ ንጉሥ፤። ፡ ወአጠየቆ! ፡ ከመ፤፤ ፡ 'ብሔረ ፡ ህንደኬ! ፡ ቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤የ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚም ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ውእተ፤!፤ ፡ ባሕታዌ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፣ ወሜሞ፤ ፡ ሎሙ፤ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆአሰ፤ ፡ ወሜጡ!" ፡፤ኀቤ
They then sent (a message) to the emperor 'Anoryos, informing him that they were converting to the faith of Christ and asked him to send them a priest to baptize them with Christian baptism. He sent them a priest, a holy hermit, and (the latter) baptized them all with Christian baptism and gave them the holy body and glorious blood of our Lord Jesus Christ. The virgin Tä'ognestä, friend of God, rejoiced greatly; she made the acquaintance of this hermit and they blessed each other. She built a convent for herself, many virgins gathered around her who loved her monastic garment, and they became like her. As for the priest, the hermit, when he had baptized them (= the people of the country) with Christian baptism, he returned to the emperor 'Anoryos and informed him that the country of Hendakë had entered into the faith of Christ, and the emperor rejoiced greatly. The emperor ordered the patriarch of the city of Rome to appoint this hermit as bishop for them (= the Indians), and he appointed him bishop for them. They (= the patriarch and the emperor) sent him back to them (= the Indians) and (the latter) rejoiced greatly.
