ሕም ፡፤ ወበሐመድ ፡ ወአውዓየ ፡ሆሳዕና ፡ ዘበቀልት፤ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ቤተ ፡ ጣዖታት፤ ፣ አጥፍኦሙ፤?" ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽኡ ፤" ፡ በሳኒታ ፡ እለ፤ጾ፤ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ስርግዋን፤። ፡ በአልባሳት፤›-፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፣ ወሶበ ፡ርእዩ፤ ፡ ለጣዖታቲሆሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተሐኾሉ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ንዴቶሙ ፡ለአማ ልክቲሆሙ ፡ ተበሀሉ፤ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፣ ወይቤሉ፤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮኑ፤ ፡ አማል ክቲነ ፤ ኢይክሉ፤ ፡ ያድኅኑ፤፤ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ እፎ ፡ 'ይትከሀሎሙ ፡ ያድኅኑ ፡ ካልኣነ፤።፤ ። 'ወአምነ ፡ ሶቤሃ፤ ፡ 'መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ! ፡ አትሬብ፤ ፡ በእግዚ እነ፤ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝብ፤ ፡ ዘኢይትፕለቀ-፤ ፡ ወአምኑ፤ ፡
heads of the idols, blackened their faces with charcoal and ash, burned the palm branches, and destroyed all the order of the temple. When (the people) arrived the next day, they who were adorned in their clothes, to do according to their custom, and they saw how their idols were destroyed, they recognized the indigence of their gods, spoke among themselves, and said: "If our gods cannot save themselves, how would it be possible for them to save others?" The governor of the city of 'Atrëb then believed in our Lord Christ, and many innumerable people believed in our Lord Christ - glory to him!
"በእግዚእነ ፤ ክርስቶስ"0-፡0፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ስብሐት ። ወእምህየ ፡ካዕበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ጡው" ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ መኩንነ ፤ ሀገረ ፡ ገምነዲ ፡ ወመኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ አትሬብ" ። ወተጋብአ' ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ዩልዮስ ፡ "ኀበ ፡ እስክንድሮስ" ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ጡው" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፈጽም ፡ ገድልየ ፡ ወአዝዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንሣእ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምፅ ፡ ወእሖር ፡ ኅበ ፣ እግዚእየቦ" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እስክንድ ሮስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይኬቤንነከ ፤ ወኢይገብር ፡ ብከ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ" ፡ እምእኩይ ። ወአ ዘዞሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ለ፳፻አግብርቲሁ ፡ ወመልሑ" ፡ አስ[ይ]ፍቲሆሙ ፡፤ ወተንሥኡ ፤ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እስክንድሮስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ፤፤፤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢአዘዝከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሰነ፤፤ ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ ወንከውን፤ ፡ ሰማዕቶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወእመአኮ፤፤ ፡፣ ንቀትለከ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡'ዲበ ፡ እስክንድሮስ፤" ፡ 'ወተጽዕነ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ፤ ፡ ወጸሐፈ፤ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ ለቅዱ ሳን።።፤ ፡ ወመተሩ"፤፤፤ ፡ ርእሶሙ"፤፤ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡፤ በአስይፍት"፤ ፡ ወእለ" ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፣ ወለቅዱስ" ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፤ ወልዱ ፡ ወለቅዱ ስ"። ፣ ዮንያስ" ፣ እኑሁ ፣ ወለ፳፻ አግብርቲሁ፤ቦየ ፡ ወለመኩንነ ፡ ገምኑዲ"፤፤ ፡
From there, the saint Yulyos also went to the city of Tuw (= Taouah), and with him the governor of the city of Gamnudi and the governor of the city of 'Atrëb, and the saint Yulyos went to 'Eskenderos (= Alexander), the governor of Tuw, and said to him: "Put an end to my life and order my head to be cut off so that I may receive the crown of martyrdom and go to my Lord Jesus Christ." 'Eskenderos said to him: "I will not torture you and will do you no harm." The saint Yulyos gave an order to his five hundred servants; they drew their swords, rose against 'Eskenderos, and said to him: "If you do not order our heads to be cut off, all of us (so that) we may be martyrs of Christ, we will kill you." Then the saint Yulyos ordered an unclean spirit to climb onto 'Eskenderos; the unclean spirit immediately climbed onto him, ('Eskenderos) wrote and ordered the heads of the saints to be cut off, and the heads of all were cut off with swords. These are the saint Yulyos, the saint Tewodros (= Theodore), his son, the saint Yonyäs, his brother, his five hundred servants, the governor of Gamnudi, the governor of 'Atrëb, and many people. The number of those who were martyrs with the saint Yulyos on the day of his martyrdom (is) one thousand five hundred. All received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven.
ወለመኩንነ ፡ አትሬብ ፡ ወለብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝብ ፤፣ ወፕልቆሙ" ፡ ፲፻ወ፳፻እለ ፤ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ስምዑ ። ወነሥኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ሰማያት ። ወጾሩ ፡ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ወሥጋ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወሥጋ ፡ እኑሁ ፡ ወወሰድዎሙ ‹፣ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፣የኀድር" ፣ ውስቴታ፤፤ ፣ ወልደቱሰ ፡ እምሀገረ ፤ አቅፋህስ› ። ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ሠናይ ። ወእም ድኅረዝ ፤ ሐነጹ ፣ ሎሙ0 ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ሠናይተ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡፣ ዓበይት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
The body of the saint Yulyos, the body of his son, and the body of his brother were taken and brought to the city of Alexandria, for he (= Yulyos) had lived there, but he was born in the city of 'Agfähes. Their bodies were placed in a beautiful place and, after that, a beautiful church was built for them. Great miracles and wonders occurred through them. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.


[p. 466] {#po.43.p466}
ወበዛቲ ፤ ፅለት፤ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ባላን ። በረከቱ፤ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡፤ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day (is) the commemoration of Bälän. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 23RD OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፳፻ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡፤ ቅዱሳን፤ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ አውናብዮስ ፡ ወእ[ን]ድርያስ ፤ ሰማዕታት ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እምውሉድዶ ፡ ልሂቃን ፡ 'እምሀገረ ፡ ልዳ ። ወተሰናአዉ ፡ እምንእሶሙ ፡ በምክረ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ዶደብር ፡፤ እምአድባራተ ፡ ሀገረ" ፤ ሶርያ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አርድእተ ፡ ወኀደሩ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ወተአዘዙ ፡ሎቱ ፡ወተጋደሉ ፡ በምክረ ፡ዚአሁ ። ወነበሩ ፡ይፀመዱ፤" ፡ዘመዝ፤! ፡ ዓመተ ። ወኮኑ፤" ፡ 'ይጸውሙ ፡ ወትረ!" ፡ ወይጹልዩ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ በፍቅር ፡ ወትሕትና! ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ወተጋድሎቶሙ! ፡ ወተፀምዶ ቶሙ ፡ ወኀኅረይዎ ፡ ለአውናብዮስ ፣ ወሜምዎ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆኦሰ ፡ ወለእ[ን]ድርያ ስኒ! ፡ ጫምዎ ፡ ቀሲሰ፤" ። ወዓቀብዎሙ ፡ "ለመርኤቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ፤" ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፤ ወተጋደሉ! ፡ ተጋድሎ" ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአኅሠሩ፤፤ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፣ ወሥጋሆ ሙ፣ በጻማ ፡ ወበምንዳቤ፤፦፦”፤ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወሠምዓ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ መልእክተ፤፤ ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡፣ ኅቤሁ ፤ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሐድዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይባኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ እኪት" ፡ እንተ ፡ አምልኮ ፤ ጣዖታት ። ወኢሰምዑ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፤ ወረገምዎ ፡ ወረገሙ ፡፤ጣዖታቲሁ ፡፤ርኩሳተ ። ወተምዕዓ፤ ፡ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ወኩነኖሙ ፡ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በብዙኅ ፣ መክፈልትቶ፥"፤ ፡ ወመጠዉ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ 'ለእግዚ እነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ 'ዊአክሊላተ ፡ ዘስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያትቶ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፤ ወአምልኮ?፥ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወበእንተ ፣ ተፀምዶት፤ ፡ ወተጋድ ሎ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ክፅወተ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ በእንተ" ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእ ነ0 ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day were martyred the holy righteous 'Awnäbyos (= Eunape) and ‘Endreyäs (= Andrew). These saints were among the children of the notables of the city of Ledä (= Lydda). They were united from their childhood by a divine plan. They went and became monks in a monastery among the monasteries of the land of Soryä (= Syria). After that, they went to the holy illustrious abbä Magäres (= Macarius), were disciples for him, and remained with him. They obeyed him and devoted themselves to asceticism according to his advice. They remained dedicating themselves to devotion thus for three years. They fasted constantly and prayed day and night with love and humility. Their reputation, their (ascetic) struggle, and their devotion became known, and 'Awnäbyos was chosen and consecrated bishop. As for 'Endreyäs, he was ordained priest. They guarded the flock of Christ with a good guard, led a great (ascetic) struggle, and mortified their soul and their body through fasting and torments for many years. The renegade emperor Yulyänos (= Julian) heard (of) their reputation; he sent a message, had them brought to him, and ordered them to deny Christ and to enter his evil faith which (was) the worship of idols. They did not obey his order, cursed him, and cursed his impious idols. (The emperor) was angry with them and (made) them undergo great tortures of many kinds. They gave up their soul into the hand of our Lord Christ and received three crowns of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven because of (their) monastic state and (their) beautiful adoration, because of (their) true asceticism and (ascetic) struggle, and because of their blood shed for the faith of our Lord Christ - glory to him! May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ፤ ፡ ሰማዕተ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡ ወወልዱ ፡፤ ወብእሲቱ ። "በረከተ ፤ አምላኮሙቶ ፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, the saint 'Éwostatéwos (= Eustathius), his son, and his wife became martyrs. May the blessing of their God be with us for ever and ever, amen.]


[p. 468] {#po.43.p468}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካራ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ተክላ ፡ መርዓቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወጓ፪ ሰማዕታት ፣ ወጎርጎርዮስ ፣ መንክራዊ ፡ ወእንጽናእ+፥ ፡ ወቦ ሊና" ፡ 'ወአባ ፡ ሰላማ ፡ ጸድቅ ። 'ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ አሜን ።
On this day also is the commemoration of the holy virgin Takla (= Thecla), the bride of Christ, [of the forty-four martyrs, of Gorgoryos (= Gregory) the Wonderworker, of 'Ensenä'e, of Bolinä, and of abbä Salämä the righteous.] May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
አመ ፡፤ ፳፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስ ፤ መነኮስ ። ዝንቱ! ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ ኮነ ፤ እምላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ወኮኑ ፡፤ አበዊሁ ፡ "ክርስቲያን ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ኅሩያን ፡ ወብዑላን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወብዙኅ ፤ ንዋዮሙ ። ወመሀርዎ ፡ 'ለወልዶሙ ፡፤ ጎርጎርዮስ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ትምህርት ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡፣ ወሥጋዊ ። ወእምዝ ፡ መሀርዎ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ 'ነገር ፤ ወጥበብ ፡ ወትምህርተ ፡ መጻሕፍተ” ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገሮሙ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ!" ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ አናጐንስጢስ ። ወኀሠጮሠ ፡፤ እምኔሁ ፡-ከመ ፡ያውስብ ፡ብእሲተ ፡ወአበየ ፡ዘንተ ፡ግብረ፤፤ ። ወእምድኅረዝ፤" ፡ አቅረብዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ፍጹመ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ፤፣ የሐውር ፡፤ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወይትመሀር ፡ 'እምኔሁ ፡ ፍኖተ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር፤፤ ። ወነሥአ ፤ እምአበዊሁ" ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአቅረበ ፡ ኅበ፤" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ጳኩሚስ፤-፤ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ውእተ ፡ንዋየ ፡ "በእንተ ፡ ሕንጸተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መኅበር ፡ ዘመነኮሳት ፡ በሱታፌ"" ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ በቅድመ ፡
[READING] FOR THE 24TH OF MASKARAM. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Gorgoryos (= Gregory) the monk. This saint was from Upper Egypt and his parents were righteous (and) chosen Christians; they were very rich and their fortune was considerable. They had their son Gorgoryos instructed in all the sciences of the spirit and of the body. Then they had him learn the science of speech and of medicine and the science of the books of the holy Church. After that, they led him to abbä Yeshaq (= Isaac), the bishop of their city. He laid his hand upon him and made him an anagnost (reader). His parents asked him to marry a woman, but he refused this thing. After that, they brought him to the bishop and he ordained him a full deacon. He always went to the holy abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius) and learned from him the way of God. He received a considerable fortune from his parents and brought it to the holy abbä Päkuemis. But the holy abbä Päkuemis gave this fortune in the presence of Gorgoryos for the construction of a common house for monks who were in his own community.
ጎርጎርዮስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡መነነ ፡ኩሎ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ጥሪቶ ፡ ወንዋዮ ፡ወሖረ ፡ኀኅበ፣ ቅዱስ ፡አባ፤፤ ፡ጳኩሚስ ፡ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ምንኩስና ፡ወነበረ ፣በኀቤሁ፤ ፡ "ወአጸመወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎ ት።፣፡ ወበሰጊድ ፡ ወበትጋህ ፡ ወበትሕትና ፡ ወበየውሃት ፡ ወበፍቅር ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ዘማዊያን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሬእይዎ፥ቦ ፣ ይትመሀሩ ፡ እምነ ፡ አርአያሁ ፤ ወአልባሲሁ፤ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወየኃድጉ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡እኩየ ፡ ወይነስ ሑ ፣ ወይከውኑ ፡ ንጹሓነ ። ወነበረ ፡ በኀበ፥ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ፕወ፻ጀዓመተ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡መቃርስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ" ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ፥፤ ፡ ይትመየ ጥ ፡ ኅበ። ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወይ
After that, Gorgoryos totally rejected the world, all its goods and his fortune, and went to the holy abbä Päkuemis; he clothed him in the monastic habit and he remained with him, wearying his soul and his body and waging a great (ascetic) combat in fasting, in prayer, in adoration, in vigil, in humility, in gentleness, and in charity, so much so that the lustful, when they saw him, learned from his appearance and his clothes to fear God, abandoned their evil way of acting, did penance, and became pure. Gorgoryos remained with the holy abbä Päkuemis for thirteen years. When the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius) came to the holy abbä Päkuemis, he remained with him for a few days and wanted to return to 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). This father (= Gorgoryos) prayed to the holy abbä Päkuemis and said to him:
ቤሎ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ 'ዓመታተ ፡ ብዙኃ" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጎ ፡ ይንበር፥ ፡ በባሕቲቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዖማዕት ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ግበር ፡፤ ዘትፈቅድ ። ወሖረ ፡ 'ወወቀረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ በዓት ፡ ንእስት" ፡ ወነ በረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡፤ ፪ዓመተ፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ፪ጊዜ ፡ ለለዓመት ። ፳ጊዜ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ልደት ፡ ቅድስት ። ወካልኡ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ፤ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ፥ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወይነሥ እ፣ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡፤ ወይትመየጥ ፡ ኀበ ፡ በዓቱ ። ወኮነ" ፣፡ አባ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ይስእሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ "ዙሉ ፡ ግብሩ፤ ፡ ለለዓመት ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከሥት ፡፤ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ "ዘያስተርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኮነ፤ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ የአ
"Permit me, my father, to go with the holy abbä Magäres to 'Asqêtes." Abbä Päkuemis gave him a mandate to go with him. The holy Gorgoryos went with the holy abbä Magäres and remained with him for many years. Then he asked the holy abbä Magäres to permit him to dwell alone in a cell. Abbä Magäres said to him: "Do what you wish." He went away, dug for himself a small cave in the mountain, and dwelt there for seven years. He came to the holy abbä Magäres twice a year: once on the day of the feast of the holy birth (= Christmas) and the other on the day of the feast of the resurrection of our Lord Christ (= Easter) - glory to him! He would receive the holy mysteries and return to his cave. Abbä Magäres would question him each year about his whole way of life; he would reveal to him everything that had appeared to him, and abbä Magäres would prescribe to him what he should do in the way of monastic (ascetic) exercises.


[p. 470] {#po.43.p470}
ዝዞ። ፡ በዘይገብር ፡ እምተጋድሎ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመት ፡ ወስተ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚኣብ ሔር ፤ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡-እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡፻መዓልት ፡ ትፈልስ ፡እምዝንቱ ፡ዓለም ፡ ኀላፊ ፡ ወትባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስ ፡፣ "ለአረጋዊያነ ፤ ገዳመ" ፡ አስቄጥስ ፤ ወተአምጥኖሙ ፡ ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝክር ዎ ። ወእሙንቱኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝክሮሙ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። "ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ በጾመዓልት"“ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ወሖረ ፡፤ ውስተ ፤ ትፍሥሕት'""-”፤ ።
When he had spent twenty-two years in the (ascetic) combat, God wished to grant him rest far from the pains of this world. He sent his angel to him and he said to him: "In three days, you will leave this transitory world and enter into eternal joy." The holy Gorgoryos called the elders of the desert of 'Asqêtes, embraced them, and asked them to remember him. As for them, they asked him in return to remember them before God. After that, on the third day, he died and went away into joy. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ጻሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ” ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ብድራጦስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምድወ፪አርድእት ፡ እለ ፡ ኀረዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡- ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ሐዋርያ” ፡ ኮነ?" ፡ እምውሉደ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቴና ፡ ወእምልሂቃነ ፡ ማእምራኒሃ ። ወአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተለአኮ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዛ ፡ ሖረ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ "በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ማሕያዊ፤ ። ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መግኒሳ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ በወንጌል ፡፤ ቅዱስ፣ ወአብርሃ ፡ አልባቢሆሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ ማኅያዊተ ፡ዘወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወጫመ ፡ሎሙ ፡ ቀሰውስተ ፡ ወዲያቆ ናተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቴና ፡
On this day also, there is the holy Bedrätos (= Quadratus), the apostle and martyr, who is one of the seventy-two disciples whom our Lord Christ chose - glory to him! This apostle was from the children of the notables of the city of Athènes (= Athens) and the leaders of its scholars. He believed in our Lord Christ and served him. When he had received the grace of the Holy Spirit, the Paraclete, on the day of Pentecost, he went into many countries, preached the holy life-giving Gospel, and entered into the city of Magnisä (= Magnesia); he preached the holy Gospel there, enlightened the hearts of the people of this city by the faith of our Lord Christ - glory to him! -, baptized them with Christian baptism, taught them the life-giving commandments of the holy Gospel, and ordained priests and deacons for them. Then he left them, returned to the city of Athènes, and preached there. They threw stones at him and made him undergo great


[p. 472] {#po.43.p472}
ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ በአእባን ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ 'ዙነኔ ፣ ዓቢየ ። ወእምድ ኅረዝ ፡ ወገርዎ‹-፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በመንግ ሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀለ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡
tortures. After that, they threw him into the fire and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us forever, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡፤ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቤቴርዋ ፡ ሰመዕት ፡ ወለታይን ፡ ወዕረፍቱ ፡ ለገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፡ ወቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወንጥራሌምስ ፡ ወገድሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡፤ አውሳንዮስ ፣ ወንስጥዮስ ፡፣ ወዝዮኖስ" ፡ ወዮስጦስ ፤ ወአኃዊሁ” ፡ እልስርይ ፡፤ ወፍራጦስ ፤ ቆዝሞስ ፡ ወድምያኖስ ። 'በረከቶሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ፤"0 ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also is the commemoration of Bétërwä, the martyr, and of Täyen, the death of Gabr'ël (= Gabriel), the patriarch, of Tewodros (= Theodore) and of Neträlèmes, and the (ascetic) combat of the holy 'Awsänyos (= Ausanius), of Nestyos, of Zeyonos, of Yostos (= Justus), and of his brothers ‘Elserey, Ferätos (= Phrate), Qozmos (= Cosmas), and Demyänos (= Damian). May the blessing of these righteous ones and martyrs be with our king 'lyäsu for ages of ages.]

### READING FOR THE 25TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፳፳ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ "ነቢይ ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፡ ዮናስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ወልደ ፡ መበለት ፡ እንተ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ "ሰርብጣ ፡ ሲዶና" ፡ ዘአንሥኦ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሞት ። ወተለዎ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወተለአኮ ፡ ወጻመወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወደለዎ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ በተአዝዞቱ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፤ ስሙ ፡ ወልዑል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሖር?" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ነነዌ ፡ ወስብክ ፡ ለ[ሰ]ብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወበሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፻መዓልት ፡ ትትገፈታዕ ፡ ሀገርክ ሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚኣሔር ፡ዘንተ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ኮነ ፡ 'እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ይፈቅድ ፣ ጠፊኦቶሙ!፤" ፡ "ኢኮነ ፡ የአዝዘኒ፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ወእስብክ ፡ ሎሙ ። ወለእመሰ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ወሰበኩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወእቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ያጥፍእክሙ ፡ ወአንሰ፤ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፤፡ ማሓሪ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይምህሮሙ ፡ ወእከውን ፡ አነ ፡ በኀቤሆ ሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐሳዌ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወኢይሰምዕ" ፡ 'ስብከትየ ፡፣ መኑሂ፤ ፡ ግሙራ ፣ ወፈድፋደ ፡ይቀትሉኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ፤ ፡ ተፈነውኩ ፡ በሐሰት ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚኣብ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the great prophet Yonäs (= Jonah). This righteous man was the son of a widow who was from the house of Sarbetä Sidonä (= Sarepta of Sidon); it is he whom 'Élyäs (= Elijah) the prophet raised from the dead. He followed 'Élyäs the prophet, served him, suffered with him, and merited the gift of prophecy by obeying him. God - blessed and exalted be his name! - gave him an order and said to him: "Go to the city of Nanawëe (= Nineveh), preach to its inhabitants, and say to them: In three days, your city will be destroyed." When God said this to him, Yonäs meditated in his heart and said to himself: "If God wanted to annihilate them, he would not order me to go and preach to them; and if I go and preach to them and say to them: God wants to annihilate you, I fear that, as God is merciful, he will spare them, and I will thus be to them like a lying prophet; absolutely no one will listen to my preaching and, furthermore, they will kill me, for they will believe that I have been sent falsely by God. But I will arise, flee, and I will not go to that city nor will I preach to them (= the Ninevites)."
ሔር ። አላ ፡ አንሰ፤' ፡ እትነሣእ፤ ፡ ወእጐይይ ፣ ወኢየሐውር ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኢይሰብክ ፣ ሎሙ ። ኦአኀዊነ ፣ ቦኑ ፡ ይትከሀሎ ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጉየይ ፡ እምነ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ልዑል ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘእምነቢያት ፤ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ ከመዝ ፡ 'እፎ ፡ ይከውን፤፤ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ። አላ ፡ አንሰ ፣ አሐሊ። ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ገብረ ፡ በኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይጉየይ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እስከ ፣፤ አስተርአየት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ በነቢሮቱ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ፤ ፡ አንበሪ ፡'ሠሉሰ ፡መዋዕለ ፡ ወሠሉሰ ፡ ለያልየ ፡ወወፅአ፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጥዑይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡፤ ሙስና ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ትእምርት" ፡ ለነ
Our brothers, is it possible for anyone among men to flee from God the Most High? If the heart of this prophet among the prophets of the children of Israel was thus, how must the heart of the people have been! But I think that God - blessed be his name! - acted by a ruse of his wisdom so that this prophet would flee, so that this miracle would be manifested by his stay in the belly of the great fish for three days and three nights - and he came out of it intact without


[p. 474] {#po.43.p474}
ቢሮቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ-5፤ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ስብሐት ፡ውስተ ፡መቃብር ፡ሠላ ሰ። ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወሠላ[ሰ]! ፡ ለያልየ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ እሙታን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ውላ ጤ፥!፥።
damage. This miracle occurred as a symbol for the stay of our Lord Christ - glory to him! - in the tomb for three days and three nights, and he rose from the dead without change.
ወተንሥአ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወጐየ፥ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር" ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሀገረ ፤ ጠርሴስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጽዕነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ተን ሥአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ አውሎ ፡ ወማዕ[በ]ለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወመ[ዋ]ግዲሁ ፡ ወአልጸ ቁ ፣ ለተሰጥሞ ፤ ወጠፊእ፤ ። ወወደየ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ልቡና ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፤ ለሊቀ ፡፤ ሐመር ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጹዓኑ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሐመር ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ተዐፀዉ ፡ በበይናቲክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናእምር ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘበእንቲአሁ ፡ መጽአ ፤ "ላዕሌነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ። ወሶበ ፣ ተዓፀዉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወልአ ፡ ዕፃ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዮናስ ፣ ነቢይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘገበርከ ፡ እስከ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ በእንቲአከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ነቢይ0፣ ውግሩኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወአንትሙሰ፥" ፡ ትድኅኑ ። ወሊቀ ፡ ሐመርሰ ፡ወሰብአ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አስተብሞዑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ ኢትትኃሠ ሠነ0 ፡ 'በደሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ፤ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡፤ ወውሕጦ ፡ አንበሪ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ዓንበሪ ፡ ሠሉሰ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡
Yonäs the prophet arose, fled, and wanted to go to the city of Tarsès (= Tarsus). When he had boarded a boat and they (= the travelers) had advanced a little, a storm wind rose against them and also the waves of the sea and its billows, and they were on the point of being engulfed and annihilated. God put intelligence into the heart of the captain; he spoke to those who had boarded the boat and said to them: "Cast lots among yourselves so that we may know who is the one because of whom this peril has come upon us." When they had cast lots among themselves, the lot fell upon Yonäs the prophet. The captain said to him: "What have you done so that this misfortune has come upon us because of you?" Yonäs the prophet said to them: "Throw me into the sea and you will be saved." The captain and his men implored God and said: "O Lord, do not seek a quarrel with us for the blood of this man." They took him and threw him into the sea, and a great fish swallowed him. He remained in the belly of the great fish for three days and three nights.
ወሠሉሰ ፡ ለያልየ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ጐሥዖ፤። ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ሀገረ ፡ ነነዌ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ አእምሩ ፡ "ከመ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመዓልት፥፤ ፤ ትትገፈታዕ ፤ ሀገርክሙ ፤ ወትጠፍኡ ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ ወኡዶደታ ፡ ለሀገረ ፡ ነነዌ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፻መዓ ልት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ነነዌ ፣ ስብከቶ ፡ ለዮናስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወነስሑ ፡ ወጾሙ ፡ ወለብሱ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ጸኾጐር ፡ ልሂቃን ፡ ወንኡሳን ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ስብከተ ፡ዮናስ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ንጉሠ ፡ ነነዌ ፡ወወረደ ፡ እመንበሩ ፡ ወአውረደ ፡ አ[ክ]ሊለ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ እምዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ወለብሰ ፡ሠቀ ፡ ጸጐር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ፤ ፡ ሐመድ ። ወእምዝ ፡፣ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኡድ ፥፡ አዋዲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ" ፡ ነነዌ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለመኳንንቲ ሁ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዓበያን ፡ ወንኡሳን ፡ ወአንስት" ፡ ወሕፃናት ፡ ወእንስ ሳ ፣፡ ወአልህምት ፡ወአባግፅ ፡ከመ ፡'ይጹሙ ፣ወኢይብልዑ ፣ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ወኢይስትዩ ፡ ማየ" ፡ አላ ፡ ይልበሱ ፡ ሰብእ" ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ጸጐር ። ወከማሁ ፡ እንስሳ ኒ ፤ ኢይርአዩ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይኅድጉ" ፡ 'ምግባራቲሆሙ ፡ እኪት"0፡፣ 'እሉ። እሙንቱ?፤፡ ዓመፃ፣ ወዝሙት ፡ ወጽልሑ ት?። ፡ ወሐሰት ፡ ወሥርቀት?-” ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣሀል ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕሌነ?-"" ፡ ወያእትት ፡ መዓቶ ፡ እምኔነ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነስሑ ፤ ዙሎሙ ፡ ወጾ
Then the fish vomited him onto the shore of the sea. He then arose, entered the city of Nanawë, and preached to them as God had ordered him. He said to them: "Know that, in three days from this day, your city will be destroyed and you will all be annihilated." Going around the city of Nineveh took three days. When the people of Nanawë heard the preaching of Yonäs the prophet, they were very afraid, believed in God, repented, fasted, and put on, notables and small people alike, a sack of hair. The preaching of Yonäs reached the king of Nanawë; he descended from his throne, took off his royal crown from his head, put on a sack of hair, and sat on the ashes. Then the king ordered that a herald circulate in the city of Nanawë saying: "The king orders his officers, all the people, great and small, women and children, the animals, the oxen, and the sheep, to fast, to eat nothing, and not to drink water. But let the people clothe themselves in a sack of hair and, likewise, let the animals not graze on the grass. Let all the people abandon their evil ways of acting which are injustice, lust, fraud, lying, and theft. Perhaps God will be merciful to us and

ሙ ፡ ወኀሠሥሠ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ እምኅበ” ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፣ ወመሀሮ ሙ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወሐዘነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ 'ሐዘነ ፡ ዓቢየ5፤ ፣፡ ወይቤ ፤ እግዚኦ ፡ አንሥኣ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፤ እስመ ፡ ሞት ፡፤ ይቴይሰኒ ፤ እምሕይወት ። ወወፅአ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወኖመ ። ወእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ለስርወ ፤ ሐምሐም ፡ ወበቄለ ፡ወልህቀ ፡"ወኮነ ፡ሎቱ ፡ አዕፁቃተ ፡'ወቄጽላኒ ፡ወዓርገ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፣ ወጸለለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነውም ። ወነቅሐ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ እምንዋሙ ፡ ወርአዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሐምሐም ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ዮናስ ፡ ድቃሰ ፡ ወኖመ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ለዕፄ" ፡ ወመተረትይ ፡ ሥርዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ሐምሐም ። ወአዘዘ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ነፋሰ' ፡ ዐውሎ ፤ ወአይበሳ ፤ ለሐምሐም ፡ ወአልሐበት ፡ ፀሓይ፤ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለዮናስ ፡ ወነ ቅሐ ፡ እምንዋሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፡ ለሐምሐም”። ፡ ይብስተ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኀኅሠሠ ፡ "ሞተ ፡ ለነፍሱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለዮናስ ፡ ሐዘንከኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሐምሐም ። ወይቤሎ" ፡ዮናስ ፡ እወ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ሐዘንኩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለመዊት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ አንተ ፡ ሐዘንከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሐምሐም ፡ዘኢዘራእከ ፡ ወኢያብቄልከ ፡፤ ወኢጸመውከ ፡ ቦቱ” ፡ አላ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣፤ በቄለ፤0 ፡ ወበአሐቲ!'! ፣፤ ዕለት ፡ የብሰ!" ፡ ለም ንት!የ ፡'ኢይምሕክ ፡ አነ፤" ፡ ለነነዌ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ዘውስቴታ፤ ፣ ዘይፈደፍ ድ፡ እም፲ወ፪፻፻፤!" ፡ ዘኢያአምሩ ፡ የማኖሙ፣፡ ወፀጋሞሙ!‹ ፡ ወእንስ ሳ፤ጾ ፣ ብዙኃን፤' ። እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ መሓሪ ፡ ወመስተሣህል ፡ እሣሀል!!" ፡ላዕለ ፡
remove his anger from us." Immediately, all repented, fasted, and asked for mercy from God - blessed be his name! -, and God had mercy on them. Yonäs the prophet felt great sorrow and said: "Lord, take away my soul, for death is better for me than life." Yonäs went outside the city, sat there, and fell asleep. God gave an order to a root of a gourd, it sprouted and grew, it had branches and leaves, it then rose up and shaded the sleeping Yonäs. Yonäs awoke from his sleep, saw this gourd, and rejoiced with great joy. The next day, God brought a drowsiness upon Yonäs and he fell asleep. God commanded a worm and it cut the root of the gourd. God commanded a storm wind and it dried up the gourd. The sun burned the head of Yonäs and he awoke from his sleep. When he saw the dried-up gourd, he was very afflicted and asked for death for himself. God said to Yonäs: "Are you very afflicted for the gourd?" Yonäs said to him: "Yes, Lord, I am very afflicted, even to death." God said to him: "If you are afflicted for a gourd that you did not plant, nor make sprout, and for which you did not have any fatigue - but in one day it sprouted and in one day it dried up -, why would I not have pity, I, for Nanawë, the great city, in which there are more than one hundred and twenty thousand people who do not recognize their right hand and their left, and many animals. Indeed, I am merciful and compassionate and I forgive whoever turns away from his sin and repents."
ኩሉ ፡ ዘይትመየጥ ፡ እምአበሳሁ ፡ ወይኔስሕ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ባቲ ። ወኮነ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ እምቅድመ ፤ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእ[ነ]፤!፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በ፪ ፻ዓመት፤!፤ ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአመጽያ፤! ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ "ወለወልዱ ፡ ዖዝያ፤፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ፤፤ ፡ መዋዕለ ፤ ሕይወቱ ፡ ፻ዓመት ፣፡ እም ኔሃ!! ፡፣ ድዓመተ'፤!" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትነበይ ፡፤ 'ወቋዓመተ ፣፤ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትነበ
Yonäs arose, returned to the land of Israel, and died there. His prophecy took place nine hundred years and more before the coming of our Lord Christ. He prophesied in the days of 'Amasyä (= Amaziah), the king, and of his son 'Ozyà (= Uzziah). All the days of his life were one hundred years, of which he spent seventy years prophesying and thirty years before prophesying. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us.
ይ፤!8 ። ጸሎቱ፤! ፡ ወበረከቱ ፣ ወትንብልናሁ፤" ፡ የሀሉ፤፡፤ ፡ ምስሌነ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ፤፡ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለፎቃ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ወአንድ ራዎስ ፤ ወጴጥሮስ ፤ ወእንጦንዮስ፤" ፣ 'ወበርባራ ፡ ወዮልያና ፡ እኅታ ፡፣ እንተ ፡ ገብረት ፤ ተአምረ፤ ። 'በረከተ ፤ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን፤ ። < ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡-እለ ፡ተሰምዩ ፡ አዕማደ ፡

May the blessing of their prayer be with us, amen.] <On this day also are Pêtros (= Peter) and Päwlos (= Paul) who are


[p. 478] {#po.43.p478}
ምድር ። "እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ ነገ ደ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ስእለ ፡ ጽዮን ፡፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም፤፤ ።>
called the pillars of the earth. May God have mercy on us through their prayer. May their prayer be with their dear Nagada 'lyasus Se'ela Seyon for ages of ages.>

### READING FOR THE 26TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፳5ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወልደ ፡ በራኪዩ ፡ ወአዜነዎ ፡ በልደተ፤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣፤ መጥምቅ ፡ ወልዱ ። እስመ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወኀኅለፈ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ወሊድ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥኒ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ኮነት ፡ መካነ ፡ ወኀለፈ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ፅንሰታ ፡ወወሊዶታ ። ወኮነ ፡ዘካርያስ ፡'ወትረ ፡ይጹሊ- ፡ወያስተበቀዕ ፡ኀበ ፡፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፤ ኮኑ" ፡ ይትዓየርዎ ፡ ለዘኢተወልደ ፣፤ ሎቱ ፡፤ ወልድ ፡ ወያሐጽጽዎ ፡ ወየኀሥርዎ ፡ "ወይብሉ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ!" ፣ ኃጥአ፤፤ ፡ ዘይቤ!" ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለአቡነ ፡-አዳም ፡ብዝጐ ፡ተባዝጉ፤ ፡ ወምልእዋ ፡ለምድር ። ወበእን[ተ]ዝ ፡ኮነ ፡ "ጸድቅ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ወትረ! ፡ ያስተበቀ'ዕ ፡ ኀበ ፡፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ወልደ ። ወተሣሀለ ፡ 'እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ! ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ስእ ለቶ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገብርኤልሃ ፡ መልአከ፤ ፡ ወአዜነዎ፤" ፡ 'በልደቱ ፡ ለዮሐን ስ፤! ፡ ወልዱ ። ወመጽአ ፡!'!ኀቤሁ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም፤፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣መቅደስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ"" ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሰበከ ፡ሎቱ ፡ በነቢይ" ፣ ዓቢይ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ "ወይጺሕ ፤ ፍኖቶ፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ በነቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ሰባኬ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ። ወዘካርያስሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የአምር ፡፤ ድካመ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡፣ "ወልሂቅ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ፤" ፡፣ ወርሥዕናሁ ፡ ወብእሲቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወበእንተ ዝ ፡ ተቃረኖ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይከውነኒ ፡ 'ሊተ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ 'ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ፤ ፡ ወብእሲትየ ፡ መካን ፡ 'ወልህቅት ፣ በመዋ ዕሊሃ፤ ። ወገሠጾ ፡ ወልአክ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ተፈነውኩ ፡እምኅበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ 'እዜኑከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ወኢይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትናፍቅ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። አላ ፡ ትከውን ፡ በሃመ ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ፥" ፣ ይትፈጸም ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በጊዜሁ ። ወኮነ፥ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ በሃመ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡"ጸያሒ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day God sent Gabr'ël (= Gabriel) the angel to Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) the priest, son of Baräkiyu (= Barachiah), and the angel announced to him the birth of Yohannes (= John) the Baptist, his son. Indeed, this righteous Zakäryäs was very advanced in age and had passed the time for begetting. Likewise, 'Élsäbet (= Elizabeth), his wife, was barren and the time for conception and childbirth had passed for her. Zakäryäs prayed continually and besought God - blessed be his name! - to give him a son, for the children of Israel despised the one to whom no son was born, esteemed him inferior, vilified him, and said about him: "This one does not have what God said to our father Adam: Increase, multiply, and fill the earth." Because of this, the righteous Zakäryäs besought God continually to give him a son. God - blessed be his name! - was favorable to him, heard his prayer, sent him Gabr'ël the angel, and he announced to him the birth of Yohannes his son. The angel came to him while he was standing in the sanctuary, just as it is said in the holy Gospel, and announced the great prophet Yohannes, that he would come before Christ the King and would level his path, just as it is said about him in the prophet: He will be a herald before him. But Zakäryäs knew his weakness, the advancement of his days, his old age, and that his wife was barren. Because of this, he resisted the angel, questioned him, and said to him: "How would this happen to me when I am an old man and my wife is barren and advanced in age?" The angel rebuked him, informed him, and said to him: "I have been sent from God to announce this to you and it is not fitting that you doubt it. But you will be mute until this is accomplished in its time." Zakäryäs was mute until Yohannes the leveler, his son, was born. At the moment of his circumcision, they asked him (= Zakäryäs): "How will you call him?" He wrote on a tablet his name: Yohannes. Immediately, his tongue was loosened; he spoke, praised
ወልዱ፥‹ ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡ ግዝረቱ ፡ ሰአልዎ፤ ፡ 'ምንተ ፡ ዘትሰምዮ= ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ጽላት ፡ 'ስሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ 'ተፈትሐ ፡ ልሳኑቶ ፡ ወተ ናገረ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ይከውን ፡ ነቢየ" ፡ ወየሐውር ፡ቅድመ ፡ገጸ ፡መሲሑ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። 'ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከ
God, and prophesied about his son Yohannes that he would be a prophet and would walk before the face of the Messiah of God, and he prophesied about our Lord Christ - glory to him! May the prayer and the blessing of this righteous Zakäryäs the priest be with us, amen.


[p. 480] {#po.43.p480}
ቱ ፡ "ለዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ" ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ካህን ፡ የሀሉ፤ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። 52በዛቲ፤ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቦሊ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወፍ ልሰተ ፡ ሥጋሁ፤" ። ወተዝካረ ፣ ፻፻ሰማዕታት ፣ ወዓገታ፥ ፡ ዘግብጽ ። ወአቡ ነ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። 'ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ወይባርከነ ፡ አሜን” ።
On this day is the commemoration of the holy 'Aboli, the martyr, and the translation of his body, [the commemoration of the ten thousand martyrs and of 'Agatä (= Agatha?) of Egypt and of our father Giyorgis (= George), the martyr.] May his prayer and his blessing be with us and may he bless us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 27TH OF MASKARAM
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኤውስጣቴዎስ! ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወ፪ውሉዱ ፡ ወብእሲ ቱ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሊቃነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘመንግሥተ ፡ ሮም ፡ "ወኢኮነ ፡ ያአምሮ ፣፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ወኮነ ፡ 'ቀዳሚ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ቂዶስ ። ወኮነ ፡'ብዙኃ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ወምሕረተ" ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወኢፈቀደ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር" ፤ ይሬሲ ፡ ጻማሁ ፡፣ ከንቶ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ኤውስጣቴዎስ፤ ፡ ይፀንኅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይንዕው ፡ አራዊተ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አስተርአዮ፤፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መስ ቀል ፡ ማእከለ ፡ 'አቅር[ን]ተ ፡ ሃየል፤፤ ፡ ወመስቀሉሰ ፡ ይበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደመና ። ወሰደዶ ፡ ለሃየል ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንዓዎ፤ ፡ ወተናገሮ ፡፣ እግዚእነ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ 'አቅር[ን]ተ ፡ ሃየል፤ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ስሞ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ወውሉዱ ፡ ወብእሲቱ፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆስ ፡ ዘይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወይጠመቁ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወአጠየቆ ፡ እግዚእነ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ!" ፡ ያመጽዓ! ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፤ ንዴተ! ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ኤውስጣቴዎስ' ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ"፤ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ እምደብር ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆይፅስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy 'Éwestätéwos (= Eustace), his two sons, and his wife were martyrs. This saint was one of the generals of the empire of Rome and he did not know God. His name was first Qidos (= Placidus). He gave many alms and was very charitable to the poor and the needy. God did not want to render his labor vain. 'Éwestätéwos was watching in the countryside and hunting wild beasts. One day, the image of a cross appeared to him in the middle of the horns of a stag, and the cross reached the clouds. He pursued the stag into the mountains to hunt it. Our Lord spoke to him from the middle of the horns of the stag and taught him his name. He ordered him to go, he, his sons, and his wife, to the bishop of the city and to be baptized with Christian baptism. Our Lord taught him that he would bring poverty upon him quickly. When 'Éwestätéwos heard these words, he descended from the mountain, went to the holy bishop, and was baptized by his hand with Christian baptism, he, his sons, and his wife.
ወተጠምቀ” ፡እምእዴሁ፤፤ ፡ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስ[ት]ና ፡ውእቱ ፡"ወውሉዱ ፡ወብእሲ ቱ፡።። ወወለጠ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ወተሰምየ" ፡ ኤውስጣቴዎስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሐል ቁ?» ፡ኩሉ" ፡ንዋዩ ፣ ወአግብርቲሁ፥፤ ፡'ወአዕማቲሁ ፤ ወአፍራሲሁ"፤ ። ወነሥኦ
He changed his old name and was called 'Éwestätéwos. Immediately, all his fortune, his servants, his maids, and his horses were lost. He took his

wife and his sons, left the city of Rome, and boarded a boat. He did not have with him any remuneration to give to the captain. When the sailors had reached the port, they took his wife in place of the remuneration. 'Éwestätéwos took his two sons and reached another river to cross it. He took one of them (= his sons) to have him cross to the other side of the river. When he had had him cross, he returned to take the other, but a lion had come and taken him. When he returned to the first [to have him cross, he did not find him, for a wolf had carried him off. He felt great sorrow because of the loss of his wife and his sons. But God kept them without damage. 'Éwestätéwos went into a city and was a gardener for many days.
ዕለ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡፣ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፣ ወነግሠ ፡ "ካልእ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ህየንቴሁ" ። ወኀሠሦ ፣፡ ለኤውስጣቴዎስ ፡ ወኢረከቦ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሐዋ ርያተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር5 ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥዎ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ፳እምእሉ ፥፡ ሐዋርያት0-፦፤ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዕዋድ ፡ ዘይነብር ፤ ውስቴታ ፡ ኤውስጣቴ ዎስ ፡ ወአ[እ]መሮ ፡ ወተአምጐ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወወሰዶ“ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ክብር ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡፤ ኅበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ አክበሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሜሞ ፤ ውስተ" ፡ 'ሚመቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ "፪ዕደወ ፤ እምኩሉ ፡ ለለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሀገር?" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፀብእ ። ወኮኑ ፤ ፪ውሉ ዱ?፤ ፡ ለኤውስጣቴዎስ” ፡ ተሐፅኑ ፡ !ኀበ ፤ አሐቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኢያአመረ" ፡ ፳ለካልኡ ። ወወሀብዎሙ ፡ 'ለእሉ ፤ ፪ውሉድ"፦”" ፡ "በእንተ ፡፣ ይእቲ"”" ፡ ሀገር ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ተጠየቆሙ"' ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አኀው ፡ ወእምዝ ፤ ወሰድዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወበፈቃ ደ፤ ፣፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፤ ረሰይዎሙ ፡ መዛግብተ ፡ ውስተ ፡'ቤተ ፡መዛግብተ ፡ አ[ቡ]ሆሙ ፡ ወኢያ[እ]መሮሙ ፡ ወለእሞሙሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡፤ ነሥኣ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረ ሚ፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ሐመር ፡'አቀባ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ እምኔሁ" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወኮነት ፡ ተዓቅብ ፡ 'አዕዋደ ፡ እምአዕዋዳተ፤5 ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወበጽሑ ፡፤ እሉ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ አዕዋድ። ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆ ሙ፤ ፣ አእመረቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውሉዳ ፡ ወተአምኀቶሙ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ት ፡ ቦሙ ፥ ወእሙንቱኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ባቲ" ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡
After these days, the emperor of Rome died and another emperor reigned in his place. He searched for 'Éwestätéwos, did not find him, and sent messengers into all the cities to search for him. One of these messengers reached the gardens where 'Éwestätéwos dwelt and recognized him. They embraced and he brought him to the emperor in great honor. When he had arrived before the emperor, he honored him greatly and installed him in his position as before. The emperor ordered to bring two men from each of all the cities for the war. The two sons of 'Éwestätéwos had been raised in one and the same city, but one did not know the other. They gave these two young men for this city. One day, while they were speaking among themselves, it was discovered to them that they were brothers. Then they were brought to the city of Rome and, by the will of God, they were made guards in the treasury of their father, but he did not recognize them. As for their mother, when the pagan captain took her, God protected her from him and kept her without damage. She kept a garden among the gardens of the city of Rome. The two young men came into this garden and, while they were speaking among themselves, their mother recognized them as being her sons. She embraced them and rejoiced over them. They too rejoiced greatly over her. All three reunited and went to


[p. 484] {#po.43.p484}
ሠለስቲሆሙ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኤውስጣቴዎስ ፡ ወአጠየቅዎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'እሙንቱ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ ወይእቲኒ ፣፤ ብእሲቱ፤"ቦ ። ወተአም“ ፡፤ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡፤ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡፤ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ፳8መካን ፡፤ በከመ ፡ ተካየዶሙ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነክሩ ፤ ወይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እምሠናያት ።
'Éwestätéwos, told him what had happened to them, and he recognized that they (= the young men) were his sons and that she was his wife. They embraced and felt great joy. They were all reunited in one place, as God had promised them, and they dwelt together among themselves, marveling and glorifying God for what he had done for them as beautiful things.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ውእቱ፤‹፤ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ 'ዘኮነ ፡ ያፈቅሮ፤'" ፡ ለኤውስጣቴ ዎስ፤፤-፤0ፅ ፥ ወነግሠ ፡ ሆ[የ]ንቴሁ ፡፤ ካልእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አረሚ!" ፡ ዘያመልክ ፡ ጣዖታተ፤"ቦ ። ወአብጽሖ፤ቦ ፡ ለኤውስጣቴዎስ፤" ፡ ወለውሉዱ፤"የ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይ ቤሎሙ ፡ ውግሩ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለአማልክት፤!" ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ንሕነሰ ፤ ኢናመ ልክ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወኢንሰግድ ፡ ሎሙ ። አላ ፡ ንሰግድ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ፤፤፤ ፡ ወልደ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣፤ ወአዘዘ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎሙ ፡ ወኩነንዎሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ዓቢየ ፡ ወእግዚኣብሔር ፤ ያነሥኦሙ ፡ ጥዑየ!!። ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወእምድኅ[ረ]ዝ፤፤" ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብዕራ፤፤ ፡ ዘብርት ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ፤፤ ፡ በከመ፣ አዘዘ፤፤ ፣ ወአንደዱ ፡ "እሳተ ፡ ዲቤሆሙ፤፤' ፡ ወመጠዉ ፡ ነፍሶሙ፤! ፡ ውስተ ፣ 'እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር፤! ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት፤ ፡እምኅበ ፡'እግዚእ
After that, the emperor who loved 'Éwestätéwos died and another pagan emperor who worshipped idols reigned in his place. He brought 'Éwestätéwos and his sons before him and said to them: "Offer incense to the gods." The saints said to him: "We do not worship idols nor do we prostrate ourselves before them, but we prostrate ourselves before our Lord Jesus Christ, the Son of the living God." The emperor then became irritated against them and ordered them to be tortured. They were made to undergo great tortures, but God raised them up intact without damage. After that, the emperor ordered them to be thrown into a bronze bull; they did to them as he had ordered and they lit a fire under them. They surrendered their soul into the hand of God and received the crown of life from our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ages of ages, amen.
ነ ፡-ኢየሱስ ፡ክርስቶስ፤"፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ። ጸሎቶሙ! ፡ ወበረከቶሙ፤ ፡ የሀሉ፤ ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፥ ካዕበ ፡፤ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጤቀላ ፡ ዘኮነት ፣ ሰማዕተ፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ክፅወተ ፡ ደም ። ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ክቡራነ ፡ ወብዑላነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ መቄዶንያ ፡ ወሐፀንዋ ፡ በተግሣጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕገ፤፤ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ እስመ ፤ እሙንቱ ፡ ያመልኩ ፡፤ ጣዖተ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ጳውሎስ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአኃዘ ፡ ይስብክ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር! ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ያነድዩ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይላህዉ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይእዜ፤፤ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይነድዱ፤" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ይእቲ ፡፤ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ወይ ከውኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘአልቦሙ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይወርሱ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡
[On this day also died the holy Tëgalä (= Thecla) who was a martyr without shedding of blood. The parents of this saint were honored and rich among the people of Magëdonyä (= Macedonia). They raised her with rigor according to the law of their country, for they worshipped idols. When Päwlos (= Saint Paul) arrived in this country, he began to preach in the name of God, saying: "Blessed are those who become poor for justice, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven! Blessed are those who lament now, for they will soon rejoice! Blessed are those who are burned because of justice, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven! Blessed are those who have a wife and who are like those who do not have a wife, for they will inherit eternal life!"
ዘለዓለም ። ዘንተ፤፤፤ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጤቀላ ፡ ነበረት ፡ የዕለተ ፤ እንዘ ፡ ኢትወርድ ፡ እምተስላሰ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ። ወትቤላ ፤ እማ ፤ ለምንት ፡ ዘኢትወርዲ ፤ እመስኮተ ፤ ቤትኪ ፡ ወኢትበልዒ ፡ እክለ ፣ ወወረደት ፡ እምተስላሰ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ በጽሚት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወዓሰበቶ ፣ ለዓፃዌ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ከንበሎሃ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግር፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፡ ጳው ሎስ ፤ ተመጠዋ ፡ በትፍሥሕት ፡ ወመሐራ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡
When the holy Tëgalä heard this and other things that resembled it, she remained three days without descending from the upper floor of her house, without food or drink. Her mother said to her: "Why do you not descend from the window of your house and eat food?" Tëgalä descended from the upper floor of her house, went in secret to Päwlos, and paid the doorkeeper of her house with her golden comb so that he would not speak.


[p. 486] {#po.43.p486}
ሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ እማ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ረከበታ ፡ ነቢራ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረት ፡ኀበ ፣መኩንን ፡ወአስተዋደየታ ፡ከመ ፡ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ይእቲ ፤ ወአዘዘ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዋ፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አብጽሕዋ ፡ ተም ዕዓ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ ይውርውዎ ፡ ውስተ፤፥ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወእግዚአብ ሔር ፤ አድኃኖ ። ለጤቀላኒ፤፤ ፡ 'አዘዘ ፡ ከመ!፤3 ፡ ይውርውዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አዋልደ ፡ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአያ ፡ ኩነኔሃ ። ወእንበለ ፡ ያገብር ዋ፣፤ ወረወት፤»፣ ርእሳ ፤ "ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ለሊሃ፤0 ። ወእምድኅረ፤፤ ፡ ሐተመት ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በማኅተመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወፅአት፤ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፤ ዘይለ ክፋ! ፡ ወሖረት ፡፤ ኀበ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወቀረጸት፤ ፡ ሥዕርታ ፡ ወቀነተት ፡ ወተለወቶ ፤ ለጳውሎስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ካልእ ፡ አኃዛ ፡ ወአገበራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኅድግ ፡ ሃይማኖታ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዓበየት ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይ]ደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ! ፣ አናብስት ፡ ወድብ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ላቲ ፤ ወለሐሱ ፡ ፀበለ ፡ እገሪሃ ። ወሶበ፤ ፡ ረከብዋ ፤ ሕያውታ ፡ አምኑ፤" ፡ በእደዊሃ ፣፡ መኩንን ፡፣ ወእ ሊአሁ ፡ ወተጠምቁ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይእቲሰ ፡ ነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትተልዎ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ወትሰብክ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ በሰላም ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ገብረት ፡፤ ብዙኃ ፡ ተኣምራተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡
When Päwlos saw her, he welcomed her with joy and taught her the faith of Christ. The next day, when her mother arrived at the saint Päwlos', she found her (= Tëgalä) sitting with him. Then, she went to the governor and denounced her as being a Christian. The governor ordered her to be brought with Päwlos. When she had been brought, he became irritated against Päwlos and ordered him to be thrown into the fire, but God saved him. As for Tëgalä, he ordered her to be thrown into the fire as well and he gathered all the young girls of the city so that they would see her torture. Without being forced, she threw herself into the fire. After having sealed herself with the seal of Christ, she came out from the middle of the fire without anything having touched her. She went to Päwlos, cut her hair, girded herself, and followed Päwlos. When another governor heard this, he had her seized and pressed her to abandon her faith. As she refused, he ordered her to be thrown into a menagerie of lions and bears. These prostrated themselves before her and licked the dust from her feet. When they found her alive, the governor and his people believed through her and were baptized in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. As for her, she remained following Päwlos and preaching in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! Then she died in peace after having performed many miracles. May God, through her prayer, have mercy on our king 'lyäsu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ages of ages.]
ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቀሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ እንጦላርዮስ፤ ። ወዝንቱ፤፡" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ሊቀ ፤ መጸብሓን ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡፤ ሎቱ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መኑሂ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ፀሐይ ፡ ወአኃዙ ፡ይዝክሩ ፡መሓርያነ ፡ ወቂቁያነ ፡ ወዘከርዎ ፡ ለአ[ን]ጢላርዮን፤፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢመጽወተ ፡ ለ፳፤'እምኔሆ ሙ ። ወይቤ ፡ ፳እምነዳያን ፡ ምንተ ፡፤ ትሁቡኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኣመጽእ ፡ እምኀቤሁ፤፥፤ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፤ ወአሰፈውዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብዎ ፡ ንዋየ! ። ወሖረ ፡፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ይውግሮ፤ ፡ በመዓት ፡ ዐቢይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ እብነ ፡ ወገሮ ፡ በኅብስተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሐምል ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ በእዱ ። ወነሚኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፤ ሖረ ፡፤ ኀበ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነሥአ ፡፤ 'ምጽዋተ ፡ እም ኔሁ፤" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ርእየ ፣ በሕልሙ ፡፣ አንጢላርዮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ሹነኔ ፣ ወይትዋቀሥዎ፤፤ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ጸሊማን ፡ ወካልኣን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ብሩሃን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቄጽሩ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ኢረከብነ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አሐቲ፤፤ ፡ ዛቲ ፣ ኅብስተ፤ ፡ ሐምል ። ወሶበ ፡ ወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መዳልው ፡ ተዓርየት ፡ ምስለ ፣ ኩሉ ፤ ኃጣውኢሁ?፤ ።
[On this day also died the holy 'Entoläryos (= Antilarius). This saint was a chief of the publicans and he had no pity for anyone. One day, some poor people were sitting in the sun and they began to speak of charitable and avaricious people. They spoke of 'Antiläryon as having never given alms to any of them. One of the poor said: "What will you give me if I bring back an alms from him?" They promised to give him money. He went to him (= 'Antiläryon) and asked him for alms; he wanted to throw a stone at him in great anger, but, as he did not have a stone, he threw him some bread made of vegetable seeds that was in his hand. Having taken it, the poor man went to his comrades and told them that he had received an alms from him (= 'Antiläryon). Then, 'Antiläryos saw himself in his dream at the Judgment: black men were arguing with other men of light, with austere faces, who said: "We have not found any alms from him, except this single vegetable bread." When they put it (= the bread) on the scale, it outweighed all his (= 'Antiläryos') sins.
ወነቂሆ ፡ እምንዋሙ ፡ ዘረወ ፤ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ለነዳያን ፤ ወለምስኪናን!፡ ፡ ወሖረ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወጠ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቋዲናር ፤ ወመጽወተ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ወነበረ ፡
Awakened from his sleep, he distributed all his fortune to the poor and the needy, went to Jerusalem, sold himself for thirty dinars, gave in

እንዘ ፡ ይትቀነይ ፤ ከመ ፡፣ ገብር ። ወሶበ ፡ ያኃዝንዎ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ እግዚኡ ፤ ያስተርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ ብእሲ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትኅዝን ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡፤ ነሣ እኩ ፤ ሜጠከ ፡ ወነያ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ከሠቱ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ እደው ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እምሀገሩ ፡ ጐየ ፡ በኅቡእ ። "ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሀገር ፡ወኮነ ፡አፃዌ ፤ጥኅት ፤በሐመ ፣፤ወጽሙመ ። ወሶበ ፡ይቤሎ ፡ አንጢላርዮስ ፡ አርጐ ፤ ሊተ፤ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወፈትሐ! ፡ እዘኒሁ ። ወነበበ ፡፤ አፉሁ፤ ፡ ወአርኃወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ አዕረፈ ። ወሶበ ፡ኃጥእዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ አፃዊ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ዘኮነ ፡'እምቅድስናሁ ፡ ወአንከሩ! ፡ መንክራቲሁ፤ ። በረከቱ ፣ ተሀሉ፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ፅለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ጽጌ ። ለዝንቱ፤'" ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ እምብዝኃ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ መጽለወ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምድር ፡ይቡስ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ከዓዉ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መልአ ፡ ኮራ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኢነጠበ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ንስቲት ፣እምብዝኃ ፡ይብሰተ ፡ሥጋሁ ። በረከቱ ፡ተሀሉ፤?፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡
alms (the price of) his sale and remained to serve as a slave. As his master's slaves tormented him, a luminous man appeared to him and said: "Do not grieve, for I have received the price of your sale and here it is in my hand." Then, when people who had come from his city revealed what he had done, he fled in secret. When Antiläryos arrived at the city gate - the porter was mute and deaf - and he said to him: "Open for me," there came out of his (= Antiläryos's) mouth (something) in the likeness of fire and (that) opened his (= the porter's) ears, and his mouth spoke. He opened the gate for him, (Antiläryos) went out into the desert and died there. As the people could not find him, the porter told them everything that had happened through his holiness, and they admired his miracles. May his blessing be with our king 'lyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.] [On this day also died Father Yohannes (= John) of Dabra Segë. Because of the excess of fasting, prayer, and vigil, the body of this saint withered and became like dry earth. One day, as they were pouring a full cup of water over him, only a little flowed (to the ground) because of the excess of the dryness of his body. May his blessing be with our king 'lyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 28TH OF MASKARAM
ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፳፳ቿለመስከረም ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባዲር ፡ ሰማዕት፤ ፡ ወኢራኢ ፡እኅቱ ፡ምስሌሁ ። ዝንቱ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ወልደ ፡እኅቱ ፡ ለፋሲላዳስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡'መንግሥት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አባዲር ፡ሊቀ ፡ሰራዊት ፡ህየንተ ፡አቡሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ "ሎቱ ፡ ጽርሕ ፡ ይጹሊ ፤ ውስቴቱ ። ወአስተርአዮ፤ ፡ ሎቱ፤፤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ በመንፈቀ ፤ ሌሊት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ "ወንሥኣ ፡ ለእኅትከ፤ ፡ ኢራኢ ፡ ወሖር፤ ፡ ኀበ! ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሥኡ፤" ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወአነ! ፡ አአዝዞ! ፡ ለ፳ብእሲ ፤ ዘስሙ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐሊ ፡ በሥጋክሙ!፤ ፡ ወይገንዞሙ! ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወዓርገ ፥ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያ ት፡9-2፤ «። ወከማሁ ፡ ለእኅቱ ፡ ኢራኢ ፡ አስተርአያ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ስምዒ ፡ 'እምነ ፡ እጉኪ፥ ፡ ወኢትትዓደዊ ፡ ትእዛዞ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሃት ፣ እምንዋማ ፡ ርዕደት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወመጸአት"" ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሃ ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አስተርአያ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ይቤላ ። ወተፈሥሔሑ” ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ ወተሰናአወት ፡ ምስለ ፡ እቱሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ፥" ፡ ኢትትዓደው ፡ ትእዛዞ፥፤ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day were martyred the saint 'Abädir and 'Irä'i (= Iraï), his sister, with him. This saint was the son of the sister of Fäsilädäs (= Basilides), the imperial general of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), and 'Abädir became general in the place of his relative. He had a room in which he prayed. Our Lord Christ appeared to him in the middle of the night and said to him: "Arise, take your sister 'Irä'i and go to the land of Egypt so that you may receive the crown of martyrdom. I will order a man whose name (is) Sämu'êl (= Samuel) to take care of your bodies and to bury them." He gave him peace and ascended into the heavens. Likewise, our Lord Christ appeared to his sister ‘Irä'i and said to her: "Obey your brother and do not transgress his order." When she awoke from her sleep, she trembled greatly, came to her brother, and told him how our Lord had appeared to her and how he had spoken to her. They rejoiced greatly, she agreed with her brother not to


[p. 490] {#po.43.p490}
ወተመሐሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ክመ ፡ እሙንቱ?፥ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወይትከዐው፥ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረት፥" ፡ እሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባዲር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሰጠጠት ፡፣ አልባሲሃ ፡ 'ይእቲ ፡ ወአእማቲሃ፥ ፡ ወበጽሐት+" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ፥፤ ፡ ወበከያ ፡ ቅድሜሁ፡ ። ወኮነት ፡ ተምሕሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወመሐለ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢ[ይ]ትናገሮ ፤ ለዲዮቅልጢያኖስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ ወአርመመ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወሀድአት ፡ እምሐዘና ። ወኢሐለየት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡
transgress his order and they swore to each other to go to the land of Egypt and that their blood should be shed for the name of our Lord Christ - glory to him! When the mother of the saint 'Abädir knew this thing, she tore her clothes, she and her servants, came to the saint, and they wept before him; (his mother) adjured him not to become a martyr. He swore to her not to speak to Diyogletiyänos (= Diocletian) about martyrdom and her (= the mother's) heart calmed and her grief subsided. She did not think that he would go to another place and become a martyr (there).
የሐውር ፡ ኀበ ፡ "ካልእ ፡ መካን ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ሰማዕተ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባዲር ፣፡ ኩላየ ፣፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ይወልጥ! ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ እምቤቱ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወይቀድሕ ፡ ማየ ፡ ለሙቁሓን ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ "ዙላ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እስከ ፡ 'ይቀርብ ፡ ጽባሕ" ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ለዓቃቤ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግር ፡ ወኢለመኑሂ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ከሠትከ ፡ "ዘንተ ፤ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ አነ ፡ እመትር ፡ ርእሰከ ፣ በሰይፍ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዳግመ፤ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ወይከውን ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ አባዲር ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ 'ለኢራኢ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወበጽሑ ፡፤ 'ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ" ፡ እስክንድርያ ። 'ወአእመርዎ ፡ ቦእምነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወይቤልዎ” ፡ አንተ?" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አባዲር ፡ እግዚእነ?፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሰራ ዊት ። ወአክሞሰሰ ፣፤ ወይቤ?" ፣ ብዙኃን ፡ 'ከመዝ ፡ ይብሉኒ” ፡ 'እስመ ፡ አንተ ፣ አባዲር ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ እቤሎሙ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ አነ' ፡ አባዲር ፡ አላ ፡ እመስል ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በካልእ ፡ መካን ፡፣ አእመርዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አንተ ፡ ውእቱ"”" ፡ አባዲር ፡ 'ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይብሎሙ?" ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ አነ ፡ አባዲር ። ወእምዝ ፣ ወፅኡ ፡ እምሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወበጽሑ'" ፡ "ምስር ፡ ሀገር” ፣፡ ዓቢይ ፡
The saint 'Abädir, all night long, changed his clothes. He went out of his house in secret and drew water for the prisoners in the prisons all night until the morning was near. He ordered the keeper of the city gates to tell this to no one and said to him: "If you reveal this to anyone, I will cut off your head with the sword." After that, our Lord appeared to him again and ordered him to go to the land of Egypt (where) he would become a martyr. Immediately, the saint 'Abädir arose, took ‘Irä'i, his sister, and they went to the land of Egypt. They arrived at the city of Alexandria. There were soldiers who recognized him and said to him: "You are 'Abädir, our lord the general." He smiled and said: "Many have said to me thus: You are 'Abädir, but I said to them: I am not 'Abädir, but I resemble him." In another place, he was recognized again and they said to him: "You are 'Abädir." And he said to them (= the people): "I am not 'Abädir." Then they went out of the city of Alexandria and arrived at Mesr (= Cairo), the great city. They found there the saint 'Abakarzun, the saint 'Abädir recognized him and blessed him.
ወረከብዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡፤ አበከርዙን ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአእመሮ፤፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ አባዲር ፡ "ወባረከ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወእምህየ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስሙነይን ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ምስለ ፡ 'ዲያቆን ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እንጽና ። ወተአምኑ ፡ "በእግዚእነ ፣፤ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን፣ ወኩነኖ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ 'ዓቢየ ፤ ኩነኔ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባዲር ፡ ይስእል ፤ ኀበ ፡ 'እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወያጽንዕ፤ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ ወሃይማኖተ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ኢራኢ= ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወመሰጠ ፡፤ እግዚእነ ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ለእኅቱ” ፡ ወወሰዳ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ወርእየት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ 'ማኅበረ ፡ ሰማ ዕት ፡ ወጻድቃን ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ መየጥዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩ ንን ፣ እምኩነኔሆሙ”" ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ[ሙ]"-” ።
From there, they went to the city of 'Esmunayn (= Ashmunein) and met with the deacon Sämu'ël. The next day, the deacon went with them to the city of 'Ensenä (= Antinoë). They confessed our Lord Christ before 'Aryänos (Arianus), the governor, and he (made) them undergo great tortures. The saint 'Abädir prayed to our Lord Christ and (the latter) strengthened his faith and the faith of his sister ‘Irä'i in the torture. Our Lord took the soul of his sister and carried it into the heavenly Jerusalem. She saw the whole assembly of the martyrs and the righteous, then she was brought back into her body. When the governor was weary of torturing them, he ordered their heads to be cut off.


[p. 492] {#po.43.p492}
"ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፤ ርእሶሙ፤"ቦ ፡ ወይቤሎ፤"፤ ፡ አርያኖስ ፤ መኩንን ፤ ለቅዱስ ፤ አባዲር፤"" ፡ አምሕለከ ፡ በአምላክከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታይድዓኒ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፣ ስምከ ፤ ወእምአይቴ ፡ አንተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መሐል ፡ ሊተ፣ ከመ ፡ አንተ፤" ፡ ኢትወልጥ ፡፤ ትእዛዘከ ፡ ዘአዘዝከ፤ቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሰ ነ፤69-፤07 ። ወመሐለ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አባዲር ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሰራዊት ። ወከልሐ ፡ 'መኩ ንን ፤ አርያኖስ፤" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፤ አሌሊተ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ይደልወኒ፤! ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙት ፡ ይእዜ፤!፤ ፡ በቅድሜከ ፡ እፎ፤፤ ፡ ለምንት፤፤፤ ፣ ኢታጠይቀኒ'! ፡ ከመ ፣ አንተ ፡ እግዚእየ ። አላ ፡ ኀባእከ ፡ ርእሰከ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ፤፤ ፡ ኩነንኩከ ፡ ዘንተ፤! ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባዲር ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ሀለወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትነሥእ፤፤'" ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ የኀሥሠኒ፤፤-፤!» ፥ ወኢይረክበኒ ፡ ወይሰምዕ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ አንተ ፡ ቀተልከኒ ፡ ወይቤንነከ ፡ 'ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ፤ ፡ ወትመውት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ 'ለእግዚ እነ ፡ክርስቶስ፤"፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ አፍጥን ፡ ወፈጽም ፡ገድለነ፤፡ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ!" ፡ "ሎቱ ፣ ወለእኅቱ ፣ ወመተሩ ፣ ርእሶሙ፤፤ ፡ በሰይፍ፤፤ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወሰፍሑ!፤ ፡ ሎቱ!"'" ፡ አልባሳተ ፡ ሠናያነ፤፤ ፡ ወገነዙ ፡ 'ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ሥጋ ሆሙ ፡ ንጹሓነ፤፤» ። ወነሥኦሙ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ወአንበሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ፤ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፡ ወተሐንጸ፤፤ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፣
Before their heads were cut off, 'Aryänos the governor said to the saint 'Abädir: "I conjure you by your God to let me know what your name is and where you are from." The saint said to him: "Swear to me that you will not change your order that you have given to cut off my head." The governor swore (it) to him as the saint had told him. The saint spoke and said to him: "I am 'Abädir, the general." The governor cried out, saying: "Woe to me, my lord! I must die now before you. How, why did you not inform me that you (were) my lord? On the contrary, you hid yourself from me until I had (made) you undergo these great tortures!" The saint 'Abädir answered him and said to him: "Do not be afraid, for you will receive the crown of martyrdom; indeed, the emperor will search for me and will not find me. He will learn that you killed me, he will (make) you undergo great tortures, and you will die for the name of our Lord Christ - glory to him! Now, hasten to put an end to our life." (The governor) then ordered their heads to be cut off, his and his sister's; their heads were cut off with the sword and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Beautiful clothes were spread for him (= 'Abädir) and their pure bodies were buried in them. Sämu'ël, the deacon, took them and placed them in his house until the end of the days of persecution. A beautiful church was built for them and there were, from their bodies, great miracles and wonders. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ክርስቲያን ፤ ሠናይት፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። < ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ አበው ፡ ክቡ ራን ፡ እለ፡ ጾሩ፣ ብነ ፡ ዓለም ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይምሐረነ ፡፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፥ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ ስእለ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።> ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሶስና ። ለዛቲ፤ ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ኮነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ኬልቅያስ ፤ ወኮነት ፣ ብእሲቱ፤ ፡ ለኢዮዓቄም ፡፣ እምዓበይተ ፡ አይ ሁድ ፡ እምእለ፤ ፡ ተፄወዉ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ይመጽኡ!፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ይትኬነኑ ፡ ወሶስና፤ ፡ ላህይት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወትፈርሆ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወአዝ ማዲሃኒ ፤ ጻድቃን ፡ ወመሀርዋ ፡ ለወለቶሙ ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ዓፀደ ፡ አቅ ማኅ ፡ ዘምታ ፡ ወጊዜ ፡ ቀትር ፡ ትበውእ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወታንሶሱ ።
<On this day also, there is 'Abrehäm (= Abraham); Yeshaq (= Isaac) and Yä'qob (= Jacob), the illustrious fathers who have borne the world with us. May God have mercy on us through their prayer and may their blessing be with their dear Se'ela Seyon for ever and ever.> [On this day also died the saint Sosenä (= Susanna). The father of this saint was Këlgeyäs (= Helkias) and she was the wife of 'lyo'ägêm (= Joachim), one of the Jewish notables among those (who) had been deported to Bäbilon (= Babylon). All her relatives came (to him) to receive his judgment. Sosenä (was) very beautiful and feared God. Her parents also (were) righteous and had taught their daughter the law of Moses. She had an orchard that (belonged to) her husband and, at the time of noon, she would go there and walk about.
ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አስተርአዩ ፡ ፪ረበናት ፡መደልዋን ፡እለ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ነገረ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ እምባቢሎን ፡ ወፅአት ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ እምረበናት ፡ መደልዋን ፡ እለ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ነዓቅቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ። ወእሙንቱሰ፤፤ ፡ ይለምዱ ፡
